6	under Sprint 1's writer_epoch CAS (apex contract preserved).

1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Astrid top-level command gateway.
3	
4	Sprint 1 wires the session CLI gate: every verb outside the unbound
5	allowlist requires ``ASTRID_SESSION_ID`` to resolve to a valid session
6	record. Unbound callers are pointed at ``astrid attach <project>``.
7	
8	The unbound allowlist mirrors the brief: ``attach``, ``status``,
9	``projects ls``, ``projects create``, ``sessions ls``,
10	``sessions takeover``, ``sessions detach``, ``init`` (first-run
11	bootstrap), and the help flags. ``author test --project <slug>`` is also
12	a documented exception so workflow tests can run without an operator
13	session.
14	
15	Subcommands dispatch to focused module CLIs. Brief / video flags fall
16	through to the ``builtin.hype`` orchestrator resolved through the
17	orchestrator registry.
18	"""
19	
20	from __future__ import annotations
21	
22	import json
23	import sys
24	from typing import Any, Iterable
25	
26	
27	# Phase 5 lifecycle verbs short-circuit the implicit task-mode gate at the top
28	# of main(): for these verbs the --project flag identifies the run, NOT a
29	# command to dispatch through plan[cursor]. cmd_ack approve re-enters the gate
30	# explicitly (see lifecycle_ack._ack_approve), so the short-circuit only
31	# bypasses the gate's command-match step.
32	LIFECYCLE_VERBS = {"start", "next", "ack", "abort", "status", "runs", "hook", "plan", "claim", "unclaim", "step"}
33	
34	
35	# Sprint 1 session-gate allowlist. A first-token (or two-token) match against
36	# this set lets the verb run without a bound session. Everything else needs
37	# ``ASTRID_SESSION_ID`` to resolve to a session record.
38	_UNBOUND_TOP_LEVEL = {
39	    "attach",
40	    "status",
41	    "sessions",  # sub-verbs handled below
42	    "init",
43	    "-h",
44	    "--help",
45	}
46	_UNBOUND_PROJECTS_SUBVERBS = {"ls", "create"}
47	_UNBOUND_SESSIONS_SUBVERBS = {"ls", "takeover", "detach"}
48	
49	
50	def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
51	    raw = sys.argv[1:] if argv is None else list(argv)
52	    if raw and raw[0] in {"-h", "--help"}:
53	        _print_entrypoint_help()
54	        return 0
55	    # Nudge runs once per CLI invocation, before the command itself, but never
56	    # for the `skills` subcommand (would be silly) or help. Cheap state-file
57	    # read; bails early if no harness is detected or ARTAGENTS_NO_NUDGE is set.
58	    try:
59	        from .skills import nudge_if_needed
60	
61	        nudge_if_needed(argv=raw)
62	    except Exception:
63	        # Never let the nudge break a real command.
64	        pass
65	
66	    # Session gate. Verbs outside the unbound allowlist require a resolvable
67	    # session record; print the documented hint and exit 2 otherwise.
68	    if not _verb_is_unbound_allowlisted(raw):
69	        from .core.session.binding import (
70	            ASTRID_SESSION_ID_ENV,  # noqa: F401 — referenced in the error path

72	            resolve_current_session,
73	        )
74	
75	        try:
76	            session = resolve_current_session()
77	        except SessionBindingError as exc:
78	            print(f"session: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
79	            return 2
80	        if session is None:
81	            print(
82	                "no session bound — run `astrid attach <project>`",
83	                file=sys.stderr,
84	            )
85	            return 2
86	
87	    if raw and raw[0] in LIFECYCLE_VERBS:
88	        return _dispatch(raw)
89	    project_slug = _extract_project_slug(raw)
90	    if project_slug is None:
91	        return _dispatch(raw)
92	
93	    from .core.task import gate as task_gate

99	        return 1

132	def _verb_is_unbound_allowlisted(raw: list[str]) -> bool:
133	    """Decide whether the invocation may run without a bound session.
134	
135	    The allowlist is the canonical Sprint 1 set (brief §CLI gate):
136	
137	    * ``attach``, ``init``, ``-h`` / ``--help`` (full-verb).
138	    * ``status`` — both the new session breadcrumb (no ``--project``) and
139	      the legacy ``astrid status --project <slug>``.
140	    * ``projects ls`` and ``projects create``.
141	    * ``sessions ls`` / ``sessions takeover`` / ``sessions detach``.
142	    * ``author test --project <slug>`` — documented exception for the
143	      workflow test runner.
144	    """
145	
146	    if not raw:
147	        return True  # empty argv → entrypoint help
148	
149	    top = raw[0]
150	    if top in {"-h", "--help"}:
151	        return True
152	    if top in {"attach", "init", "status"}:
153	        return True
154	    if top == "projects" and len(raw) >= 2 and raw[1] in _UNBOUND_PROJECTS_SUBVERBS:
155	        return True
156	    if top == "timelines" and len(raw) >= 2 and raw[1] == "ls":
157	        return True
158	    if top == "sessions" and len(raw) >= 2 and raw[1] in _UNBOUND_SESSIONS_SUBVERBS:
159	        return True
160	    if top == "runpod":
161	        # --help anywhere in the runpod subcommand tree is always allowed.
162	        if "--help" in raw or "-h" in raw or len(raw) == 1:
163	            return True
164	        # `runpod volumes ls/create` and `runpod ensure-storage` operate on RunPod
165	        # cloud state only — no Astrid run/lease/event mutation, so unbound is fine.
166	        if len(raw) >= 2 and raw[1] == "volumes":
167	            return True
168	        if len(raw) >= 2 and raw[1] == "ensure-storage":
169	            return True
170	        # `runpod sweep` writes pod_terminated_by_sweep events to owning runs'
171	        # events.jsonl — requires a bound session per the S4 brief.
172	        return False
173	    # `author test --project <slug>` exception. The orchestrate.cli wires the
174	    # `test` sub-verb regardless of whether a session is bound, so we open the
175	    # gate explicitly to match.
176	    if top == "author" and "test" in raw[1:] and "--project" in raw:
177	        return True
178	    return False
179	
180

182	    if raw and raw[0] == "attach":
183	        from .core.session.cli import build_parser as _sb
184	        from .core.session.cli import cmd_attach
185	
186	        args = _sb().parse_args(["attach", *raw[1:]])
187	        return int(cmd_attach(args))
188	    if raw and raw[0] == "sessions":
189	        return _dispatch_sessions(raw[1:])
190	    if raw and raw[0] == "start":
191	        from .core.task.lifecycle import cmd_start

193	        return cmd_start(raw[1:])
194	    if raw and raw[0] == "next":

196	
197	        return cmd_next(raw[1:])
198	    if raw and raw[0] == "ack":

201	        return cmd_ack(raw[1:])
202	    if raw and raw[0] == "abort":
203	        from .core.task.lifecycle import cmd_abort
204	
205	        return cmd_abort(raw[1:])
206	    if raw and raw[0] == "status":
207	        # The new session-status verb fires when no --project is given; the
208	        # legacy lifecycle status verb keeps working with --project.
209	        if "--project" in raw[1:]:
210	            from .core.task.lifecycle import cmd_status

212	            return cmd_status(raw[1:])
213	        from .core.session.cli import build_parser as _sb

1	"""Inbox surface for external completion signals (Sprint 3 T10).
2	
3	External processes drop JSON files into ``runs/<run-id>/inbox/`` to signal

9	- ``schema_version: 2`` discriminates new entries from legacy.
10	- Manual-adapter completion requires ``submitted_by`` AND ``submitted_by_kind``.
11	- Stale entries (tombstoned or fully-superseded) route to ``.rejected/``.
12	- STOP-LINE: every entry lands in exactly one of inbox/, .consumed/, or .rejected/.

1	"""Hash-chained task-run event log.
2	
3	Sprint 1 — apex contract: every event appended to ``runs/<id>/events.jsonl``
4	is gated by a single :func:`fcntl.flock(LOCK_EX)` critical section that
5	re-reads the tail hash and the lease's ``writer_epoch`` *under the lock*
6	before computing the new prev-hash and writing the line. Tail CAS catches
7	concurrent appenders; epoch CAS catches stale writers that lost a takeover
8	race. The takeover event in ``events.jsonl`` is observability — the lock +
9	two CAS checks are the actual fence (see DEC-007).
10	
11	``verify_chain`` is retained as an offline / audit primitive only. The
12	production hot path uses :func:`append_event_locked` which relies on the
13	tail-hash CAS for integrity (DEC-007 / FLAG-019 — events do not store a
14	``prev_hash`` field, so a v3-style "re-verify the last link" is not
15	expressible without reading the full chain).
16	"""
17	
18	from __future__ import annotations
19	
20	import errno
21	import fcntl
22	import hashlib
23	import json
24	import os
25	from datetime import UTC, datetime
26	from pathlib import Path
27	from typing import Any
28	
29	ZERO_HASH = "sha256:" + "0" * 64
30	
31	LEASE_FILENAME = "lease.json"
32	EVENTS_FILENAME = "events.jsonl"
33	
34	_TAIL_SEEK_INITIAL_WINDOW = 4096
35	
36	
37	class EventLogError(RuntimeError):
38	    """Raised when a task event log cannot be read or written."""
39	
40	
41	class StaleTailError(EventLogError):
42	    """Raised when the on-disk tail hash differs from the writer's expectation.
43	
44	    Carries the conflicting hashes in ``.expected`` / ``.actual`` and the same
45	    values are interpolated into the message so log scrapers can pick them up.
46	    """
47	
48	    def __init__(self, *, expected: str, actual: str) -> None:
49	        self.expected: str = expected
50	        self.actual: str = actual
51	        super().__init__(
52	            f"stale tail: expected prev_hash={expected!r} but events.jsonl tail is {actual!r}"
53	        )
54	
55	
56	class StaleEpochError(EventLogError):
57	    """Raised when the on-disk ``writer_epoch`` differs from the writer's expectation.
58	
59	    Carries the conflicting epochs in ``.expected`` / ``.actual``.
60	    ``expected`` may be ``None`` when the caller intentionally bypasses
61	    the epoch CAS (e.g. sweeper ``--hard`` mode).
62	    """
63	
64	    def __init__(self, *, expected: int | None, actual: int) -> None:
65	        self.expected: int | None = expected
66	        self.actual: int = actual
67	        super().__init__(
68	            f"stale epoch: expected writer_epoch={expected!r} but lease.json holds {actual!r}"
69	        )
70	
71	
72	class NotWriterError(EventLogError):
73	    """Raised when a session attempts to append without holding the lease.
74	
75	    Carries the offending session id and the actual lease writer in
76	    ``.session_id`` / ``.writer_id`` (the writer may be ``None`` for an
77	    orphan-pending lease).
78	    """
79	
80	    def __init__(self, *, session_id: str, writer_id: str | None) -> None:
81	        self.session_id: str = session_id
82	        self.writer_id: str | None = writer_id
83	        super().__init__(
84	            f"session {session_id!r} is not the writer for this run "
85	            f"(lease.attached_session_id={writer_id!r})"
86	        )
87	
88	
89	def canonical_event_json(event: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
90	    payload = {key: value for key, value in event.items() if key != "hash"}
91	    return json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=False)
92	
93	
94	def append_event_locked(
95	    run_dir: str | Path,
96	    event: dict[str, Any],
97	    *,
98	    expected_writer_epoch: int | None = None,
99	    expected_prev_hash: str,
100	) -> dict[str, Any]:

17	def cmd_run_show(
18	    argv: Sequence[str],
19	    *,
20	    projects_root: Optional[Path] = None,
21	) -> int:
22	    """Pretty-print a run summary; ``--json`` for structured output."""
23	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="astrid run show", add_help=True)
24	    parser.add_argument("run_id", help="run identifier")
25	    parser.add_argument("--project", required=True, help="project slug")
26	    parser.add_argument("--json", dest="json_out", action="store_true", help="emit JSON instead of pretty-print")
27	    try:
28	        args = parser.parse_args(list(argv))
29	    except SystemExit as exc:
30	        return int(exc.code or 2)
31	
32	    try:
33	        slug = validate_project_slug(args.project)
34	    except Exception as exc:
35	        print(f"run show: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
36	        return 1
37	
38	    proj_root = project_dir(slug, root=projects_root)
39	    run_root = proj_root / "runs" / args.run_id
40	    if not run_root.is_dir():
41	        print(f"run show: run {args.run_id!r} not found in project {slug!r}", file=sys.stderr)
42	        return 1
43	
44	    events_path = run_root / "events.jsonl"
45	    plan_path = proj_root / "plan.json"
46	
47	    events = read_events(events_path) if events_path.exists() else []
48	    plan = load_plan(plan_path) if plan_path.exists() else None
49	
50	    # Run status
51	    run_status = _run_status(events)
52	
53	    # Plan info
54	    plan_hash_val = plan.plan_id if plan else "unknown"
55	    initial_steps = len(plan.steps) if plan else 0
56	    mutation_count = sum(1 for e in events if e.get("kind") in {"plan_mutated"})
57	
58	    # Step list
59	    step_rows = _build_step_rows(events, run_root)
60	
61	    # Cost

63	
64	    # Acks

66	    attested_decisions = sum(1 for e in events if e.get("kind") in {"step_attested", "item_attested"})
67	
68	    # Consumes

70	    run_json_path = run_root / "run.json"

72	        try:
73	            run_json = json.loads(run_json_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
74	            consumes = run_json.get("consumes", [])
75	        except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError):
76	            pass
77	
78	    # Timestamps
79	    started_ts = ""
80	    completed_ts = ""

83	            started_ts = str(e.get("ts", ""))
84	        if e.get("kind") == "run_completed":
85	            completed_ts = str(e.get("ts", ""))
86	
87	    total_cost = sum(c.get("amount", 0) for c in cost_summary.values() if isinstance(c, dict))
88	
89	    if args.json_out:

91	            "run_id": args.run_id,
92	            "status": run_status,
93	            "project": slug,
94	            "started": started_ts,
95	            "completed": completed_ts or None,

97	            "initial_steps": initial_steps,
98	            "mutations": mutation_count,
99	            "effective_steps": len(step_rows),

66	    if op == "supersede":

+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# events verify (Sprint 5b T5)
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+def cmd_events_verify(
+    argv: Sequence[str],
+    *,
+    projects_root: Optional[Path] = None,
+) -> int:
+    """Verify the hash chain for a run's events.jsonl.
+
+    Thin CLI wrapper around :func:`astrid.core.task.events.verify_chain`.
+    On success prints ``verified: N events, plan_hash=<...>``.
+    On failure prints ``broken at line N: <reason>`` and exits 1.
+
+    ``--strict`` additionally replays ``plan_mutated`` events through
+    :func:`astrid.core.task.validator.validate_mutation` to check that
+    every mutation passes the six-invariant gate.
+    """
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="astrid events verify", add_help=True)
+    parser.add_argument("--run", required=True, dest="run_id", help="run identifier")
+    parser.add_argument("--project", required=True, help="project slug")
+    parser.add_argument(
+        "--strict",
+        action="store_true",
+        help="also validate plan_mutated events against the six-invariant validator",
+    )
+    try:
+        args = parser.parse_args(list(argv))
+    except SystemExit as exc:
+        return int(exc.code or 2)
+
+    try:
+        slug = validate_project_slug(args.project)
+    except Exception as exc:
+        print(f"events verify: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
+        return 1
+
+    proj_root = project_dir(slug, root=projects_root)
+    run_root = proj_root / "runs" / args.run_id
+    if not run_root.is_dir():
+        print(
+            f"events verify: run {args.run_id!r} not found in project {slug!r}",
+            file=sys.stderr,
+        )
+        return 1
+
+    events_path = run_root / "events.jsonl"
+    if not events_path.exists():
+        print("verified: 0 events, plan_hash=(no events)")
+        return 0
+
+    ok, line_idx, err_msg = verify_chain(events_path)
+
+    events = read_events(events_path)
+    n_events = len(events)
+
+    # Extract plan_hash from run_started event
+    plan_hash = "unknown"
+    for e in events:
+        if e.get("kind") == "run_started":
+            plan_hash = str(e.get("plan_hash", "unknown"))
+            break
+
+    if not ok:
+        if line_idx == -1:
+            print(f"broken: {err_msg}")
+        else:
+            print(f"broken at line {line_idx + 1}: {err_msg}")
+        return 1
+
+    # ── --strict: replay plan mutations through the validator ──────────
+    strict_failures = 0
+    if args.strict:
+        plan_path = proj_root / "plan.json"
+        if not plan_path.exists():
+            print("strict: plan.json not found; cannot validate mutations")
+        else:
+            try:
+                from astrid.core.task.plan_verbs import (
+                    PLAN_MUTATED_KIND,
+                    _apply_diff as plan_apply_diff,
+                )
+                from astrid.core.task.validator import (
+                    MutationInvariantError,
+                    validate_mutation,
+                )
+
+                plan = load_plan(plan_path)
+                current = plan
+                mutation_events = [
+                    e for e in events if e.get("kind") == PLAN_MUTATED_KIND
+                ]
+                for i, ev in enumerate(mutation_events):
+                    diff = ev.get("diff")
+                    if not isinstance(diff, dict):
+                        strict_failures += 1
+                        print(
+                            f"strict: mutation event {i + 1} missing diff field"
+                        )
+                        continue
+                    try:
+                        proposed = plan_apply_diff(current, diff)
+                        # lease_epoch is not recoverable from event log;
+                        # pass epoch=0,0 as a best-effort audit check
+                        # (I6 is skipped in audit mode)
+                        validate_mutation(
+                            prior=current,
+                            proposed=proposed,
+                            lease_epoch_actual=0,
+                            lease_epoch_expected=0,
+                        )
+                        current = proposed
+                    except (MutationInvariantError, Exception) as exc:
+                        strict_failures += 1
+                        print(
+                            f"strict: mutation event {i + 1} failed: {exc}"
+                        )
+            except Exception as exc:
+                print(f"strict: error loading plan: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
+
+    print(f"verified: {n_events} events, plan_hash={plan_hash}")
+    if args.strict:
+        if strict_failures == 0:
+            print("strict: all mutation events pass invariant checks")
+        else:
+            print(
+                f"strict: {strict_failures} mutation event(s) failed validation"
+            )
+            return 1
+    return 0
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# events tail (Sprint 5b T6)
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+def cmd_events_tail(
+    argv: Sequence[str],
+    *,
+    projects_root: Optional[Path] = None,
+) -> int:
+    """Print the last *N* events from a run's ``events.jsonl``.
+
+    ``-f`` polls the file every second for new lines (follow mode).
+    ``-n`` controls how many lines to show (default: 20).
+    """
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="astrid events tail", add_help=True)
+    parser.add_argument("--run", required=True, dest="run_id", help="run identifier")
+    parser.add_argument("--project", required=True, help="project slug")
+    parser.add_argument(
+        "-n",
+        type=int,
+        default=20,
+        help="number of lines to print (default: 20)",
+    )
+    parser.add_argument(
+        "-f",
+        dest="follow",
+        action="store_true",
+        help="follow the file, polling every second for new lines",
+    )
+    try:
+        args = parser.parse_args(list(argv))
+    except SystemExit as exc:
+        return int(exc.code or 2)
+

120	@dataclass
121	class _Frame:
122	    plan: TaskPlan
123	    path_prefix: tuple[str, ...]
124	    child_index: int = 0
125	    iteration: int | None = None
126	    item_id: str | None = None
127	    repeat_step_id: str | None = None
128	
129	
130	@dataclass
131	class CursorPath:
132	    frames: list[_Frame] = field(default_factory=list)
133	    for_each_progress: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=dict)
134	    pinned_failure: tuple[str, str] | None = None  # (reason, host_path) for iteration_exhausted=fail
135	
136	    @property
137	    def at_root_done(self) -> bool:
138	        return len(self.frames) == 1 and self.frames[-1].child_index >= len(self.frames[-1].plan.steps)
139	
140	    @property
141	    def top_exhausted(self) -> bool:
142	        top = self.frames[-1]
143	        return top.child_index >= len(top.plan.steps)
144	
145	
146	def derive_cursor(plan: TaskPlan, events: Sequence[dict[str, Any]], *, slug: str = "") -> CursorPath:
147	    """Replay ``events.jsonl`` left-to-right to reconstruct the path-stack cursor.
148	
149	    Reconstructible from events alone (supports partial-replay resume):
150	    ``nested_entered`` pushes, ``nested_exited`` pops + advances parent,
151	    ``step_completed`` / ``step_attested`` mark a step advance-eligible iff it
152	    has no produces; otherwise advance is deferred until the contiguous block
153	    contains a ``produces_check_passed`` for every declared produces name.
154	    ``produces_check_failed`` / ``cursor_rewind`` clear pending state without
155	    advancing. ``iteration_started`` pushes an iteration frame; ``iteration_failed``
156	    pops it without advancing the host. ``for_each_expanded`` records the host's
157	    item set on the cursor (used as the source of truth — derive_cursor never
158	    re-reads disk during replay). ``item_started`` pushes a per-item frame;
159	    ``item_completed`` / ``item_attested`` pop the item frame and advance the
160	    host once every item is done. ``step_dispatched`` / ``run_started`` do not
161	    advance.
162	    """
163	    frames: list[_Frame] = [_Frame(plan=plan, path_prefix=(), child_index=0)]
164	    pending: list[set[str] | None] = [None]
165	    for_each_progress: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
166	    pinned_failure: tuple[str, str] | None = None
167	    for event in events:
168	        kind = event.get("kind")
169	        if kind == "iteration_exhausted":
170	            on_exhaust = event.get("on_exhaust")
171	            host_path = _path_str_from_event(event)
172	            if on_exhaust == "fail":
173	                pinned_failure = ("repeat.until max_iterations exhausted", host_path)
174	                continue
175	            if on_exhaust == "escalate":
176	                top = frames[-1]
177	                if top.child_index < len(top.plan.steps):
178	                    host_step = top.plan.steps[top.child_index]
179	                    override_plan = TaskPlan(
180	                        plan_id=f"__exhaust_{host_step.id}",
181	                        version=1,
182	                        steps=(_make_exhaust_override_step(slug, host_path),),
183	                    )
184	                    frames.append(
185	                        _Frame(
186	                            plan=override_plan,
187	                            path_prefix=top.path_prefix + (host_step.id,),
188	                            child_index=0,
189	                            repeat_step_id=host_step.id,
190	                        )
191	                    )
192	                    pending.append(None)
193	            continue
194	        if kind == "nested_entered":
195	            top = frames[-1]
196	            if top.child_index >= len(top.plan.steps):
197	                raise TaskRunGateError(
198	                    reason="nested_entered points past end of frame",
199	                    recovery="inspect events.jsonl",
200	                )
201	            step = top.plan.steps[top.child_index]
202	            if not is_group_step(step):
203	                raise TaskRunGateError(
204	                    reason="nested_entered did not land on a group step",
205	                    recovery="inspect events.jsonl",
206	                )
207	            frames.append(
208	                _Frame(
209	                    plan=step.plan,
210	                    path_prefix=top.path_prefix + (step.id,),
211	                    child_index=0,
212	                )
213	            )
214	            pending.append(None)
215	        elif kind == "nested_exited":
216	            if len(frames) <= 1:
217	                raise TaskRunGateError(
218	                    reason="nested_exited at root frame",
219	                    recovery="inspect events.jsonl",
220	                )
221	            frames.pop()
222	            pending.pop()
223	            frames[-1].child_index += 1
224	            pending[-1] = None
225	        elif kind == "iteration_started":
226	            top = frames[-1]
227	            if top.child_index >= len(top.plan.steps):
228	                continue
229	            host_step = top.plan.steps[top.child_index]
230	            iteration = int(event.get("iteration", 1))
231	            frames.append(_make_iteration_frame(host_step, top.path_prefix, iteration))
232	            pending.append(None)
233	        elif kind == "iteration_failed":
234	            if frames[-1].repeat_step_id is None:
235	                continue
236	            frames.pop()
237	            pending.pop()
238	            pending[-1] = None
239	        elif kind == "for_each_expanded":
240	            host_path = _path_str_from_event(event)
241	            items = tuple(event.get("item_ids") or ())
242	            for_each_progress.setdefault(host_path, {"items": items, "completed": set()})
243	            for_each_progress[host_path]["items"] = items
244	        elif kind == "item_started":
245	            top = frames[-1]
246	            if top.child_index >= len(top.plan.steps):
247	                continue
248	            host_step = top.plan.steps[top.child_index]
249	            item_id = event.get("item_id")
250	            if not isinstance(item_id, str):
251	                continue
252	            frames.append(_make_item_frame(host_step, top.path_prefix, item_id))
253	            pending.append(None)
254	        elif kind in ("item_completed", "item_attested"):
255	            host_path = _path_str_from_event(event)
256	            item_id = event.get("item_id")
257	            if not isinstance(item_id, str):
258	                continue
259	            entry = for_each_progress.setdefault(host_path, {"items": (), "completed": set()})
260	            entry["completed"].add(item_id)
261	            if frames[-1].item_id == item_id:
262	                frames.pop()
263	                pending.pop()
264	                pending[-1] = None
265	            # If all items now completed and the host's parent frame is on top, advance host.
266	            if entry["items"] and set(entry["items"]) <= entry["completed"]:
267	                host_segments = host_path.split(STEP_PATH_SEP) if host_path else []
268	                expected_parent_prefix = tuple(host_segments[:-1])
269	                if tuple(frames[-1].path_prefix) == expected_parent_prefix:
270	                    if frames[-1].child_index < len(frames[-1].plan.steps):
271	                        candidate = frames[-1].plan.steps[frames[-1].child_index]
272	                        if host_segments and candidate.id == host_segments[-1]:
273	                            frames[-1].child_index += 1
274	                            pending[-1] = None
275	        elif kind in ("step_completed", "step_attested"):
276	            top = frames[-1]
277	            if top.child_index >= len(top.plan.steps):
278	                continue
279	            step = top.plan.steps[top.child_index]
280	            produces = getattr(step, "produces", ())
281	            if not produces:
282	                top.child_index += 1
283	                pending[-1] = None
284	            else:
285	                pending[-1] = {entry.name for entry in produces}
286	        elif kind == "produces_check_passed":
287	            current = pending[-1]
288	            if current is not None:
289	                name = event.get("produces_name")
290	                current.discard(name)
291	                if not current:
292	                    frames[-1].child_index += 1
293	                    pending[-1] = None
294	        elif kind in ("produces_check_failed", "cursor_rewind"):
295	            pending[-1] = None
296	        elif kind == "step_dispatched":
297	            pending[-1] = None
298	        # run_started / iteration_exhausted: no-op for cursor
299	    _finalize_cursor(frames, pending, for_each_progress)
300	    return CursorPath(frames=frames, for_each_progress=for_each_progress, pinned_failure=pinned_failure)
301	
302	
303	def _make_iteration_frame(host_step: Any, parent_prefix: tuple[str, ...], iteration: int) -> _Frame:
304	    body = dataclasses.replace(host_step, repeat=None) if hasattr(host_step, "repeat") else host_step
305	    body_plan = TaskPlan(plan_id=f"__iter_{host_step.id}_{iteration}", version=1, steps=(body,))
306	    return _Frame(
307	        plan=body_plan,
308	        path_prefix=parent_prefix,
309	        child_index=0,
310	        iteration=iteration,
311	        repeat_step_id=host_step.id,
312	    )
313	
314	
315	def _make_item_frame(host_step: Any, parent_prefix: tuple[str, ...], item_id: str) -> _Frame:
316	    body = dataclasses.replace(host_step, repeat=None) if hasattr(host_step, "repeat") else host_step
317	    body_plan = TaskPlan(plan_id=f"__item_{host_step.id}_{item_id}", version=1, steps=(body,))
318	    return _Frame(
319	        plan=body_plan,

80	
81	FIRST COMMAND (Sprint 1 / T15)
82	- astrid status                    # session breadcrumb; ALWAYS run first
83	- astrid attach {slug}     # bind this tab to {slug} if status reports unbound
84	
85	RECOVERY COMMANDS
86	- See next legal action:    astrid next --project {slug}
87	- Acknowledge attested:     astrid ack <step> --project {slug} --decision approve [--agent <id> | --actor <name>]
88	- View run state:           astrid status --project {slug}
89	- End the run:              astrid abort --project {slug}
90	- Take over a stuck run:    astrid sessions takeover <run-id|session-id>
91	- Detach the current tab:   astrid sessions detach
92	
93	STOP HOOK
94	- The `astrid hook stop` command is the Claude Code Stop-hook entry point.
95	  When wired into .claude/settings.json (see docs/HOOKS.md) it re-injects this
96	  preamble and the current step on every Stop boundary so the rules above
97	  stay live for the entire run. The hook is a silent no-op outside task mode.
98	
99	INBOX SURFACE
100	- External processes (humans, scripts, other tools) signal completion of an
101	  attested step by dropping a JSON file into runs/{run_id}/inbox/.
102	- File shape:
103	    {{
104	      "step_id": "<id of the current attested step>",
105	      "decision": "approve" | "retry" | "abort",
106	      "evidence": {{ "<key>": "<non-empty string>", ... }},
107	      "submitted_at": "<ISO 8601 timestamp>",
108	      "submitted_by": "<external system or operator name>",
109	      "item_id": "<optional for_each item id>"
110	    }}
111	- Consume-on-next: astrid next reads inbox/, validates each file against
112	  the current cursor, and appends a step_attested / item_attested /
113	  cursor_rewind / run_aborted event before computing the next step.
114	- Agent attestations only — actor-ack steps must use `astrid ack` (the
115	  inbox file would be quarantined to inbox/.rejected/ otherwise).
116	- WARNING: `astrid next` is state-mutating when inbox/ has files.
117	"""

131	def _qualified_split(qualified_id: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
132	    if not isinstance(qualified_id, str) or "." not in qualified_id:
133	        raise ValueError(
134	            f"orchestrator id {qualified_id!r} must be '<pack>.<name>'"
135	        )
136	    pack, _, name = qualified_id.partition(".")
137	    if not pack or not name or "." in name:
138	        raise ValueError(
139	            f"orchestrator id {qualified_id!r} must be exactly '<pack>.<name>'"
140	        )
141	    return pack, name

145	    stamp = datetime.now(UTC).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")
146	    return f"run-{stamp}-{secrets.token_hex(4)}"

154	def cmd_start(
155	    argv: Sequence[str],
156	    *,
157	    packs_root: Optional[Path] = None,
158	    projects_root: Optional[Path] = None,
159	) -> int:
160	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="astrid start", add_help=True)
161	    parser.add_argument("orchestrator_id", help="qualified id <pack>.<name>")
162	    parser.add_argument("--project", required=True, help="project slug")
163	    parser.add_argument("--name", default=None, help="optional run id (slug-validated)")
164	    parser.add_argument("--timeline", default=None, help="timeline slug")
165	    try:
166	        args = parser.parse_args(list(argv))
167	    except SystemExit as exc:
168	        return int(exc.code or 2)
169	
170	    try:
171	        slug = validate_project_slug(args.project)
172	    except Exception as exc:
173	        _print_err(f"start: {exc}")
174	        return 1
175	
176	    # Resolve timeline ULID (timeline_id) and slug for display.
177	    timeline_id: str | None = None
178	    timeline_slug: str | None = None
179	    if args.timeline is not None:
180	        found = find_timeline_by_slug(slug, args.timeline, root=projects_root)
181	        if found is None:
182	            _print_err(
183	                f"start: timeline {args.timeline!r} not found in project {slug!r}"
184	            )
185	            return 1
186	        timeline_id = found[0]
187	        timeline_slug = args.timeline
188	    else:
189	        default_ulid = read_project_default(slug, root=projects_root)
190	        if default_ulid is not None:
191	            resolved_slug = find_timeline_slug_for_ulid(slug, default_ulid, root=projects_root)
192	            if resolved_slug is not None:
193	                timeline_id = default_ulid
194	                timeline_slug = resolved_slug
195	                _print_err(
196	                    f"Using default timeline: {timeline_slug}. "
197	                    f"Use --timeline to override."
198	                )
199	    # If still no timeline, list available timelines and error — but allow the
200	    # bootstrap case (project.json absent) to proceed unbound, mirroring
201	    # ``astrid attach``'s zero-timeline handling. This keeps legacy callers
202	    # that pre-date Sprint 2's container model working until the project is
203	    # explicitly initialized.
204	    if timeline_id is None:
205	        from astrid.core.timeline.crud import list_timelines
206	        from astrid.core.project.paths import project_json_path
207	        available = list_timelines(slug, root=projects_root)
208	        if available:
209	            _print_err("No default timeline; pass --timeline <slug>. Available:")
210	            for ts in available:
211	                _print_err(f"  {ts.slug}  ({ts.name})")
212	            return 1
213	        if project_json_path(slug, root=projects_root).exists():
214	            _print_err(
215	                f"start: no timelines exist for project {slug!r}; "
216	                f"create one with `astrid timelines create <slug>`"
217	            )
218	            return 1
219	        # Bootstrap: project.json doesn't exist yet — proceed without a timeline
220	        # binding. The run record will carry timeline_id=None.
221	
222	    try:
223	        pack, name = _qualified_split(args.orchestrator_id)
224	    except ValueError as exc:
225	        _print_err(f"start: {exc}")
226	        return 1
227	
228	    if read_current_run(slug, root=projects_root) is not None:
229	        _print_err(
230	            f"start: active run already exists for project {slug!r}; "
231	            f"recovery: astrid abort --project {slug}"
232	        )
233	        return 1
234	
235	    packs = _resolve_packs_root(packs_root)
236	    build_path = packs / pack / "build" / f"{name}.json"
237	    if not build_path.is_file():
238	        _print_err(
239	            f"start: compiled plan not found at {build_path}; "
240	            f"recovery: astrid author compile {args.orchestrator_id}"
241	        )
242	        return 1
243	
244	    try:
245	        compiled_payload = json.loads(build_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
246	    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
247	        _print_err(f"start: failed to read {build_path}: {exc}")
248	        return 1
249	
250	    proj_root = project_dir(slug, root=projects_root)
251	    proj_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
252	    plan_path = proj_root / "plan.json"
253	    write_json_atomic(plan_path, compiled_payload)
254	
255	    try:
256	        load_plan(plan_path)
257	    except Exception as exc:
258	        _print_err(f"start: compiled plan failed validation: {exc}")
259	        return 1
260	
261	    plan_hash = compute_plan_hash(plan_path)
262	
263	    if args.name is not None:
264	        try:
265	            run_id = validate_run_id(args.name)
266	        except Exception as exc:
267	            _print_err(f"start: --name {exc}")
268	            return 1
269	    else:
270	        run_id = _generate_run_id()
271	
272	    run_dir = proj_root / "runs" / run_id
273	    run_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
274	
275	    # Lease-first ordering: any reader that observes current_run.json is
276	    # guaranteed to find a corresponding lease.json. The session id on the
277	    # lease is whatever ASTRID_SESSION_ID resolves to (CLI gate enforces
278	    # the session is bound before cmd_start dispatch); fall back to
279	    # 'legacy' for non-CLI callers that haven't migrated yet (tests etc).

472	def peek_current_step(
473	    plan: TaskPlan,
474	    events: Sequence[dict[str, Any]],
475	    slug: str,
476	    *,
477	    project_root: Path,

482	
483	    Shares ``_auto_traverse_to_leaf`` with ``gate_command`` so peek and dispatch
484	    cannot drift on iteration / for_each / nested transitions (FLAG-P5-003).
485	    The captured events are kept in ``events_view`` so prior-event scans inside
486	    the auto-traverse helpers (``_count_iteration_failed``, the
487	    ``for_each_expanded`` lookup) see them; after every append we let the helper
488	    proceed and re-evaluate the cursor — which is equivalent to recomputing
489	    ``derive_cursor(plan, events + captured, slug=slug)`` because the helper
490	    performs the same frame mutations that ``derive_cursor`` would on replay.
491	    """
492	    cursor = derive_cursor(plan, events, slug=slug)
493	    if cursor.pinned_failure is not None or cursor.at_root_done:
494	        return PeekResult(step=None, path_tuple=(), iteration=None, item_id=None, exhausted=True)
495	
496	    events_view = list(events)
497	    captured: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
498	
499	    def _peek_append(ev: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
500	        captured.append(ev)
501	        events_view.append(ev)
502	
503	    leaf = _auto_traverse_to_leaf(
504	        slug=slug,
505	        cursor=cursor,
506	        events_view=events_view,
507	        incoming_command="",
508	        project_root=project_root,
509	        run_id=run_id,
510	        append_fn=_peek_append,
511	        raise_on_exhausted=False,
512	    )
513	    if leaf is None:
514	        return PeekResult(step=None, path_tuple=(), iteration=None, item_id=None, exhausted=True)
515	    step, path_tuple = leaf
516	    top = cursor.frames[-1]
517	    iteration = top.iteration if top.repeat_step_id is not None else None
518	    item_id = top.item_id if top.repeat_step_id is not None else None
519	    return PeekResult(
520	        step=step,
521	        path_tuple=path_tuple,
522	        iteration=iteration,
523	        item_id=item_id,
524	        exhausted=False,
525	    )

528	def gate_command(
529	    slug: str,
530	    command: str,
531	    argv: Sequence[str],
532	    *,
533	    root: str | Path | None = None,
534	    reentry: bool = False,
535	) -> GateDecision:
536	    active_run = read_active_run(slug, root=root)
537	    if active_run is None:
538	        if not is_in_task_run(slug):
539	            return GateDecision(active=False)
540	        _reject(slug, "active_run.json is missing", abort=True)
541	
542	    project_root = project_dir(slug, root=root)
543	    plan_path = project_root / "plan.json"
544	    plan_hash = compute_plan_hash(plan_path)
545	    if plan_hash != active_run["plan_hash"]:
546	        _reject(slug, "plan.json hash does not match active_run.json pin", abort=True)
547	
548	    run_id = active_run["run_id"]
549	    events_path = project_root / "runs" / run_id / "events.jsonl"
550	    # Sprint 1 (T9 / DEC-007): the tail-only CAS in append_event_locked is
551	    # the integrity gate for the hot append path; mid-chain corruption
552	    # detection is delegated to offline audit (verify_chain remains
553	    # callable for that purpose). Removing the per-verb O(n) re-walk here
554	    # is an acceptable trade-off documented in the brief.
555	
556	    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
557	    events = read_events(events_path)
558	    cursor = derive_cursor(plan, events, slug=slug)
559	    if cursor.pinned_failure is not None:
560	        reason, _host_path = cursor.pinned_failure
561	        raise TaskRunGateError(reason=reason, recovery=f"astrid abort --project {slug}")
562	    run_started_actor = _find_run_started_actor(events)
563	
564	    # Auto-traverse: nested_entered/exited for nested plans; iteration_started/
565	    # for_each_expanded/item_started for repeat hosts. We loop until we land on a
566	    # dispatchable leaf (CodeStep or AttestedStep) inside the appropriate frame.
567	    events_view = list(events)
568	
569	    def _gate_append(ev: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
570	        append_event(events_path, ev)
571	        events_view.append(ev)

from astrid.core.task.plan import compute_plan_hash, load_plan


def test_two_step_plan_drives_kernel_end_to_end(tmp_projects_root: Path) -> None:
    create_project("demo", root=tmp_projects_root)
    plan_path = tmp_projects_root / "demo" / "plan.json"
    plan_payload = {
        "plan_id": "p1",
        "version": 2,
        "steps": [
            {"id": "step-1", "kind": "code", "adapter": "local", "command": "python3 -c \"print('ok')\"", "cost": {"amount": 0, "currency": "USD", "source": "local"}},
            {"id": "step-2", "kind": "code", "adapter": "local", "command": "python3 -c \"print('ok')\"", "cost": {"amount": 0, "currency": "USD", "source": "local"}},

    events_path = None
    for step in plan.steps:
        decision = gate_command("demo", step.command, [], root=tmp_projects_root)
        assert decision.active is True
        assert decision.reentry is False
        assert decision.plan_step_id == step.id
        events_path = decision.events_path

        env = child_subprocess_env(base=os.environ)
        completed = subprocess.run(
            shlex.split(step.command),
            env=env,
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            check=False,

Perfect! Now I have enough context. Let me create a concise summary:

Based on my exploration of the Astrid orchestrator codebase, here's a faithful orientation of the task kernel:

## 1. Task List Abstraction

**Storage**: 
- Plan: `/projects/<slug>/plan.json` — stateful JSON file storing the task tree schema (TaskPlan dataclass)
- Run state: `/projects/<slug>/runs/<run-id>/events.jsonl` — immutable hash-chained event log (line-delimited JSON)
- Active run pin: `/projects/<slug>/active_run.json` — records run_id + plan_hash (simple resolver)
- Inbox: `/projects/<slug>/runs/<run-id>/inbox/` — directory where external systems drop completion signals as JSON files

**Entry structure** (plan.json):
- TaskPlan has `plan_id`, `version: 2`, and `steps: [Step, ...]`
- Each Step: `id`, `command` (leaf) or `children` (group), `requires_ack`, `adapter` (local/manual/remote-artifact), `repeat` (until/for_each), `produces`, `version`
- Produces: tuple of (name, path, Check) entries for artifact validation
- Repeat: `until` (condition: user_approves/verifier_passes/quorum, max_iterations, on_exhaust) or `for_each` (static/from, items)

## 2. Task Iteration & Claiming

**Who picks next task** (lifecycle.py:549, gate.py:528–625):
- `cmd_next` calls `peek_current_step()` which replays events through `derive_cursor()` to reconstruct execution position as a stack of `_Frame` objects (one per nesting level)
- `gate_command()` does the same but actually emits events; both use shared `_auto_traverse_to_leaf()` helper
- Cursor tracks: parent plan, child_index, iteration count, item_id (for for_each), pinned_failure (repeat.until exhausted=fail)

**Claiming** (claim.py):
- `cmd_claim`/`cmd_unclaim` emit `claim`/`unclaim` events (not step-level mutations)
- Claim pins agent_id + actor_name + role to a step under the writer_epoch CAS (per Sprint 1's apex contract)
- Read-only sessions are blocked; events go through `append_event_locked()` 

**Lifecycle states** (lifecycle.py:1–50):
- Frames: root → [nested_entered → frame.children] → [iteration_started → repeat body] → [item_started → for_each item]
- Leaf steps: code (no ack) vs attested (requires_ack=true)
- Transitions via events: `step_dispatched` → `step_completed` / `step_attested` → produces checks (`produces_check_passed`) → advance cursor
- On repeat exhaustion: `iteration_exhausted` (fail=pinned_failure, escalate=override step injected)

## 3. Events & Audit

**Event log** (events.py:1–100, run_audit.py:382–531):
- Append-only JSONL; each line hashed with sha256 (stored in next line's `prev_hash` conceptually, but only tail-hash CAS enforced in production per DEC-007)
- Lease epoch CAS + tail hash CAS under fcntl.flock(LOCK_EX) critical section (takeover detection)
- Event kinds: `run_started`, `step_dispatched`, `step_completed`, `step_attested`, `nested_entered/exited`, `iteration_started/failed`, `for_each_expanded`, `item_started/completed/attested`, `produces_check_passed/failed`, `plan_mutated`, `claim/unclaim`

**Audit verbs** (Sprint 5b additions, run_audit.py:382+):
- `cmd_events_verify()` (line 384): hash chain validation; `--strict` replays plan_mutated through validator
- `cmd_events_tail()` (line 526): prints last N events; `-f` polls for new lines (follow mode)
- Both wired through `_dispatch_events()` in pipeline.py:293

**Inbox** (inbox.py):
- Schema v2: `plan_step_path`, `step_version`, `item_id?`, `decision` (approve/retry/abort), `submitted_by`, `submitted_by_kind`
- `cmd_next` calls `scan_inbox()` → `consume_inbox_entry()` before peeking next step
- Valid entries → `.consumed/`, stale/superseded → `.rejected/`, invalid → stay in inbox or fail

## 4. Pipeline Integration (pipeline.py)

**Entry points** (pipeline.py:87–234):
- **Gate dispatch** (line 87–129): LIFECYCLE_VERBS (start, next, ack, abort, status, runs, claim, unclaim, etc.) bypass implicit gate at line 96
- `astrid start <pack>.<name> --project <slug>` → `cmd_start()` (line 191) — loads compiled JSON → writes plan.json → creates active_run.json
- `astrid next --project <slug>` → `cmd_next()` (line 195) — consumes inbox, peeks via gate, prints dispatch
- `astrid ack <step> ... ` → `cmd_ack()` (line 199) — lifecycle_ack.py attests step
- **Task-mode gate** (line 96): `task_gate.gate_command()` picks current step and routes to adapter dispatch
- **Sprint 5b events** (line 293): `_dispatch_events()` → `cmd_events_verify/tail()`

**Post-dispatch record** (line 118–129): wraps `record_dispatch_complete()` in writer context (T9 migration)

## 5. Sprint 5 Changes

- **plan.py** (line 375): removed legacy `_read_legacy_plan_payload()`; upgraded error msg to point to migration script for v1→v2
- **run_audit.py** (line 56): changed mutation_count to tally `plan_mutated` events (not discrete `step_added/removed`)
- **run_audit.py** (382–620): added `cmd_events_verify()` with `--strict` validation and `cmd_events_tail()` with `-f` follow
- **pipeline.py** (line 293, 423–446): wired `astrid events {verify,tail}` dispatcher; updated help text (line 768)

**File line refs**: claim.py:1–80, lifecycle.py:496–650, gate.py:472–625, plan_verbs.py:45–80, events.py:1–100, inbox.py:1–150, run_audit.py:17–620, pipeline.py:87–450.

# Feature: optional steps + step_skipped event

The Astrid task kernel is an event-sourced plan executor. Plan trees live in `projects/<slug>/plan.json`; runs replay `runs/<run-id>/events.jsonl` to derive cursor position. See `astrid/core/task/{plan,events,gate,lifecycle,validator,inbox,run_audit}.py` and `astrid/pipeline.py`.

Today every step is mandatory — the cursor walks all of them. We want to add **optional** steps that an operator can skip without aborting the run.

## Implementation spec

### 1. Schema — `astrid/core/task/plan.py`

Add to the `Step` dataclass (currently ends at line ~99 with `poll_interval_seconds`):

```python
optional: bool = False
```

In `__post_init__` (line 108+) or `_validate_step` (find it in the same file), enforce:
- `optional=True` is incompatible with `requires_ack=True` — raise `TaskPlanError` with a clear message.
- `optional=True` is allowed on both leaf and group steps, and on steps with `repeat`.

Also wire `optional` through plan serialization (`to_dict`/`as_dict` and the parser in `_load_step`/`_parse_step` — find by following the `repeat` field's serialization to match the pattern). Default-false fields should be omitted from serialized output when False, matching existing conventions.

### 2. New event kinds — `astrid/core/task/events.py`

Add factories mirroring `make_step_attested_event` (line 390) and the item-level equivalents:

- `make_step_skipped_event(path: str, *, actor_kind: Literal["agent","actor"], actor_id: str, reason: str | None = None)` — emits `{"kind": "step_skipped", "step": path, "actor_kind": ..., "actor_id": ..., "reason": ...?, ...}`. Omit `reason` if None (follow the None-omission convention used by `make_step_completed_event` etc).
- `make_item_skipped_event(...)` — same shape but for `for_each` items; mirror `make_item_attested_event` if present (search for `item_attested`).

Export both via the `__all__` list at the bottom of the file. Update any event-kind registries if you find one (grep for the existing kinds to confirm).

### 3. Cursor advance — `astrid/core/task/gate.py`

Around line 275 there's a branch:
```python
elif kind in ("step_completed", "step_attested"):
```
Add `"step_skipped"` to that tuple. Do the same for any `item_completed`/`item_attested` group (search the file). For group steps, ensure `_auto_traverse_to_leaf()` treats a `step_skipped` event on the group as "frame popped, don't descend" — read the function and adapt; if the existing skip-already-completed logic handles this naturally, great, just verify with a test.

For `repeat.until` bodies, a `step_skipped` on the iteration's body should bail the loop cleanly — no `iteration_exhausted` event, no `on_exhaust` escalation. Trace the loop logic and confirm; add handling if needed.

### 4. Validator — `astrid/core/task/validator.py`

When the validator replays events (used by `events verify --strict` in `run_audit.py:382+`), reject any `step_skipped`/`item_skipped` event whose target step has `optional=False` in the resolved plan version. Look at how the validator currently checks event legality and add a parallel check.

### 5. New CLI verbs

**`astrid skip <step-path>`** — new `cmd_skip` in `astrid/core/task/lifecycle.py`, modeled on `cmd_ack` (line ~373).
- Args: `<step-path>` positional, `--project <slug>`, `--reason "..."` (optional), `--item <id>` (optional, for for_each).
- Resolves step against active run's plan, asserts `optional=True`, asserts the step is the current cursor frontier (i.e. would be the next dispatch) — refuse to skip arbitrary future steps.
- Emits `step_skipped` (or `item_skipped` if `--item`) via `append_event_locked()` under the writer epoch + tail-hash CAS, same pattern as `cmd_ack`.

**`astrid next --skip [--reason "..."]`** — in `cmd_next` (lifecycle.py:~549), if `--skip` is set, after `peek_current_step()` resolves the leaf:
- If the peeked step is `optional=True`, emit `step_skipped` instead of printing the dispatch, then loop back and re-peek.
- If `optional=False`, exit non-zero with a clear error.

Wire `skip` into `LIFECYCLE_VERBS` in `astrid/pipeline.py:87-129` so it bypasses the implicit gate.

### 6. Inbox — `astrid/core/task/inbox.py`

Extend the v2 decision enum to include `"skip"` alongside `approve|retry|abort`. In `consume_inbox_entry()`, route `decision: "skip"` to emit `step_skipped` (or `item_skipped` if the entry has `item_id`) — but only if the target step is `optional=True`; otherwise reject to `.rejected/` with a clear reason.

### 7. Audit — `astrid/core/task/run_audit.py`

- Add `skipped_steps` count to the run summary tally near `mutation_count` (line ~56). Count distinct `step_skipped` + `item_skipped` events.
- In `cmd_events_verify`'s `--strict` branch (line 384+), reject runs containing skips of non-optional steps. This is partly redundant with the validator change but provides a defense-in-depth check on already-written event logs.

### 8. Tests

The repo uses pytest. There's a `tests/` dir and a `tests/task/` subdir already in flight. Add:

- **`tests/task/test_optional_steps.py`** (new file):
  - `optional=True` + `requires_ack=True` rejected at plan load with `TaskPlanError`.
  - `optional=True` round-trips through serialization.
  - `cmd_skip` on optional leaf: emits `step_skipped`, cursor advances.
  - `cmd_skip` on non-optional step: exits non-zero, no event emitted.
  - `astrid next --skip` on optional leaf advances; on mandatory step errors cleanly.
  - Group step with `optional=True` and `cmd_skip`: cursor advances past entire subtree, no descendant `step_dispatched` events.
  - `for_each` parent with `optional=False`, item-level skip via `--item`: that item skipped, others run.
  - `repeat.until` body skip: loop ends, no `iteration_exhausted`.
  - Validator rejects illegal skip via `events verify --strict`.

- Extend `tests/test_task_kernel_e2e.py` and `tests/test_task_kernel_events.py` (both already modified per `git status`) with one e2e case each — but be careful, those files have user WIP. **Read them first**, then add tests at the end without touching existing test logic. If you can't cleanly extend without conflict, put all new tests in `test_optional_steps.py`.

- If `tests/task/test_events_verify.py` exists (it's untracked), add a strict-mode rejection case there too.

## How to work

1. Start by reading the existing structure end-to-end: `plan.py` (full file), `events.py:1-100 + 280-440 + 770-790`, `gate.py:140-340 + 950-1300`, `lifecycle.py:373-700`, `validator.py` (whole), `inbox.py` (whole), `run_audit.py:1-100 + 380-540`, `pipeline.py:80-300`. Don't skip this — the cursor/event flow is subtle and you'll waste time if you guess.
2. Implement in this order: schema → event factories → cursor advance → CLI verbs → inbox → validator → audit → tests.
3. After each module change, run only the targeted tests for that module to avoid burning time on irrelevant failures.
4. At the end run `python -m pytest tests/ -x -q` (or `pytest tests/task/ tests/test_task_kernel_e2e.py tests/test_task_kernel_events.py -x -q` if the full suite is too slow) and make sure it's green. **Do not skip failing tests; fix the underlying issue.**
5. Do NOT commit. Just leave changes in the working tree.

## Reporting back

When done, give a concise report (~300 words):
- Files modified (with line ranges).
- Files added.
- Test command + pass/fail summary.
- Anything you had to deviate from the spec, and why.
- Any followups the human should know about (e.g. design call-outs you made that warrant review).

If you hit a fundamental design problem mid-implementation that the spec doesn't cover, stop and report rather than papering over it.

198	def step_dir_for_path(
199	    slug: str,
200	    run_id: str,
201	    plan_step_path: tuple[str, ...],
202	    *,
203	    step_version: int = 1,
204	    iteration: int | None = None,
205	    item_id: str | None = None,
206	    root: str | Path | None = None,
207	) -> Path:
208	    validate_project_slug(slug)
209	    validate_run_id(run_id)
210	    if not plan_step_path:
211	        raise TaskPlanError("plan_step_path must contain at least one segment")
212	    for segment in plan_step_path:
213	        validate_run_id(segment)
214	    if not isinstance(step_version, int) or isinstance(step_version, bool) or step_version < 1:
215	        raise TaskPlanError("step_dir_for_path: step_version must be an int >= 1")
216	    if iteration is not None and item_id is not None:
217	        raise TaskPlanError("step_dir_for_path: iteration and item_id are mutually exclusive")
218	    base = project_dir(slug, root=root) / "runs" / run_id / "steps"
219	    for idx, segment in enumerate(plan_step_path):
220	        base = base / segment
221	        # Versioned segment lives directly under the leaf step id only.
222	        if idx == len(plan_step_path) - 1:
223	            base = base / f"v{step_version}"
224	    if iteration is not None:
225	        if not isinstance(iteration, int) or isinstance(iteration, bool) or iteration < 1:
226	            raise TaskPlanError("step_dir_for_path: iteration must be an int >= 1")
227	        base = base / "iterations" / f"{iteration:03d}"
228	    elif item_id is not None:
229	        validate_run_id(item_id)
230	        base = base / "items" / item_id
231	    return base

238	    *,
239	    step_version: int = 1,
240	    root: str | Path | None = None,
241	) -> Path:
242	    return step_dir_for_path(slug, run_id, (plan_step_id,), step_version=step_version, root=root)

245	def iter_steps_with_path(plan: TaskPlan) -> Iterator[tuple[tuple[str, ...], Step]]:
246	    """Yield ``(path, step)`` in pre-order for every step in the plan tree."""
247	
248	    def _walk(steps: tuple[Step, ...], prefix: tuple[str, ...]) -> Iterator[tuple[tuple[str, ...], Step]]:
249	        for step in steps:
250	            path = prefix + (step.id,)
251	            yield path, step
252	            if step.children is not None:
253	                yield from _walk(step.children, path)
254	
255	    yield from _walk(plan.steps, ())

258	def parse_from_ref(from_ref: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
259	    sep = ".produces."
260	    idx = from_ref.find(sep)
261	    if idx <= 0 or idx + len(sep) >= len(from_ref):
262	        raise TaskPlanError(
263	            f"repeat.for_each.from must match '<step-id>.produces.<name>', got {from_ref!r}"
264	        )
265	    return from_ref[:idx], from_ref[idx + len(sep):]
266	
267	
268	def _parse_assignee(assignee: str, *, step_id: str) -> tuple[AssigneeForm, str | None]:
269	    """Validate assignee string and return (form, identity-or-None)."""
270	    if not isinstance(assignee, str) or not assignee:
271	        raise TaskPlanError(f"step {step_id!r}: assignee must be a non-empty string")
272	    if assignee in ("system", "any-agent", "any-human"):
273	        return assignee, None  # type: ignore[return-value]
274	    for prefix, kind in (("agent:", "agent"), ("human:", "actor")):
275	        if assignee.startswith(prefix):
276	            ident = assignee[len(prefix):]
277	            if not ident:
278	                raise TaskPlanError(
279	                    f"step {step_id!r}: assignee {assignee!r} missing identity after {prefix!r}"
280	                )
281	            return kind, ident  # type: ignore[return-value]
282	    raise TaskPlanError(
283	        f"step {step_id!r}: assignee must be one of 'system'|'any-agent'|'any-human'|'agent:<id>'|'human:<name>', got {assignee!r}"
284	    )

286	
287	def _step_to_dict(step: Step) -> dict[str, Any]:
288	    out: dict[str, Any] = {"id": step.id, "adapter": step.adapter}
289	    if step.version != 1:
290	        out["version"] = step.version
291	    if step.requires_ack:
292	        out["requires_ack"] = True
293	    if step.assignee != "system":
294	        out["assignee"] = step.assignee
295	    if step.command is not None:
296	        out["command"] = step.command
297	    if step.instructions is not None:
298	        out["instructions"] = step.instructions
299	    if step.children is not None:
300	        out["children"] = [_step_to_dict(c) for c in step.children]
301	    if step.produces:
302	        out["produces"] = _produces_to_dict(step.produces)
303	    if step.repeat is not None:
304	        out["repeat"] = _repeat_to_dict(step.repeat)
305	    if step.ack is not None:
306	        out["ack"] = {"kind": step.ack.kind}
307	    if step.cost is not None:
308	        out["cost"] = {"amount": step.cost.amount, "currency": step.cost.currency, "source": step.cost.source}
309	    if step.superseded_by is not None:
310	        out["superseded_by"] = {"to_version": step.superseded_by.to_version, "scope": step.superseded_by.scope}
311	    if step.re_export is not None:
312	        out["re_export"] = {name: ref for name, ref in step.re_export}
313	    if step.poll_interval_seconds != 30:
314	        out["poll_interval_seconds"] = step.poll_interval_seconds
315	    return out
316	
317	
318	def _produces_to_dict(produces: tuple[ProducesEntry, ...]) -> dict[str, Any]:
319	    sorted_entries = sorted(produces, key=lambda entry: entry.name)
320	    result: dict[str, Any] = {}
321	    for entry in sorted_entries:
322	        item: dict[str, Any] = {
323	            "path": entry.path,
324	            "check": {
325	                "check_id": entry.check.check_id,
326	                "params": canonical_check_params(entry.check.params),
327	                "sentinel": entry.check.sentinel,
328	            },
329	        }
330	        if entry.checksum is not None:
331	            item["checksum"] = entry.checksum
332	        result[entry.name] = item
333	    return result
334	
335	
336	def _repeat_to_dict(repeat: Repeat) -> dict[str, Any]:
337	    if isinstance(repeat, RepeatUntil):
338	        out: dict[str, Any] = {
339	            "until": repeat.condition,
340	            "max_iterations": repeat.max_iterations,
341	            "on_exhaust": repeat.on_exhaust,
342	        }
343	        if repeat.quorum_n is not None:
344	            out["quorum_n"] = repeat.quorum_n
345	        return out
346	    if isinstance(repeat, RepeatForEach):
347	        if repeat.items_source == "static":
348	            return {"for_each": {"items": list(repeat.items)}}
349	        return {"for_each": {"from": repeat.from_ref}}
350	    raise TaskPlanError(f"unknown repeat type: {type(repeat)!r}")

353	def _read_plan_payload(plan_path: str | Path) -> Any:
354	    path = Path(plan_path)
355	    try:
356	        return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
357	    except FileNotFoundError:
358	        raise
359	    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
360	        raise TaskPlanError(f"invalid JSON in {path}: {exc.msg}") from exc
361	    except OSError as exc:
362	        raise TaskPlanError(f"failed to read {path}: {exc}") from exc
363	
364	
365	def _validate_plan(payload: Any, *, _is_root: bool = True) -> TaskPlan:
366	    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
367	        raise TaskPlanError("plan must be an object")
368	    plan_id = payload.get("plan_id")
369	    version = payload.get("version")
370	    steps = payload.get("steps")
371	    if not isinstance(plan_id, str) or not plan_id:
372	        raise TaskPlanError("plan plan_id must be a non-empty string")
373	    if version != 2 or isinstance(version, bool):
374	        raise TaskPlanError(
375	            "plan version must be 2; if you have a legacy v1 plan, "
376	            "run scripts/migrations/sprint-3/migrate_plans.py to migrate it"
377	        )
378	    if not isinstance(steps, list):
379	        raise TaskPlanError("plan steps must be a list")
380	
381	    validated_steps: list[Step] = []
382	    for index, step in enumerate(steps):
383	        validated_steps.append(_validate_step(step, index, validated_steps))
384	
385	    plan = TaskPlan(plan_id=plan_id, version=2, steps=tuple(validated_steps))
386	    if _is_root:
387	        _assert_unique_paths(plan)
388	    return plan
389	
390	
391	def _validate_step(step: Any, index: int, prior_siblings: list[Step]) -> Step:
392	    if not isinstance(step, dict):
393	        raise TaskPlanError(f"plan steps[{index}] must be an object")
394	    step_id_raw = step.get("id")
395	    if not isinstance(step_id_raw, str) or not step_id_raw:
396	        raise TaskPlanError(f"plan steps[{index}].id must be a non-empty string")
397	    step_id = validate_run_id(step_id_raw)
398	
399	    adapter = step.get("adapter", "local")
400	    if adapter not in ADAPTERS:
401	        raise TaskPlanError(
402	            f"plan steps[{index}].adapter must be one of {ADAPTERS}, got {adapter!r}"
403	        )
404	
405	    requires_ack = step.get("requires_ack", False)
406	    if not isinstance(requires_ack, bool):
407	        raise TaskPlanError(f"plan steps[{index}].requires_ack must be a bool")
408	
409	    assignee = step.get("assignee", "system")
410	    _parse_assignee(assignee, step_id=step_id)  # validates shape
411	
412	    command = step.get("command")
413	    instructions = step.get("instructions")
414	    raw_children = step.get("children")
415	
416	    if command is not None and not (isinstance(command, str) and command):
417	        raise TaskPlanError(f"plan steps[{index}].command must be a non-empty string when present")
418	    if instructions is not None and not (isinstance(instructions, str) and instructions):
419	        raise TaskPlanError(f"plan steps[{index}].instructions must be a non-empty string when present")
420	
421	    children: tuple[Step, ...] | None = None
422	    if raw_children is not None:
423	        if not isinstance(raw_children, list):
424	            raise TaskPlanError(f"plan steps[{index}].children must be a list")
425	        if command is not None:
426	            raise TaskPlanError(f"plan steps[{index}]: leaf (command) and group (children) are mutually exclusive")
427	        validated_children: list[Step] = []
428	        for child_idx, child in enumerate(raw_children):
429	            validated_children.append(_validate_step(child, child_idx, validated_children))
430	        children = tuple(validated_children)
431	    elif command is None:
432	        raise TaskPlanError(f"plan steps[{index}]: must define either 'command' or 'children'")
433	
434	    produces = _validate_produces(step.get("produces"), index, allow_legacy_list=(adapter == "local" and children is None))

541	def _validate_produces(raw: Any, index: int, *, allow_legacy_list: bool) -> tuple[ProducesEntry, ...]:
542	    if raw is None:

562	                )

566	    if isinstance(raw, dict):

619	def _validate_repeat(raw: Any, index: int, prior_siblings: list[Step]) -> Repeat | None:
620	    if raw is None:

639	def _validate_repeat_until(raw: dict[str, Any], index: int) -> RepeatUntil:
640	    condition = raw.get("until")
641	    if condition not in {"user_approves", "verifier_passes", "quorum"}:
642	        raise TaskPlanError(

644	        )

646	    if not isinstance(max_iterations, int) or isinstance(max_iterations, bool) or max_iterations < 1:
647	        raise TaskPlanError(
648	            f"plan steps[{index}].repeat.max_iterations must be an int >= 1"
649	        )
650	    on_exhaust = raw.get("on_exhaust")
651	    if on_exhaust not in {"escalate", "fail"}:
652	        raise TaskPlanError(
653	            f"plan steps[{index}].repeat.on_exhaust must be 'escalate' or 'fail', got {on_exhaust!r}"
654	        )
655	    quorum_n = raw.get("quorum_n")
656	    if condition == "quorum":
657	        if not isinstance(quorum_n, int) or isinstance(quorum_n, bool) or quorum_n < 1:
658	            raise TaskPlanError(

660	            )
661	    elif quorum_n is not None:

663	            f"plan steps[{index}].repeat.quorum_n only valid when until='quorum'"
664	        )
665	    return RepeatUntil(

667	        max_iterations=max_iterations,
668	        on_exhaust=on_exhaust,

673	def _validate_repeat_for_each(raw: Any, index: int, prior_siblings: list[Step]) -> RepeatForEach:
674	    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
675	        raise TaskPlanError(
676	            f"plan steps[{index}].repeat.for_each must be an object"
677	        )
678	    has_items = "items" in raw
679	    has_from = "from" in raw
680	    if has_items == has_from:
681	        raise TaskPlanError(
682	            f"plan steps[{index}].repeat.for_each must have exactly one of 'items' or 'from'"
683	        )
684	    if has_items:
685	        items = raw["items"]
686	        if not isinstance(items, list) or not all(isinstance(x, str) and x for x in items):
687	            raise TaskPlanError(
688	                f"plan steps[{index}].repeat.for_each.items must be a list of non-empty strings"
689	            )
690	        return RepeatForEach(items_source="static", items=tuple(items), from_ref=None)
691	    from_ref = raw["from"]
692	    if not isinstance(from_ref, str) or not from_ref:
693	        raise TaskPlanError(
694	            f"plan steps[{index}].repeat.for_each.from must be a non-empty string"
695	        )
696	    target_id, produces_name = parse_from_ref(from_ref)
697	    target = next((s for s in prior_siblings if s.id == target_id), None)
698	    if target is None:
699	        raise TaskPlanError(
700	            f"plan steps[{index}].repeat.for_each.from references unknown prior sibling step {target_id!r}"
701	        )
702	    if not any(p.name == produces_name for p in target.produces):
703	        raise TaskPlanError(
704	            f"plan steps[{index}].repeat.for_each.from references unknown produces {produces_name!r} on step {target_id!r}"
705	        )
706	    return RepeatForEach(items_source="from", items=(), from_ref=from_ref)

709	def _reject_orchestrators_run(step: Step) -> None:
710	    """Reject leaf local-adapter steps that try to shell out to `astrid orchestrators run`."""
711	    if step.command is None:
712	        return
713	    try:
714	        tokens = shlex.split(step.command)
715	    except ValueError as exc:
716	        raise TaskPlanError(
717	            f"step {step.id!r}: command is not shell-parseable: {exc}"
718	        ) from exc
719	    tokens = _strip_astrid_prefix(tokens)
720	    if len(tokens) >= 2 and tokens[0] == "orchestrators" and tokens[1] == "run":
721	        raise TaskPlanError(
722	            f"step {step.id!r}: local-adapter command targets 'astrid orchestrators run'; use a group step (children) instead"
723	        )
724	
725	
726	def _strip_astrid_prefix(tokens: list[str]) -> list[str]:
727	    if (
728	        len(tokens) >= 3
729	        and Path(tokens[0]).name.startswith("python")
730	        and tokens[1:3] == ["-m", "astrid"]
731	    ):
732	        return tokens[3:]
733	    if tokens and Path(tokens[0]).name.endswith("astrid"):
734	        return tokens[1:]
735	    return tokens
736	
737	
738	def _assert_unique_paths(plan: TaskPlan) -> None:
739	    """Reject sibling-id collisions inside any one frame."""
740	
741	    def _check(steps: tuple[Step, ...], prefix: tuple[str, ...]) -> None:
742	        seen: set[str] = set()
743	        for step in steps:
744	            if step.id in seen:
745	                location = STEP_PATH_SEP.join(prefix + (step.id,))
746	                raise TaskPlanError(
747	                    f"duplicate step id {step.id!r} among siblings at {location!r}"
748	                )
749	            seen.add(step.id)
750	            if step.children is not None:
751	                _check(step.children, prefix + (step.id,))
752	
753	    _check(plan.steps, ())
754
