1	"""Hash-chained task-run event log.
2	
3	Sprint 1 — apex contract: every event appended to ``runs/<id>/events.jsonl``
4	is gated by a single :func:`fcntl.flock(LOCK_EX)` critical section that
5	re-reads the tail hash and the lease's ``writer_epoch`` *under the lock*
6	before computing the new prev-hash and writing the line. Tail CAS catches
7	concurrent appenders; epoch CAS catches stale writers that lost a takeover
8	race. The takeover event in ``events.jsonl`` is observability — the lock +
9	two CAS checks are the actual fence (see DEC-007).
10	
11	``verify_chain`` is retained as an offline / audit primitive only. The
12	production hot path uses :func:`append_event_locked` which relies on the
13	tail-hash CAS for integrity (DEC-007 / FLAG-019 — events do not store a
14	``prev_hash`` field, so a v3-style "re-verify the last link" is not
15	expressible without reading the full chain).
16	"""
17	
18	from __future__ import annotations
19	
20	import errno
21	import fcntl
22	import hashlib
23	import json
24	import os
25	from datetime import UTC, datetime
26	from pathlib import Path
27	from typing import Any
28	
29	ZERO_HASH = "sha256:" + "0" * 64
30	
31	LEASE_FILENAME = "lease.json"
32	EVENTS_FILENAME = "events.jsonl"
33	
34	_TAIL_SEEK_INITIAL_WINDOW = 4096
35	
36	
37	class EventLogError(RuntimeError):
38	    """Raised when a task event log cannot be read or written."""
39	
40	
41	class StaleTailError(EventLogError):
42	    """Raised when the on-disk tail hash differs from the writer's expectation.
43	
44	    Carries the conflicting hashes in ``.expected`` / ``.actual`` and the same
45	    values are interpolated into the message so log scrapers can pick them up.
46	    """
47	
48	    def __init__(self, *, expected: str, actual: str) -> None:
49	        self.expected: str = expected
50	        self.actual: str = actual
51	        super().__init__(
52	            f"stale tail: expected prev_hash={expected!r} but events.jsonl tail is {actual!r}"
53	        )
54	
55	
56	class StaleEpochError(EventLogError):
57	    """Raised when the on-disk ``writer_epoch`` differs from the writer's expectation.
58	
59	    Carries the conflicting epochs in ``.expected`` / ``.actual``.
60	    ``expected`` may be ``None`` when the caller intentionally bypasses
61	    the epoch CAS (e.g. sweeper ``--hard`` mode).
62	    """
63	
64	    def __init__(self, *, expected: int | None, actual: int) -> None:
65	        self.expected: int | None = expected
66	        self.actual: int = actual
67	        super().__init__(
68	            f"stale epoch: expected writer_epoch={expected!r} but lease.json holds {actual!r}"
69	        )
70	
71	
72	class NotWriterError(EventLogError):
73	    """Raised when a session attempts to append without holding the lease.
74	
75	    Carries the offending session id and the actual lease writer in
76	    ``.session_id`` / ``.writer_id`` (the writer may be ``None`` for an
77	    orphan-pending lease).
78	    """
79	
80	    def __init__(self, *, session_id: str, writer_id: str | None) -> None:
81	        self.session_id: str = session_id
82	        self.writer_id: str | None = writer_id
83	        super().__init__(
84	            f"session {session_id!r} is not the writer for this run "
85	            f"(lease.attached_session_id={writer_id!r})"
86	        )
87	
88	
89	def canonical_event_json(event: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
90	    payload = {key: value for key, value in event.items() if key != "hash"}
91	    return json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=False)
92	
93	
94	def append_event_locked(
95	    run_dir: str | Path,
96	    event: dict[str, Any],
97	    *,
98	    expected_writer_epoch: int | None = None,
99	    expected_prev_hash: str,
100	) -> dict[str, Any]:
101	    """Atomically append ``event`` to ``run_dir/events.jsonl``.
102	
103	    Holds a single ``fcntl.flock(LOCK_EX)`` on the events file across:
104	      1. Tail re-read + CAS against ``expected_prev_hash`` → :class:`StaleTailError`.
105	      2. ``lease.json`` re-read + CAS against ``expected_writer_epoch`` →
106	         :class:`StaleEpochError` (skipped when *expected_writer_epoch* is ``None``).
107	      3. New event hash computation, append, ``flush``+``os.fsync``.
108	
109	    Raises :class:`StaleTailError` / :class:`StaleEpochError` on CAS failure
110	    with no retry. Callers (typically :class:`WriterContext`) decide whether
111	    to surface or rebind.
112	
113	    When *expected_writer_epoch* is ``None``, the epoch CAS check is
114	    **skipped entirely** — only the tail-hash CAS runs.  This is safe
115	    for the sweeper's ``--hard`` mode where the caller has already
116	    decided to force-terminate and does not want to race on epoch.
117	    """
118	
119	    run_path = Path(run_dir)
120	    events_path = run_path / EVENTS_FILENAME
121	    lease_path = run_path / LEASE_FILENAME
122	    run_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
123	
124	    created = not events_path.exists()
125	    try:
126	        # ``a+b`` opens for append+read in binary mode; the OS guarantees the
127	        # write happens at end-of-file under POSIX append semantics, and we
128	        # additionally hold an exclusive flock across the full critical
129	        # section so concurrent writers serialize.
130	        with events_path.open("a+b") as handle:
131	            fcntl.flock(handle.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_EX)
132	            try:
133	                tail_hash = _read_tail_hash(handle)
134	                if tail_hash != expected_prev_hash:
135	                    raise StaleTailError(expected=expected_prev_hash, actual=tail_hash)
136	
137	                if expected_writer_epoch is not None:
138	                    actual_epoch = _read_lease_epoch(lease_path)
139	                    if actual_epoch != expected_writer_epoch:
140	                        raise StaleEpochError(
141	                            expected=expected_writer_epoch, actual=actual_epoch
142	                        )
143	
144	                stored = dict(event)
145	                stored.pop("hash", None)
146	                stored["hash"] = _event_hash(tail_hash, stored)
147	
148	                line = (
149	                    json.dumps(
150	                        stored,
151	                        sort_keys=True,
152	                        separators=(",", ":"),
153	                        ensure_ascii=False,
154	                    )
155	                    + "\n"
156	                ).encode("utf-8")
157	                handle.seek(0, os.SEEK_END)
158	                handle.write(line)
159	                handle.flush()
160	                os.fsync(handle.fileno())
161	            finally:
162	                fcntl.flock(handle.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_UN)
163	        if created:
164	            _fsync_dir(run_path)
165	    except (StaleTailError, StaleEpochError, NotWriterError):
166	        raise
167	    except OSError as exc:
168	        raise EventLogError(f"failed to append event to {events_path}: {exc}") from exc
169	    return stored
170	
171	
172	def append_event(path: str | Path, event: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
173	    """Test/migration only. Production code MUST use WriterContext.
174	
175	    Reads the current ``(writer_epoch, tail_hash)`` from disk and calls
176	    :func:`append_event_locked` once. Raises :class:`StaleTailError` /
177	    :class:`StaleEpochError` / :class:`NotWriterError` with no retry.
178	    """
179	
180	    events_path = Path(path)
181	    run_dir = events_path.parent
182	    lease_path = run_dir / LEASE_FILENAME
183	    # Read tail without taking the lock — append_event_locked re-reads under
184	    # the lock and CAS-checks. This pre-read just supplies an "expected"
185	    # value so the contract shape matches the locked helper.
186	    pre_tail = _peek_tail_hash(events_path)
187	    pre_epoch = _read_lease_epoch(lease_path)
188	    return append_event_locked(
189	        run_dir,
190	        event,
191	        expected_writer_epoch=pre_epoch,
192	        expected_prev_hash=pre_tail,
193	    )
194	
195	
196	def verify_chain(path: str | Path) -> tuple[bool, int, str | None]:
197	    """Walk ``events.jsonl`` end-to-end and verify every prev-hash link.
198	
199	    Audit primitive: callers must NOT use this on the hot append path.
200	    The locked-append contract relies on tail-only CAS (DEC-007); this
201	    function exists so offline tooling can still detect mid-chain
202	    corruption.
203	    """
204	
205	    events_path = Path(path)
206	    try:
207	        handle = events_path.open("r", encoding="utf-8")
208	    except FileNotFoundError:
209	        return True, -1, None
210	    except OSError as exc:
211	        return False, -1, f"failed to read {events_path}: {exc}"
212	
213	    prev_hash = ZERO_HASH
214	    last_index = -1
215	    with handle:
216	        for index, line in enumerate(handle):
217	            if not line.endswith("\n"):
218	                return False, index, f"event log line {index + 1} is not newline-terminated"
219	            raw = line[:-1]
220	            if not raw:
221	                return False, index, f"event log line {index + 1} is empty"
222	            try:
223	                event = json.loads(raw)
224	            except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
225	                return False, index, f"invalid JSON on event log line {index + 1}: {exc.msg}"
226	            if not isinstance(event, dict):
227	                return False, index, f"event log line {index + 1} is not an object"
228	            stored_hash = event.get("hash")
229	            if not isinstance(stored_hash, str):
230	                return False, index, f"event log line {index + 1} is missing hash"
231	            expected_hash = _event_hash(prev_hash, event)
232	            if stored_hash != expected_hash:
233	                return (
234	                    False,
235	                    index,
236	                    f"event log line {index + 1} hash mismatch: expected {expected_hash}, got {stored_hash}",
237	                )
238	            prev_hash = stored_hash
239	            last_index = index
240	    return True, last_index, None
241	
242	
243	def read_events(path: str | Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
244	    events_path = Path(path)
245	    try:
246	        with events_path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
247	            return [json.loads(line) for line in handle]
248	    except FileNotFoundError:
249	        return []
250	    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
251	        raise EventLogError(f"invalid JSON in {events_path}: {exc.msg}") from exc
252	    except OSError as exc:
253	        raise EventLogError(f"failed to read {events_path}: {exc}") from exc

571	
572	def _run_is_complete(plan: Any, events: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> bool:
573	    """Return True only when all leaf steps are terminal-non-aborted.
574	
575	    Terminal-non-aborted means the step has a ``step_completed`` or
576	    ``step_failed`` event and is NOT in ``awaiting_fetch`` or ``dispatched``
577	    without terminal follow-up.
578	    """
579	    # Lazy import to avoid circular dependency with plan.py.
580	    from astrid.core.task.plan import Step  # noqa: PLC0415
581	
582	    # Collect all leaf step ids from the plan.
583	    leaf_ids: set[str] = set()
584	
585	    def _collect_leaves(steps: tuple[Step, ...]) -> None:
586	        for step in steps:
587	            if step.children is None:
588	                leaf_ids.add(step.id)
589	            else:
590	                _collect_leaves(step.children)
591	
592	    if hasattr(plan, "steps") and plan.steps is not None:
593	        _collect_leaves(plan.steps)
594	
595	    if not leaf_ids:
596	        return False
597	
598	    # Map step path -> latest event kind for terminal checks.
599	    latest_by_path: dict[str, str] = {}
600	    for event in events:
601	        path_list = event.get("plan_step_path")
602	        if isinstance(path_list, list) and path_list:
603	            path_str = "/".join(str(p) for p in path_list)
604	            kind = event.get("kind")
605	            if isinstance(kind, str):
606	                latest_by_path[path_str] = kind
607	
608	    for leaf_id in leaf_ids:
609	        # Find latest event whose path matches this leaf id.
610	        # Events use plan_step_path which is a list; the last element
611	        # is typically the step's own id for leaf steps.
612	        latest_kind: str | None = None
613	        for path_str, kind in latest_by_path.items():
614	            parts = path_str.split("/")
615	            if parts and parts[-1] == leaf_id:
616	                latest_kind = kind
617	
618	        if latest_kind is None:
619	            # No event at all for this leaf — not terminal.
620	            return False
621	        if latest_kind == "step_awaiting_fetch":
622	            return False
623	        if latest_kind == "step_dispatched":
624	            return False
625	        if latest_kind not in {"step_completed", "step_failed"}:
626	            return False
627	
628	    return True
629	
630	
631	def _event_hash(prev_hash: str, event: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
632	    digest = hashlib.sha256((prev_hash + canonical_event_json(event)).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
633	    return f"sha256:{digest}"
634	
635	
636	def _utc_now_iso() -> str:
637	    return datetime.now(UTC).isoformat().replace("+00:00", "Z")
638	
639	
640	def _read_tail_hash(handle) -> str:
641	    """Return the ``hash`` of the last non-empty JSONL line, or ZERO_HASH if empty.
642	
643	    Caller must already hold an exclusive flock on ``handle``. Reads a
644	    chunked window from end-of-file, doubling until a newline is found or
645	    the whole file has been read (no fixed cap — single events larger than
646	    any starting window are still handled).
647	    """
648	
649	    handle.seek(0, os.SEEK_END)
650	    file_size = handle.tell()
651	    if file_size == 0:
652	        return ZERO_HASH
653	
654	    window = min(_TAIL_SEEK_INITIAL_WINDOW, file_size)
655	    while True:
656	        start = max(0, file_size - window)
657	        handle.seek(start)
658	        chunk = handle.read(file_size - start)
659	        last_newline = chunk.rfind(b"\n")
660	        if start == 0:
661	            # Whole file in chunk.
662	            trimmed = chunk.rstrip(b"\n")
663	            nl = trimmed.rfind(b"\n")
664	            last_line = trimmed[nl + 1 :] if nl != -1 else trimmed
665	            break
666	        if last_newline == -1:
667	            if window >= file_size:
668	                # Walked off the start without finding any newline — file is
669	                # one giant line. Use the whole chunk.
670	                last_line = chunk.rstrip(b"\n")
671	                break
672	            window = min(window * 2, file_size)
673	            continue
674	        # We have at least one newline in the chunk; the final line is what
675	        # comes after the *last* newline of the rstripped chunk.
676	        trimmed = chunk.rstrip(b"\n")
677	        if not trimmed:
678	            return ZERO_HASH
679	        nl = trimmed.rfind(b"\n")
680	        if nl == -1:
681	            # Chunk after rstrip has no newline — need more.
682	            if window >= file_size:
683	                last_line = trimmed
684	                break
685	            window = min(window * 2, file_size)
686	            continue
687	        last_line = trimmed[nl + 1 :]
688	        break
689	
690	    if not last_line:
691	        return ZERO_HASH
692	    try:
693	        record = json.loads(last_line.decode("utf-8"))
694	    except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
695	        raise EventLogError(f"events.jsonl tail is not valid JSON: {exc}") from exc
696	    tail_hash = record.get("hash") if isinstance(record, dict) else None
697	    if not isinstance(tail_hash, str):
698	        raise EventLogError("events.jsonl tail is missing 'hash' field")
699	    return tail_hash
700	
701	
702	def _peek_tail_hash(events_path: Path) -> str:
703	    """Best-effort read of the tail hash without acquiring the flock.
704	
705	    Only the legacy :func:`append_event` wrapper uses this; the real CAS
706	    happens inside :func:`append_event_locked` under the lock.
707	    """
708	
709	    if not events_path.exists():
710	        return ZERO_HASH
711	    with events_path.open("rb") as handle:
712	        return _read_tail_hash(handle)
713	
714	
715	def _read_lease_epoch(lease_path: Path) -> int:
716	    """Return ``lease.json``'s ``writer_epoch`` (default 0 when absent / malformed key).
717	
718	    A missing lease file means the run pre-dates the Sprint 1 contract; the
719	    apex contract treats that as ``writer_epoch=0`` so the wrapper / legacy
720	    callers can still operate. Malformed JSON raises :class:`EventLogError`
721	    so corruption is loud.
722	    """
723	
724	    try:
725	        raw = lease_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
726	    except FileNotFoundError:
727	        return 0
728	    except OSError as exc:
729	        raise EventLogError(f"failed to read lease {lease_path}: {exc}") from exc
730	    try:
731	        data = json.loads(raw)
732	    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
733	        raise EventLogError(f"invalid JSON in lease {lease_path}: {exc.msg}") from exc
734	    if not isinstance(data, dict):
735	        raise EventLogError(f"lease {lease_path} must be a JSON object")
736	    epoch = data.get("writer_epoch", 0)
737	    if not isinstance(epoch, int) or isinstance(epoch, bool):
738	        raise EventLogError(
739	            f"lease {lease_path} writer_epoch must be an integer, got {epoch!r}"
740	        )
741	    return epoch
742	
743	
744	def _fsync_dir(path: Path) -> None:
745	    flags = getattr(os, "O_DIRECTORY", 0) | os.O_RDONLY
746	    fd: int | None = None
747	    try:
748	        fd = os.open(path, flags)
749	        os.fsync(fd)
750	    except OSError as exc:
751	        if exc.errno not in {errno.EINVAL, errno.ENOTSUP, errno.EBADF}:
752	            raise
753	    finally:
754	        if fd is not None:
755	            os.close(fd)
756	
757

76	class TaskRunGateError(RuntimeError):
77	    """Raised when task-mode dispatch is rejected."""
78	
79	    def __init__(self, reason: str, recovery: str) -> None:
80	        super().__init__(reason)
81	        self.reason = reason
82	        self.recovery = recovery

85	@dataclass(frozen=True)
86	class GateDecision:
87	    active: bool
88	    run_id: str | None = None
89	    plan_step_id: str | None = None
90	    events_path: Path | None = None
91	    reentry: bool = False
92	    step_kind: str | None = None
93	    slug: str | None = None
94	    plan_step_path: tuple[str, ...] = ()
95	    produces: tuple[ProducesEntry, ...] = ()
96	    project_root: Path | None = None
97	    iteration: int | None = None
98	    item_id: str | None = None
99	    # Sprint 1 (T9) extensions — populated when a session is bound at
100	    # gate_command entry so post-dispatch record_* helpers can flow
101	    # through writer_context_from_decision.
102	    run_dir: Path | None = None
103	    writer_epoch_at_dispatch: int | None = None
104	    session_id: str | None = None
105	    session: Any = None  # astrid.core.session.model.Session | None
106	    # Sprint 3 (T14) adapter dispatch fields.
107	    step_version: int = 1
108	    adapter: str | None = None
109	    pid: int | None = None

120	@dataclass
121	class _Frame:
122	    plan: TaskPlan
123	    path_prefix: tuple[str, ...]
124	    child_index: int = 0
125	    iteration: int | None = None
126	    item_id: str | None = None
127	    repeat_step_id: str | None = None

130	@dataclass
131	class CursorPath:
132	    frames: list[_Frame] = field(default_factory=list)
133	    for_each_progress: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=dict)
134	    pinned_failure: tuple[str, str] | None = None  # (reason, host_path) for iteration_exhausted=fail
135	
136	    @property
137	    def at_root_done(self) -> bool:
138	        return len(self.frames) == 1 and self.frames[-1].child_index >= len(self.frames[-1].plan.steps)
139	
140	    @property
141	    def top_exhausted(self) -> bool:
142	        top = self.frames[-1]
143	        return top.child_index >= len(top.plan.steps)
144	
145	
146	def derive_cursor(plan: TaskPlan, events: Sequence[dict[str, Any]], *, slug: str = "") -> CursorPath:
147	    """Replay ``events.jsonl`` left-to-right to reconstruct the path-stack cursor.
148	
149	    Reconstructible from events alone (supports partial-replay resume):
150	    ``nested_entered`` pushes, ``nested_exited`` pops + advances parent,
151	    ``step_completed`` / ``step_attested`` mark a step advance-eligible iff it
152	    has no produces; otherwise advance is deferred until the contiguous block
153	    contains a ``produces_check_passed`` for every declared produces name.
154	    ``produces_check_failed`` / ``cursor_rewind`` clear pending state without
155	    advancing. ``iteration_started`` pushes an iteration frame; ``iteration_failed``
156	    pops it without advancing the host. ``for_each_expanded`` records the host's
157	    item set on the cursor (used as the source of truth — derive_cursor never
158	    re-reads disk during replay). ``item_started`` pushes a per-item frame;
159	    ``item_completed`` / ``item_attested`` pop the item frame and advance the
160	    host once every item is done. ``step_dispatched`` / ``run_started`` do not
161	    advance.
162	    """
163	    frames: list[_Frame] = [_Frame(plan=plan, path_prefix=(), child_index=0)]
164	    pending: list[set[str] | None] = [None]
165	    for_each_progress: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
166	    pinned_failure: tuple[str, str] | None = None
167	    for event in events:
168	        kind = event.get("kind")
169	        if kind == "iteration_exhausted":
170	            on_exhaust = event.get("on_exhaust")
171	            host_path = _path_str_from_event(event)
172	            if on_exhaust == "fail":
173	                pinned_failure = ("repeat.until max_iterations exhausted", host_path)
174	                continue
175	            if on_exhaust == "escalate":
176	                top = frames[-1]
177	                if top.child_index < len(top.plan.steps):
178	                    host_step = top.plan.steps[top.child_index]
179	                    override_plan = TaskPlan(
180	                        plan_id=f"__exhaust_{host_step.id}",
181	                        version=1,
182	                        steps=(_make_exhaust_override_step(slug, host_path),),
183	                    )
184	                    frames.append(
185	                        _Frame(
186	                            plan=override_plan,
187	                            path_prefix=top.path_prefix + (host_step.id,),
188	                            child_index=0,
189	                            repeat_step_id=host_step.id,
190	                        )
191	                    )
192	                    pending.append(None)
193	            continue
194	        if kind == "nested_entered":
195	            top = frames[-1]
196	            if top.child_index >= len(top.plan.steps):
197	                raise TaskRunGateError(
198	                    reason="nested_entered points past end of frame",
199	                    recovery="inspect events.jsonl",
200	                )
201	            step = top.plan.steps[top.child_index]
202	            if not is_group_step(step):
203	                raise TaskRunGateError(
204	                    reason="nested_entered did not land on a group step",
205	                    recovery="inspect events.jsonl",
206	                )
207	            frames.append(
208	                _Frame(
209	                    plan=step.plan,
210	                    path_prefix=top.path_prefix + (step.id,),
211	                    child_index=0,
212	                )
213	            )
214	            pending.append(None)
215	        elif kind == "nested_exited":
216	            if len(frames) <= 1:
217	                raise TaskRunGateError(
218	                    reason="nested_exited at root frame",
219	                    recovery="inspect events.jsonl",
220	                )
221	            frames.pop()
222	            pending.pop()
223	            frames[-1].child_index += 1
224	            pending[-1] = None
225	        elif kind == "iteration_started":
226	            top = frames[-1]
227	            if top.child_index >= len(top.plan.steps):
228	                continue
229	            host_step = top.plan.steps[top.child_index]
230	            iteration = int(event.get("iteration", 1))
231	            frames.append(_make_iteration_frame(host_step, top.path_prefix, iteration))
232	            pending.append(None)
233	        elif kind == "iteration_failed":
234	            if frames[-1].repeat_step_id is None:
235	                continue
236	            frames.pop()
237	            pending.pop()
238	            pending[-1] = None
239	        elif kind == "for_each_expanded":
240	            host_path = _path_str_from_event(event)
241	            items = tuple(event.get("item_ids") or ())
242	            for_each_progress.setdefault(host_path, {"items": items, "completed": set()})
243	            for_each_progress[host_path]["items"] = items
244	        elif kind == "item_started":
245	            top = frames[-1]
246	            if top.child_index >= len(top.plan.steps):
247	                continue
248	            host_step = top.plan.steps[top.child_index]
249	            item_id = event.get("item_id")
250	            if not isinstance(item_id, str):
251	                continue
252	            frames.append(_make_item_frame(host_step, top.path_prefix, item_id))
253	            pending.append(None)
254	        elif kind in ("item_completed", "item_attested"):
255	            host_path = _path_str_from_event(event)
256	            item_id = event.get("item_id")
257	            if not isinstance(item_id, str):
258	                continue
259	            entry = for_each_progress.setdefault(host_path, {"items": (), "completed": set()})
260	            entry["completed"].add(item_id)
261	            if frames[-1].item_id == item_id:
262	                frames.pop()
263	                pending.pop()
264	                pending[-1] = None
265	            # If all items now completed and the host's parent frame is on top, advance host.
266	            if entry["items"] and set(entry["items"]) <= entry["completed"]:
267	                host_segments = host_path.split(STEP_PATH_SEP) if host_path else []
268	                expected_parent_prefix = tuple(host_segments[:-1])
269	                if tuple(frames[-1].path_prefix) == expected_parent_prefix:
270	                    if frames[-1].child_index < len(frames[-1].plan.steps):
271	                        candidate = frames[-1].plan.steps[frames[-1].child_index]
272	                        if host_segments and candidate.id == host_segments[-1]:
273	                            frames[-1].child_index += 1
274	                            pending[-1] = None
275	        elif kind in ("step_completed", "step_attested"):
276	            top = frames[-1]
277	            if top.child_index >= len(top.plan.steps):
278	                continue
279	            step = top.plan.steps[top.child_index]
280	            produces = getattr(step, "produces", ())
281	            if not produces:
282	                top.child_index += 1
283	                pending[-1] = None
284	            else:
285	                pending[-1] = {entry.name for entry in produces}
286	        elif kind == "produces_check_passed":
287	            current = pending[-1]
288	            if current is not None:
289	                name = event.get("produces_name")
290	                current.discard(name)
291	                if not current:
292	                    frames[-1].child_index += 1
293	                    pending[-1] = None
294	        elif kind in ("produces_check_failed", "cursor_rewind"):
295	            pending[-1] = None
296	        elif kind == "step_dispatched":
297	            pending[-1] = None
298	        # run_started / iteration_exhausted: no-op for cursor
299	    _finalize_cursor(frames, pending, for_each_progress)
300	    return CursorPath(frames=frames, for_each_progress=for_each_progress, pinned_failure=pinned_failure)
301	
302	
303	def _make_iteration_frame(host_step: Any, parent_prefix: tuple[str, ...], iteration: int) -> _Frame:
304	    body = dataclasses.replace(host_step, repeat=None) if hasattr(host_step, "repeat") else host_step
305	    body_plan = TaskPlan(plan_id=f"__iter_{host_step.id}_{iteration}", version=1, steps=(body,))
306	    return _Frame(
307	        plan=body_plan,
308	        path_prefix=parent_prefix,
309	        child_index=0,
310	        iteration=iteration,
311	        repeat_step_id=host_step.id,
312	    )
313	
314	
315	def _make_item_frame(host_step: Any, parent_prefix: tuple[str, ...], item_id: str) -> _Frame:
316	    body = dataclasses.replace(host_step, repeat=None) if hasattr(host_step, "repeat") else host_step
317	    body_plan = TaskPlan(plan_id=f"__item_{host_step.id}_{item_id}", version=1, steps=(body,))
318	    return _Frame(
319	        plan=body_plan,
320	        path_prefix=parent_prefix,
321	        child_index=0,
322	        item_id=item_id,
323	        repeat_step_id=host_step.id,
324	    )
325	
326	
327	def _finalize_cursor(
328	    frames: list[_Frame],
329	    pending: list[set[str] | None],
330	    for_each_progress: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
331	) -> None:
332	    """Pop exhausted iteration/item frames after event replay.
333	
334	    For verifier_passes / approve cases, the iteration frame ends with
335	    ``produces_check_passed`` coverage or ``step_attested`` (no following
336	    iteration_failed) — the iter frame's child_index is at end-of-plan.
337	    Pop it and advance the host. Same for item frames whose item is in
338	    the for_each_progress.completed set.
339	    """
340	    while True:
341	        top = frames[-1]
342	        if top.repeat_step_id is None:
343	            break
344	        if top.child_index < len(top.plan.steps):
345	            break
346	        if top.item_id is not None:
347	            host_path_segments = top.path_prefix + (top.repeat_step_id,)
348	            host_path = STEP_PATH_SEP.join(host_path_segments)
349	            entry = for_each_progress.get(host_path)
350	            frames.pop()
351	            pending.pop()
352	            pending[-1] = None
353	            if entry is not None and entry["items"] and set(entry["items"]) <= entry["completed"]:
354	                frames[-1].child_index += 1
355	                pending[-1] = None
356	        else:
357	            frames.pop()
358	            pending.pop()
359	            frames[-1].child_index += 1
360	            pending[-1] = None
361	
362	
363	def _path_str_from_event(event: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
364	    path = event.get("plan_step_path")
365	    if isinstance(path, list):
366	        return STEP_PATH_SEP.join(str(p) for p in path)
367	    pid = event.get("plan_step_id")
368	    return pid if isinstance(pid, str) else ""
369	
370	
371	def _auto_traverse_to_leaf(
372	    *,
373	    slug: str,
374	    cursor: CursorPath,
375	    events_view: list[dict[str, Any]],
376	    incoming_command: str,
377	    project_root: Path,
378	    run_id: str,
379	    append_fn: Callable[[dict[str, Any]], Any],
380	    raise_on_exhausted: bool,
381	) -> tuple[Any, tuple[str, ...]] | None:
382	    """Walk the cursor through nested entries and repeat-host expansions until we
383	    land on a CodeStep / AttestedStep leaf. Mutates ``cursor.frames`` and pushes
384	    auto-traversal events through ``append_fn``. ``events_view`` must be the list
385	    that ``append_fn`` extends (or that mirrors the on-disk log) so the helpers
386	    that scan prior events (``_count_iteration_failed``, the ``for_each_expanded``
387	    lookup) see the latest state.
388	
389	    With ``raise_on_exhausted=True`` (gate dispatch) the helper raises
390	    ``TaskRunGateError`` when the plan is exhausted; with ``False`` (peek) it
391	    returns ``None`` so the caller can report exhaustion to the operator.
392	    """
393	    while True:
394	        if cursor.at_root_done:
395	            if raise_on_exhausted:
396	                _reject(slug, "plan is exhausted", abort=True)
397	            return None
398	        if cursor.top_exhausted:
399	            top = cursor.frames[-1]
400	            if top.repeat_step_id is not None:
401	                # Defensive: _finalize_cursor should have popped these already.
402	                cursor.frames.pop()
403	                cursor.frames[-1].child_index += 1
404	                continue
405	            exit_path_str = STEP_PATH_SEP.join(top.path_prefix)
406	            append_fn(make_nested_exited_event(exit_path_str, 0))
407	            cursor.frames.pop()
408	            cursor.frames[-1].child_index += 1
409	            continue
410	        top = cursor.frames[-1]
411	        current_step = top.plan.steps[top.child_index]
412	        current_path = top.path_prefix + (current_step.id,)
413	        path_str = STEP_PATH_SEP.join(current_path)
414	        repeat = getattr(current_step, "repeat", None)
415	        in_repeat_frame = top.repeat_step_id is not None
416	        if repeat is not None and not in_repeat_frame:
417	            if isinstance(repeat, RepeatUntil):
418	                _enter_repeat_until(
419	                    slug=slug,
420	                    cursor=cursor,
421	                    host=current_step,
422	                    repeat=repeat,
423	                    path_str=path_str,
424	                    parent_prefix=top.path_prefix,
425	                    events=events_view,
426	                    append_fn=append_fn,
427	                )
428	                continue
429	            if isinstance(repeat, RepeatForEach):
430	                _enter_repeat_for_each(
431	                    slug=slug,
432	                    cursor=cursor,
433	                    host=current_step,
434	                    repeat=repeat,
435	                    path_str=path_str,
436	                    parent_prefix=top.path_prefix,
437	                    events=events_view,
438	                    append_fn=append_fn,
439	                    project_root=project_root,
440	                    run_id=run_id,
441	                    incoming_command=incoming_command,
442	                )
443	                continue
444	        if is_group_step(current_step):
445	            child_hash = _compute_inline_plan_hash(current_step.plan)
446	            append_fn(make_nested_entered_event(path_str, child_hash))
447	            cursor.frames.append(
448	                _Frame(plan=current_step.plan, path_prefix=current_path, child_index=0)
449	            )
450	            continue
451	        return current_step, current_path
452	
453	
454	@dataclass(frozen=True)
455	class PeekResult:
456	    """Read-only view of the next dispatchable step under the current cursor.
457	
458	    Returned by ``peek_current_step`` for ``cmd_next`` / ``cmd_status`` /
459	    ``cmd_ack`` to inspect what the gate would dispatch on next without
460	    actually mutating ``events.jsonl``. ``exhausted=True`` covers both
461	    ``at_root_done`` (plan complete) and ``pinned_failure``
462	    (repeat.until on_exhaust=fail).
463	    """
464	
465	    step: Any
466	    path_tuple: tuple[str, ...]
467	    iteration: int | None
468	    item_id: str | None
469	    exhausted: bool
470	
471	
472	def peek_current_step(
473	    plan: TaskPlan,
474	    events: Sequence[dict[str, Any]],
475	    slug: str,
476	    *,
477	    project_root: Path,
478	    run_id: str,
479	) -> PeekResult:
480	    """Walk the cursor exactly the way the gate would, but with a list-capturing
481	    ``append_fn`` so ``events.jsonl`` is never mutated.
482	
483	    Shares ``_auto_traverse_to_leaf`` with ``gate_command`` so peek and dispatch
484	    cannot drift on iteration / for_each / nested transitions (FLAG-P5-003).
485	    The captured events are kept in ``events_view`` so prior-event scans inside
486	    the auto-traverse helpers (``_count_iteration_failed``, the
487	    ``for_each_expanded`` lookup) see them; after every append we let the helper
488	    proceed and re-evaluate the cursor — which is equivalent to recomputing
489	    ``derive_cursor(plan, events + captured, slug=slug)`` because the helper
490	    performs the same frame mutations that ``derive_cursor`` would on replay.
491	    """
492	    cursor = derive_cursor(plan, events, slug=slug)
493	    if cursor.pinned_failure is not None or cursor.at_root_done:
494	        return PeekResult(step=None, path_tuple=(), iteration=None, item_id=None, exhausted=True)
495	
496	    events_view = list(events)
497	    captured: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
498	
499	    def _peek_append(ev: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
500	        captured.append(ev)
501	        events_view.append(ev)
502	
503	    leaf = _auto_traverse_to_leaf(
504	        slug=slug,
505	        cursor=cursor,
506	        events_view=events_view,
507	        incoming_command="",
508	        project_root=project_root,
509	        run_id=run_id,
510	        append_fn=_peek_append,
511	        raise_on_exhausted=False,
512	    )
513	    if leaf is None:
514	        return PeekResult(step=None, path_tuple=(), iteration=None, item_id=None, exhausted=True)
515	    step, path_tuple = leaf
516	    top = cursor.frames[-1]
517	    iteration = top.iteration if top.repeat_step_id is not None else None
518	    item_id = top.item_id if top.repeat_step_id is not None else None
519	    return PeekResult(
520	        step=step,
521	        path_tuple=path_tuple,
522	        iteration=iteration,
523	        item_id=item_id,
524	        exhausted=False,
525	    )
526	
527	
528	def gate_command(
529	    slug: str,
530	    command: str,
531	    argv: Sequence[str],
532	    *,
533	    root: str | Path | None = None,
534	    reentry: bool = False,
535	) -> GateDecision:
536	    active_run = read_active_run(slug, root=root)
537	    if active_run is None:
538	        if not is_in_task_run(slug):
539	            return GateDecision(active=False)
540	        _reject(slug, "active_run.json is missing", abort=True)
541	
542	    project_root = project_dir(slug, root=root)
543	    plan_path = project_root / "plan.json"
544	    plan_hash = compute_plan_hash(plan_path)
545	    if plan_hash != active_run["plan_hash"]:
546	        _reject(slug, "plan.json hash does not match active_run.json pin", abort=True)
547	
548	    run_id = active_run["run_id"]
549	    events_path = project_root / "runs" / run_id / "events.jsonl"
550	    # Sprint 1 (T9 / DEC-007): the tail-only CAS in append_event_locked is
551	    # the integrity gate for the hot append path; mid-chain corruption
552	    # detection is delegated to offline audit (verify_chain remains
553	    # callable for that purpose). Removing the per-verb O(n) re-walk here
554	    # is an acceptable trade-off documented in the brief.
555	
556	    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
557	    events = read_events(events_path)
558	    cursor = derive_cursor(plan, events, slug=slug)
559	    if cursor.pinned_failure is not None:
560	        reason, _host_path = cursor.pinned_failure
561	        raise TaskRunGateError(reason=reason, recovery=f"astrid abort --project {slug}")
562	    run_started_actor = _find_run_started_actor(events)
563	
564	    # Auto-traverse: nested_entered/exited for nested plans; iteration_started/
565	    # for_each_expanded/item_started for repeat hosts. We loop until we land on a
566	    # dispatchable leaf (CodeStep or AttestedStep) inside the appropriate frame.
567	    events_view = list(events)
568	
569	    def _gate_append(ev: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
570	        append_event(events_path, ev)
571	        events_view.append(ev)
572	
573	    leaf = _auto_traverse_to_leaf(
574	        slug=slug,
575	        cursor=cursor,
576	        events_view=events_view,
577	        incoming_command=command,
578	        project_root=project_root,
579	        run_id=run_id,
580	        append_fn=_gate_append,
581	        raise_on_exhausted=True,
582	    )
583	    if leaf is None:
584	        # Defensive: raise_on_exhausted=True should always raise inside the helper.
585	        _reject(slug, "plan is exhausted", abort=True)
586	    current_step, current_path = leaf
587	    top = cursor.frames[-1]
588	    path_str = STEP_PATH_SEP.join(current_path)
589	
590	    iteration = top.iteration if top.repeat_step_id is not None else None
591	    item_id = top.item_id if top.repeat_step_id is not None else None
592	
593	    if is_code_kind(current_step):
594	        return _dispatch_code(
595	            slug=slug,
596	            command=command,
597	            step=current_step,
598	            path_str=path_str,
599	            path_tuple=current_path,
600	            events_path=events_path,
601	            run_id=run_id,
602	            reentry=reentry,
603	            project_root=project_root,
604	            iteration=iteration,
605	            item_id=item_id,
606	        )
607	    if is_attested_kind(current_step):
608	        return _dispatch_attested(
609	            slug=slug,
610	            command=command,
611	            step=current_step,
612	            path_str=path_str,
613	            path_tuple=current_path,
614	            events_path=events_path,
615	            run_id=run_id,
616	            run_started_actor=run_started_actor,
617	            project_root=project_root,
618	            iteration=iteration,
619	            item_id=item_id,
620	        )
621	    raise TaskRunGateError(
622	        reason=f"unexpected step kind: {type(current_step).__name__}",
623	        recovery=f"astrid next --project {slug}",
624	    )
625	
626	
627	def _count_iteration_failed(events: Sequence[dict[str, Any]], host_path: str) -> int:
628	    return sum(
629	        1
630	        for ev in events
631	        if isinstance(ev, dict)
632	        and ev.get("kind") == "iteration_failed"
633	        and _path_str_from_event(ev) == host_path
634	    )
635	
636	
637	EXHAUST_OVERRIDE_ID = "exhaust-override"
638	
639	
640	def _has_iteration_exhausted(events: Sequence[dict[str, Any]], host_path: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
641	    for ev in events:
642	        if (
643	            isinstance(ev, dict)
644	            and ev.get("kind") == "iteration_exhausted"
645	            and _path_str_from_event(ev) == host_path
646	        ):
647	            return ev
648	    return None
649	
650	
651	def _make_exhaust_override_step(slug: str, host_path: str) -> Step:
652	    override_path = f"{host_path}{STEP_PATH_SEP}{EXHAUST_OVERRIDE_ID}"
653	    return Step(
654	        id=EXHAUST_OVERRIDE_ID,
655	        adapter="manual",
656	        command=f"ack --project {slug} --step {override_path}",
657	        instructions="repeat.until max_iterations exhausted; human override required to advance",
658	        ack=AckRule(kind="actor"),
659	        requires_ack=True,
660	        assignee="any-human",
661	    )
662	
663	
664	def _enter_repeat_until(
665	    *,
666	    slug: str,
667	    cursor: CursorPath,
668	    host: Any,
669	    repeat: RepeatUntil,
670	    path_str: str,
671	    parent_prefix: tuple[str, ...],
672	    events: Sequence[dict[str, Any]],
673	    append_fn: Callable[[dict[str, Any]], Any],
674	) -> None:
675	    failed = _count_iteration_failed(events, path_str)
676	    iteration = failed + 1
677	    path_tuple = parent_prefix + (host.id,)
678	    if iteration > repeat.max_iterations:
679	        existing = _has_iteration_exhausted(events, path_str)
680	        if existing is None:
681	            append_fn(
682	                make_iteration_exhausted_event(
683	                    path_tuple,
684	                    on_exhaust=repeat.on_exhaust,
685	                    max_iterations=repeat.max_iterations,
686	                )
687	            )
688	        if repeat.on_exhaust == "fail":
689	            raise TaskRunGateError(
690	                reason="repeat.until max_iterations exhausted",
691	                recovery=f"astrid abort --project {slug}",
692	            )
693	        # escalate: park on a synthetic exhaust-override attested step.
694	        override_step = _make_exhaust_override_step(slug, path_str)
695	        override_plan = TaskPlan(
696	            plan_id=f"__exhaust_{host.id}",
697	            version=1,
698	            steps=(override_step,),
699	        )
700	        cursor.frames.append(
701	            _Frame(
702	                plan=override_plan,
703	                path_prefix=path_tuple,
704	                child_index=0,
705	                repeat_step_id=host.id,
706	            )
707	        )
708	        return
709	    append_fn(make_iteration_started_event(path_tuple, iteration))
710	    cursor.frames.append(_make_iteration_frame(host, parent_prefix, iteration))
711	
712	
713	def _resolve_for_each_items(
714	    *,
715	    slug: str,
716	    repeat: RepeatForEach,
717	    project_root: Path,
718	    run_id: str,
719	) -> tuple[str, ...]:
720	    if repeat.items_source == "static":
721	        items = repeat.items
722	    else:
723	        target_id, produces_name = parse_from_ref(repeat.from_ref or "")
724	        prior_step_dir = step_dir_for_path(slug, run_id, (target_id,), step_version=1, root=project_root.parent)
725	        # Find the prior step's declared produces path. We need the plan for that.
726	        # We re-load plan.json which is allowed at gate_command (not in derive_cursor).
727	        plan = load_plan(project_root / "plan.json")
728	        target_step = next((s for s in plan.steps if s.id == target_id), None)
729	        if target_step is None:
730	            raise TaskRunGateError(
731	                reason=f"for_each.from references unknown sibling step {target_id!r}",
732	                recovery=f"astrid abort --project {slug}",
733	            )
734	        produces_entry = next((p for p in target_step.produces if p.name == produces_name), None)
735	        if produces_entry is None:
736	            raise TaskRunGateError(
737	                reason=f"for_each.from references unknown produces {produces_name!r}",
738	                recovery=f"astrid abort --project {slug}",
739	            )
740	        try:
741	            payload = json.loads((prior_step_dir / produces_entry.path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
742	        except (FileNotFoundError, json.JSONDecodeError, OSError) as exc:
743	            raise TaskRunGateError(
744	                reason=f"for_each.from cannot read produces JSON: {exc}",
745	                recovery=f"astrid abort --project {slug}",
746	            ) from exc
747	        if not isinstance(payload, list):
748	            raise TaskRunGateError(reason="for_each items must be unique strings", recovery=f"astrid next --project {slug}")
749	        items = tuple(payload)
750	    if not all(isinstance(x, str) and x for x in items):
751	        raise TaskRunGateError(reason="for_each items must be unique strings", recovery=f"astrid next --project {slug}")
752	    if len(set(items)) != len(items):
753	        raise TaskRunGateError(reason="for_each items must be unique strings", recovery=f"astrid next --project {slug}")
754	    return items
755	
756	
757	def _enter_repeat_for_each(
758	    *,
759	    slug: str,
760	    cursor: CursorPath,
761	    host: Any,
762	    repeat: RepeatForEach,
763	    path_str: str,
764	    parent_prefix: tuple[str, ...],
765	    events: Sequence[dict[str, Any]],
766	    append_fn: Callable[[dict[str, Any]], Any],
767	    project_root: Path,
768	    run_id: str,
769	    incoming_command: str,
770	) -> str | None:
771	    # FLAG-P3-004: scan events for an existing for_each_expanded; if absent, append once.
772	    existing = next(
773	        (
774	            ev
775	            for ev in events
776	            if isinstance(ev, dict)
777	            and ev.get("kind") == "for_each_expanded"
778	            and _path_str_from_event(ev) == path_str
779	        ),
780	        None,
781	    )
782	    if existing is None:
783	        items = _resolve_for_each_items(slug=slug, repeat=repeat, project_root=project_root, run_id=run_id)
784	        path_tuple = parent_prefix + (host.id,)
785	        append_fn(make_for_each_expanded_event(path_tuple, items))
786	        cursor.for_each_progress[path_str] = {"items": items, "completed": set()}
787	    else:
788	        items = tuple(existing.get("item_ids") or ())
789	    progress = cursor.for_each_progress.setdefault(path_str, {"items": items, "completed": set()})
790	    completed = progress["completed"]
791	    # For attested host: the incoming command may target a specific item via --item.
792	    target_item: str | None = None
793	    if is_attested_kind(host):
794	        _, args = match_attested_command(incoming_command, host.command)
795	        if args.item is not None:
796	            target_item = args.item
797	    if target_item is None:
798	        # Pick first not-yet-completed item.
799	        target_item = next((it for it in items if it not in completed), None)
800	    if target_item is None:
801	        # All items done — finalize will pop. Just return.
802	        return None
803	    if target_item not in items:
804	        _reject(slug, f"for_each --item {target_item!r} not in expanded item set", abort=False)
805	    if target_item in completed:
806	        _reject(slug, f"for_each --item {target_item!r} already completed", abort=False)
807	    path_tuple = parent_prefix + (host.id,)
808	    append_fn(make_item_started_event(path_tuple, target_item))
809	    cursor.frames.append(_make_item_frame(host, parent_prefix, target_item))
810	    return target_item
811	
812	
813	def _resolve_adapter(step: Step):
814	    """Return the concrete adapter instance for ``step.adapter``."""
815	    from astrid.core.adapter.local import LocalAdapter
816	    from astrid.core.adapter.manual import ManualAdapter
817	    from astrid.core.adapter.remote_artifact import RemoteArtifactAdapter
818	
819	    if step.adapter == "local":
820	        return LocalAdapter()
821	    if step.adapter == "manual":
822	        return ManualAdapter()
823	    if step.adapter == "remote-artifact":
824	        return RemoteArtifactAdapter()
825	    raise TaskRunGateError(
826	        reason=f"unknown adapter {step.adapter!r}",
827	        recovery="use --adapter local or manual",
828	    )
829	
830	
831	def _make_run_ctx(
832	    slug: str,
833	    run_id: str,
834	    path_tuple: tuple[str, ...],
835	    step_version: int,
836	    project_root: Path,
837	    iteration: int | None = None,
838	    item_id: str | None = None,
839	):
840	    """Return a RunContext for adapter dispatch. Return type is adapter.RunContext (lazy import)."""
841	    from astrid.core.adapter import RunContext
842	
843	    return RunContext(
844	        slug=slug,
845	        run_id=run_id,
846	        project_root=project_root,
847	        plan_step_path=path_tuple,
848	        step_version=step_version,
849	        iteration=iteration,
850	        item_id=item_id,
851	    )

853	
854	def _dispatch_code(
855	    *,
856	    slug: str,
857	    command: str,
858	    step: Step,
859	    path_str: str,
860	    path_tuple: tuple[str, ...],
861	    events_path: Path,
862	    run_id: str,
863	    reentry: bool,
864	    project_root: Path,
865	    iteration: int | None = None,
866	    item_id: str | None = None,
867	) -> GateDecision:
868	    if command != step.command:
869	        _reject(slug, "incoming command does not match plan[cursor]", abort=False)
870	
871	    adapter = _resolve_adapter(step)
872	    step_version = step.version
873	    run_ctx = _make_run_ctx(
874	        slug, run_id, path_tuple, step_version, project_root,
875	        iteration=iteration, item_id=item_id,
876	    )
877	
878	    if reentry:
879	        # FLAG-P3-005: scan back to the latest event for THIS plan_step_id rather than events[-1];
880	        # produces_check_failed must permit redispatch (cursor hasn't advanced).
881	        events = read_events(events_path)
882	        latest = _latest_event_for_step(events, path_str)
883	        if (
884	            isinstance(latest, dict)
885	            and latest.get("kind") == "step_dispatched"
886	            and latest.get("command") == command
887	        ):
888	            apply_task_run_env(run_id, slug, path_str, item_id=item_id, iteration=iteration)
889	            return _code_decision(
890	                run_id=run_id,
891	                slug=slug,
892	                path_str=path_str,
893	                path_tuple=path_tuple,
894	                events_path=events_path,
895	                produces=step.produces,
896	                project_root=project_root,
897	                reentry=True,
898	                iteration=iteration,
899	                item_id=item_id,
900	                adapter=step.adapter,
901	                step_version=step_version,
902	            )
903	        if isinstance(latest, dict) and latest.get("kind") == "produces_check_failed":
904	            dispatch_result = _adapter_dispatch(adapter, step, run_ctx, path_str, command, events_path)
905	            apply_task_run_env(run_id, slug, path_str, item_id=item_id, iteration=iteration)
906	            return _code_decision(
907	                run_id=run_id,
908	                slug=slug,
909	                path_str=path_str,
910	                path_tuple=path_tuple,
911	                events_path=events_path,
912	                produces=step.produces,
913	                project_root=project_root,
914	                reentry=False,
915	                iteration=iteration,
916	                item_id=item_id,
917	                adapter=step.adapter,
918	                step_version=step_version,
919	                pid=dispatch_result.pid if dispatch_result else None,
920	            )
921	        _reject(slug, "incoming command does not match plan[cursor]", abort=False)
922	
923	    dispatch_result = _adapter_dispatch(adapter, step, run_ctx, path_str, command, events_path)
924	    apply_task_run_env(run_id, slug, path_str, item_id=item_id, iteration=iteration)
925	    return _code_decision(
926	        run_id=run_id,
927	        slug=slug,
928	        path_str=path_str,
929	        path_tuple=path_tuple,
930	        events_path=events_path,
931	        produces=step.produces,
932	        project_root=project_root,
933	        reentry=False,
934	        iteration=iteration,
935	        item_id=item_id,
936	        adapter=step.adapter,
937	        step_version=step_version,
938	        pid=dispatch_result.pid if dispatch_result else None,
939	    )
940	
941	
942	def _adapter_dispatch(
943	    adapter,
944	    step: Step,
945	    run_ctx,
946	    path_str: str,
947	    command: str,
948	    events_path: Path,
949	):
950	    """Call adapter.dispatch() and emit step_dispatched. Returns DispatchResult or None on reject."""
951	    result = adapter.dispatch(step, run_ctx)
952	    if result.status == "rejected":
953	        _reject(run_ctx.slug, f"adapter {step.adapter!r} rejected dispatch: {result.reason}", abort=False)
954	    # Single emission point: cmd_next/gate emits step_dispatched.
955	    dispatched = make_step_dispatched_event(
956	        path_str, command,
957	        adapter=step.adapter,
958	        step_version=run_ctx.step_version,
959	        pid=result.pid,
960	    )
961	    append_event(events_path, dispatched)
962	    return result
963

965	def _code_decision(
966	    *,
967	    run_id: str,
968	    slug: str,
969	    path_str: str,
970	    path_tuple: tuple[str, ...],
971	    events_path: Path,
972	    produces: tuple[ProducesEntry, ...],
973	    project_root: Path,
974	    reentry: bool,
975	    iteration: int | None = None,
976	    item_id: str | None = None,
977	    adapter: str | None = None,
978	    step_version: int = 1,
979	    pid: int | None = None,
980	) -> GateDecision:
981	    return GateDecision(
982	        active=True,
983	        run_id=run_id,
984	        plan_step_id=path_str,
985	        events_path=events_path,
986	        reentry=reentry,
987	        step_kind="code",
988	        slug=slug,
989	        plan_step_path=path_tuple,
990	        produces=produces,
991	        project_root=project_root,
992	        iteration=iteration,
993	        item_id=item_id,
994	        adapter=adapter,
995	        step_version=step_version,
996	        pid=pid,
997	    )

1000	def _latest_event_for_step(events: Sequence[dict[str, Any]], path_str: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
1001	    path_list = path_str.split(STEP_PATH_SEP)
1002	    for ev in reversed(events):
1003	        if not isinstance(ev, dict):
1004	            continue
1005	        if ev.get("plan_step_id") == path_str:
1006	            return ev
1007	        if ev.get("plan_step_path") == path_list:
1008	            return ev
1009	    return None
1010	
1011	
1012	def _dispatch_attested(
1013	    *,
1014	    slug: str,
1015	    command: str,
1016	    step: Step,
1017	    path_str: str,
1018	    path_tuple: tuple[str, ...],
1019	    events_path: Path,
1020	    run_id: str,
1021	    run_started_actor: str | None,
1022	    project_root: Path,
1023	    iteration: int | None = None,
1024	    item_id: str | None = None,
1025	) -> GateDecision:
1026	    matched, args = match_attested_command(command, step.command)
1027	    if not matched:
1028	        _reject(slug, "incoming command does not match plan[cursor]", abort=False)
1029	
1030	    attestor_kind, attestor_id = validate_attested_identity(
1031	        slug=slug,
1032	        step=step,
1033	        args=args,
1034	        run_started_actor=run_started_actor,
1035	    )
1036	
1037	    if item_id is not None:
1038	        event = make_item_attested_event(
1039	            path_tuple,
1040	            item_id,
1041	            attestor_kind=attestor_kind,
1042	            attestor_id=attestor_id,
1043	            evidence=args.evidence,
1044	        )
1045	    else:
1046	        event = make_step_attested_event(path_str, attestor_kind, attestor_id, args.evidence)
1047	    if is_author_test_mode():
1048	        event["source"] = "author_test"
1049	    append_event(events_path, event)
1050	    decision = GateDecision(
1051	        active=True,
1052	        run_id=run_id,
1053	        plan_step_id=path_str,
1054	        events_path=events_path,
1055	        reentry=False,
1056	        step_kind="attested",
1057	        slug=slug,
1058	        plan_step_path=path_tuple,
1059	        produces=step.produces,
1060	        project_root=project_root,
1061	        iteration=iteration,
1062	        item_id=item_id,
1063	        adapter=step.adapter,
1064	        step_version=step.version,
1065	    )
1066	    if step.produces:
1067	        _run_inline_checks(decision, step.produces)
1068	    if iteration is not None:
1069	        feedback = _extract_iterate_feedback(args.evidence)
1070	        if feedback is not None:
1071	            write_iteration_feedback(decision, feedback)
1072	            append_event(
1073	                events_path,
1074	                make_iteration_failed_event(
1075	                    path_tuple,
1076	                    iteration,
1077	                    reason="iterate_feedback",
1078	                ),
1079	            )
1080	    return decision
1081	
1082	
1083	def _extract_iterate_feedback(evidence: tuple[str, ...]) -> str | None:
1084	    for item in evidence:
1085	        if item.startswith(ITERATE_FEEDBACK_PREFIX):
1086	            return item[len(ITERATE_FEEDBACK_PREFIX):]
1087	    return None
1088	
1089	
1090	def write_iteration_feedback(decision: GateDecision, feedback: str) -> None:
1091	    if (
1092	        decision.slug is None
1093	        or decision.run_id is None
1094	        or decision.iteration is None
1095	        or decision.project_root is None
1096	        or not decision.plan_step_path
1097	    ):
1098	        return
1099	    iter_dir = step_dir_for_path(
1100	        decision.slug,
1101	        decision.run_id,
1102	        decision.plan_step_path,
1103	        step_version=1,
1104	        iteration=decision.iteration,
1105	        root=decision.project_root.parent,
1106	    )
1107	    iter_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
1108	    feedback_path = iter_dir / "feedback.json"
1109	    cumulative: list[str] = []
1110	    if decision.iteration > 1:
1111	        prev_dir = step_dir_for_path(
1112	            decision.slug,
1113	            decision.run_id,
1114	            decision.plan_step_path,
1115	            step_version=1,
1116	            iteration=decision.iteration - 1,
1117	            root=decision.project_root.parent,
1118	        )
1119	        prev_path = prev_dir / "feedback.json"
1120	        if prev_path.exists():
1121	            try:
1122	                prev = json.loads(prev_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
1123	                if isinstance(prev, list):
1124	                    cumulative = [str(x) for x in prev]
1125	            except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError):
1126	                cumulative = []
1127	    cumulative.append(feedback)
1128	    feedback_path.write_text(
1129	        json.dumps(cumulative, ensure_ascii=False),
1130	        encoding="utf-8",
1131	    )
1132	
1133	
1134	def match_attested_command(incoming: str, expected_prefix: str) -> tuple[bool, AttestedArgs]:
1135	    """Strip ``--agent``/``--actor``/``--evidence``/``--item`` (repeatable evidence)
1136	    tokens from ``incoming`` and compare the canonical remainder to ``expected_prefix``.
1137	    """
1138	    try:
1139	        tokens = shlex.split(incoming)
1140	    except ValueError:
1141	        return False, AttestedArgs(agent=None, actor=None, evidence=())
1142	    agent: str | None = None
1143	    actor: str | None = None
1144	    item: str | None = None
1145	    evidence: list[str] = []
1146	    remaining: list[str] = []
1147	    i = 0
1148	    while i < len(tokens):
1149	        token = tokens[i]
1150	        if token == "--agent" and i + 1 < len(tokens):
1151	            agent = tokens[i + 1]
1152	            i += 2
1153	            continue
1154	        if token == "--actor" and i + 1 < len(tokens):
1155	            actor = tokens[i + 1]
1156	            i += 2
1157	            continue
1158	        if token == "--item" and i + 1 < len(tokens):
1159	            item = tokens[i + 1]
1160	            i += 2
1161	            continue
1162	        if token == "--evidence" and i + 1 < len(tokens):
1163	            evidence.append(tokens[i + 1])
1164	            i += 2
1165	            continue
1166	        remaining.append(token)
1167	        i += 1
1168	    rejoined = " ".join(shlex.quote(token) for token in remaining)
1169	    matched = rejoined == expected_prefix
1170	    return matched, AttestedArgs(agent=agent, actor=actor, evidence=tuple(evidence), item=item)
1171	
1172	
1173	def validate_attested_identity(
1174	    *,
1175	    slug: str,
1176	    step: Step,
1177	    args: AttestedArgs,
1178	    run_started_actor: str | None,
1179	) -> tuple[str, str]:
1180	    if args.agent is None and args.actor is None:
1181	        _reject(slug, "attested step requires --agent or --actor", abort=False)
1182	    if args.agent is not None and args.actor is not None:
1183	        _reject(slug, "attested step rejects both --agent and --actor", abort=False)
1184	    if step.ack.kind == "agent":
1185	        if args.agent is None:
1186	            _reject(slug, "attested step ack.kind=agent requires --agent", abort=False)
1187	        return "agent", args.agent  # type: ignore[return-value]
1188	    # ack.kind == "actor"
1189	    if args.actor is None:
1190	        _reject(slug, "attested step ack.kind=actor requires --actor", abort=False)
1191	    if is_author_test_mode():
1192	        # Author-test mode: skip ARTAGENTS_ACTOR-match and self-ack checks so the
1193	        # harness can drive attestations under a synthetic actor. Canonical kind
1194	        # ("actor") is preserved — author-test provenance rides on event["source"].
1195	        return "actor", args.actor  # type: ignore[return-value]
1196	    if task_actor_env() != args.actor:
1197	        _reject(slug, "attested --actor does not match ARTAGENTS_ACTOR env", abort=False)
1198	    # FLAG-005: self-ack rejection only applies to actor attestations because agents
1199	    # do not start runs in V1; an agent_id on run_started would be required to
1200	    # symmetrically block agent self-acks, which is out of scope for Phase 2.
1201	    if (
1202	        run_started_actor is not None
1203	        and run_started_actor == args.actor
1204	        and task_actor_env() == args.actor
1205	    ):
1206	        _reject(slug, "self-ack rejected", abort=False)
1207	    return "actor", args.actor  # type: ignore[return-value]

1209	
1210	def record_dispatch_complete(decision: GateDecision, returncode: int) -> None:
1211	    if not decision.active or decision.events_path is None or decision.plan_step_id is None:
1212	        return
1213	    if decision.step_kind == "attested":
1214	        # attested steps are advanced by step_attested itself; do not double-emit
1215	        return
1216	
1217	    # Resolve adapter to get completion (cost, status) — the adapter owns the
1218	    # completion logic, not the gate.
1219	    run_ctx = _make_run_ctx(
1220	        decision.slug or "",
1221	        decision.run_id or "",
1222	        decision.plan_step_path,
1223	        decision.step_version,
1224	        decision.project_root or Path("."),
1225	        iteration=decision.iteration,
1226	        item_id=decision.item_id,
1227	    )
1228	    # Load step from plan to get the full Step (for adapter.complete which needs produces).
1229	    step = _load_step_for_decision(decision)
1230	    adapter = _resolve_adapter(step) if step else None
1231	
1232	    if adapter is not None:
1233	        complete_result = adapter.complete(step, run_ctx)
1234	        cost_dict: dict[str, Any] | None = None
1235	        if complete_result.cost is not None:
1236	            cost_dict = {
1237	                "amount": complete_result.cost.amount,
1238	                "currency": complete_result.cost.currency,
1239	                "source": complete_result.cost.source,
1240	            }
1241	        # requires_ack enforcement: cursor stays on step until non-stale ack arrives.
1242	        # Do NOT emit step_completed for requires_ack steps — the ack event will advance.
1243	        if step is not None and step.requires_ack:
1244	            return
1245	        if complete_result.status == "failed":
1246	            append_event(
1247	                decision.events_path,
1248	                make_step_failed_event(
1249	                    decision.plan_step_id,
1250	                    complete_result.returncode,
1251	                    reason=complete_result.reason,
1252	                    cost=cost_dict,
1253	                    adapter=decision.adapter,
1254	                ),
1255	            )
1256	        elif complete_result.status == "awaiting_fetch":
1257	            missing, mismatched = _read_awaiting_fetch_items(run_ctx)
1258	            append_event(
1259	                decision.events_path,
1260	                make_step_awaiting_fetch_event(
1261	                    decision.plan_step_id,
1262	                    missing=missing,
1263	                    mismatched=mismatched,
1264	                    reason=complete_result.reason,
1265	                    adapter=decision.adapter,
1266	                ),
1267	            )
1268	        else:
1269	            append_event(
1270	                decision.events_path,
1271	                make_step_completed_event(
1272	                    decision.plan_step_id,
1273	                    returncode if returncode != -1 else (complete_result.returncode or 0),
1274	                    cost=cost_dict,
1275	                    adapter=decision.adapter,
1276	                ),
1277	            )
1278	    else:
1279	        # Legacy path: no adapter info on decision — use raw returncode.
1280	        append_event(
1281	            decision.events_path,
1282	            make_step_completed_event(decision.plan_step_id, returncode),
1283	        )
1284	
1285	    if decision.produces:
1286	        _run_inline_checks(decision, decision.produces)
1287	
1288	
1289	def _read_awaiting_fetch_items(run_ctx: Any) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
1290	    """Read missing/mismatched artifact names from the step's remote_state.json sidecar.
1291	
1292	    The remote-artifact adapter persists fetch results into ``remote_state.json``
1293	    when fetch_artifacts returns ``awaiting_fetch``.  Returns (missing, mismatched)
1294	    lists — empty lists if the sidecar is missing or unreadable.
1295	    """
1296	    step_dir = _step_dir_from_run_ctx(run_ctx)
1297	    remote_state_path = step_dir / "remote_state.json"
1298	    if not remote_state_path.exists():
1299	        return [], []
1300	    try:
1301	        state = json.loads(remote_state_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
1302	    except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError):
1303	        return [], []
1304	    missing = state.get("missing", [])
1305	    mismatched = state.get("mismatched", [])
1306	    if not isinstance(missing, list):
1307	        missing = []
1308	    if not isinstance(mismatched, list):
1309	        mismatched = []
1310	    return list(missing), list(mismatched)
1311	
1312	
1313	def _step_dir_from_run_ctx(run_ctx: Any) -> Path:
1314	    """Resolve runs/<run>/steps/<id>/v<N>/ for a RunContext."""
1315	    base = run_ctx.project_root / "runs" / run_ctx.run_id / "steps"
1316	    for segment in run_ctx.plan_step_path:
1317	        base = base / segment
1318	    base = base / f"v{run_ctx.step_version}"
1319	    if getattr(run_ctx, "iteration", None) is not None:
1320	        base = base / "iterations" / f"{run_ctx.iteration:03d}"
1321	    elif getattr(run_ctx, "item_id", None) is not None:
1322	        base = base / "items" / run_ctx.item_id
1323	    return base
1324	
1325	
1326	def _load_step_for_decision(decision: GateDecision) -> Step | None:
1327	    """Load the Step object for a decision by reading plan.json + events."""
1328	    if decision.project_root is None or not decision.plan_step_path:
1329	        return None
1330	    plan_path = decision.project_root / "plan.json"
1331	    if not plan_path.exists():
1332	        return None
1333	    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
1334	    events_path = decision.project_root / "runs" / (decision.run_id or "") / "events.jsonl"
1335	    events = read_events(events_path) if events_path.exists() else []
1336	    from astrid.core.task.plan_verbs import apply_mutations
1337	    effective = apply_mutations(plan, events)
1338	    for path_tuple, s in iter_steps_with_path(effective):
1339	        if path_tuple == decision.plan_step_path:
1340	            return s
1341	    return None
1342	
1343	
1344	# step_dir_for_path is the ONLY directory API used in this gate path (FLAG-P3-001).
1345	def _run_inline_checks(decision: GateDecision, produces: tuple[ProducesEntry, ...]) -> bool:
1346	    if (
1347	        decision.events_path is None
1348	        or decision.run_id is None
1349	        or decision.slug is None
1350	        or decision.project_root is None
1351	        or not decision.plan_step_path
1352	    ):
1353	        return True
1354	    projects_root = decision.project_root.parent
1355	    step_dir = step_dir_for_path(
1356	        decision.slug,
1357	        decision.run_id,
1358	        decision.plan_step_path,
1359	        step_version=1,
1360	        iteration=decision.iteration,
1361	        item_id=decision.item_id,
1362	        root=projects_root,
1363	    )
1364	    for entry in produces:
1365	        artifact_path = step_dir / entry.path
1366	        result = entry.check.run(artifact_path)
1367	        if not result.ok:
1368	            append_event(
1369	                decision.events_path,
1370	                make_produces_check_failed_event(
1371	                    decision.plan_step_path,
1372	                    entry.name,
1373	                    check_id=entry.check.check_id,
1374	                    reason=result.reason,
1375	                ),
1376	            )
1377	            if decision.iteration is not None:
1378	                append_event(
1379	                    decision.events_path,
1380	                    make_iteration_failed_event(
1381	                        decision.plan_step_path,
1382	                        decision.iteration,
1383	                        reason=f"produces check failed: {entry.name}",
1384	                    ),
1385	                )
1386	            else:
1387	                append_event(
1388	                    decision.events_path,
1389	                    make_cursor_rewind_event(
1390	                        decision.plan_step_path,
1391	                        reason=f"produces check failed: {entry.name}",
1392	                    ),
1393	                )
1394	            return False
1395	        cas_sha256 = _intern_produces_artifact(decision, artifact_path)
1396	        append_event(
1397	            decision.events_path,
1398	            make_produces_check_passed_event(
1399	                decision.plan_step_path,
1400	                entry.name,
1401	                check_id=entry.check.check_id,
1402	                cas_sha256=cas_sha256,
1403	            ),
1404	        )
1405	    return True
1406

1408	def _intern_produces_artifact(decision: GateDecision, artifact_path: Path) -> str | None:
1409	    if decision.project_root is None:
1410	        return None
1411	    if artifact_path.is_symlink():
1412	        return None
1413	    cas_target = intern(decision.project_root, artifact_path)
1414	    link_into_produces(cas_target, artifact_path)
1415	    return cas_target.name

1453	def command_for_argv(argv: Sequence[str]) -> str:
1454	    tokens = [str(token) for token in argv]
1455	    if len(tokens) >= 3 and Path(tokens[0]).name.startswith("python") and tokens[1:3] == ["-m", "astrid"]:
1456	        tokens = tokens[3:]
1457	    elif tokens and Path(tokens[0]).name.endswith("astrid"):
1458	        tokens = tokens[1:]
1459	    return " ".join(shlex.quote(token) for token in tokens)

1462	def _compute_inline_plan_hash(plan: TaskPlan) -> str:
1463	    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical_event_json(plan.to_dict()).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
1464	    return f"sha256:{digest}"
1465	
1466	
1467	def _find_run_started_actor(events: Sequence[dict[str, Any]]) -> str | None:
1468	    for event in events:
1469	        if event.get("kind") == "run_started":
1470	            actor = event.get("actor")
1471	            return actor if isinstance(actor, str) else None
1472	    return None
1473

1475	def _reject(slug: str, reason: str, *, abort: bool) -> None:
1476	    verb = "abort" if abort else "next"
1477	    raise TaskRunGateError(reason=reason, recovery=f"astrid {verb} --project {slug}")

79	TIMELINE ID: {timeline_id}
80	
81	FIRST COMMAND (Sprint 1 / T15)
82	- astrid status                    # session breadcrumb; ALWAYS run first

85	RECOVERY COMMANDS

87	- Acknowledge attested:     astrid ack <step> --project {slug} --decision approve [--agent <id> | --actor <name>]
88	- View run state:           astrid status --project {slug}
89	- End the run:              astrid abort --project {slug}

93	STOP HOOK
94	- The `astrid hook stop` command is the Claude Code Stop-hook entry point.
95	  When wired into .claude/settings.json (see docs/HOOKS.md) it re-injects this
96	  preamble and the current step on every Stop boundary so the rules above
97	  stay live for the entire run. The hook is a silent no-op outside task mode.
98	
99	INBOX SURFACE
100	- External processes (humans, scripts, other tools) signal completion of an
101	  attested step by dropping a JSON file into runs/{run_id}/inbox/.
102	- File shape:
103	    {{
104	      "step_id": "<id of the current attested step>",
105	      "decision": "approve" | "retry" | "abort",
106	      "evidence": {{ "<key>": "<non-empty string>", ... }},
107	      "submitted_at": "<ISO 8601 timestamp>",
108	      "submitted_by": "<external system or operator name>",
109	      "item_id": "<optional for_each item id>"
110	    }}
111	- Consume-on-next: astrid next reads inbox/, validates each file against
112	  the current cursor, and appends a step_attested / item_attested /
113	  cursor_rewind / run_aborted event before computing the next step.
114	- Agent attestations only — actor-ack steps must use `astrid ack` (the
115	  inbox file would be quarantined to inbox/.rejected/ otherwise).
116	- WARNING: `astrid next` is state-mutating when inbox/ has files.

131	def _qualified_split(qualified_id: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
132	    if not isinstance(qualified_id, str) or "." not in qualified_id:
133	        raise ValueError(
134	            f"orchestrator id {qualified_id!r} must be '<pack>.<name>'"
135	        )
136	    pack, _, name = qualified_id.partition(".")
137	    if not pack or not name or "." in name:
138	        raise ValueError(
139	            f"orchestrator id {qualified_id!r} must be exactly '<pack>.<name>'"
140	        )
141	    return pack, name

144	def _generate_run_id() -> str:
145	    stamp = datetime.now(UTC).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")
146	    return f"run-{stamp}-{secrets.token_hex(4)}"

154	def cmd_start(
155	    argv: Sequence[str],
156	    *,
157	    packs_root: Optional[Path] = None,
158	    projects_root: Optional[Path] = None,
159	) -> int:
160	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="astrid start", add_help=True)
161	    parser.add_argument("orchestrator_id", help="qualified id <pack>.<name>")
162	    parser.add_argument("--project", required=True, help="project slug")
163	    parser.add_argument("--name", default=None, help="optional run id (slug-validated)")
164	    parser.add_argument("--timeline", default=None, help="timeline slug")
165	    try:
166	        args = parser.parse_args(list(argv))
167	    except SystemExit as exc:
168	        return int(exc.code or 2)
169	
170	    try:
171	        slug = validate_project_slug(args.project)
172	    except Exception as exc:
173	        _print_err(f"start: {exc}")
174	        return 1
175	
176	    # Resolve timeline ULID (timeline_id) and slug for display.
177	    timeline_id: str | None = None
178	    timeline_slug: str | None = None
179	    if args.timeline is not None:
180	        found = find_timeline_by_slug(slug, args.timeline, root=projects_root)
181	        if found is None:
182	            _print_err(
183	                f"start: timeline {args.timeline!r} not found in project {slug!r}"
184	            )
185	            return 1
186	        timeline_id = found[0]
187	        timeline_slug = args.timeline
188	    else:
189	        default_ulid = read_project_default(slug, root=projects_root)
190	        if default_ulid is not None:
191	            resolved_slug = find_timeline_slug_for_ulid(slug, default_ulid, root=projects_root)
192	            if resolved_slug is not None:
193	                timeline_id = default_ulid
194	                timeline_slug = resolved_slug
195	                _print_err(
196	                    f"Using default timeline: {timeline_slug}. "
197	                    f"Use --timeline to override."
198	                )
199	    # If still no timeline, list available timelines and error — but allow the
200	    # bootstrap case (project.json absent) to proceed unbound, mirroring
201	    # ``astrid attach``'s zero-timeline handling. This keeps legacy callers
202	    # that pre-date Sprint 2's container model working until the project is
203	    # explicitly initialized.
204	    if timeline_id is None:
205	        from astrid.core.timeline.crud import list_timelines
206	        from astrid.core.project.paths import project_json_path
207	        available = list_timelines(slug, root=projects_root)
208	        if available:
209	            _print_err("No default timeline; pass --timeline <slug>. Available:")
210	            for ts in available:
