```
212	            return 1
213	        if project_json_path(slug, root=projects_root).exists():
214	            _print_err(
215	                f"start: no timelines exist for project {slug!r}; "
216	                f"create one with `astrid timelines create <slug>`"
217	            )
218	            return 1
219	        # Bootstrap: project.json doesn't exist yet — proceed without a timeline
220	        # binding. The run record will carry timeline_id=None.
221	
222	    try:
223	        pack, name = _qualified_split(args.orchestrator_id)
224	    except ValueError as exc:

226	        return 1
227	
228	    if read_current_run(slug, root=projects_root) is not None:
229	        _print_err(
230	            f"start: active run already exists for project {slug!r}; "
231	            f"recovery: astrid abort --project {slug}"
232	        )
233	        return 1

241	        )
242	        return 1

244	    try:
245	        compiled_payload = json.loads(build_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
246	    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
247	        _print_err(f"start: failed to read {build_path}: {exc}")
248	        return 1

262	
263	    if args.name is not None:
264	        try:
265	            run_id = validate_run_id(args.name)
266	        except Exception as exc:
267	            _print_err(f"start: --name {exc}")
268	            return 1
269	    else:
270	        run_id = _generate_run_id()
271	
272	    run_dir = proj_root / "runs" / run_id
273	    run_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
274	
275	    # Lease-first ordering: any reader that observes current_run.json is
276	    # guaranteed to find a corresponding lease.json. The session id on the
277	    # lease is whatever ASTRID_SESSION_ID resolves to (CLI gate enforces
278	    # the session is bound before cmd_start dispatch); fall back to
279	    # 'legacy' for non-CLI callers that haven't migrated yet (tests etc).
280	    from astrid.core.session.binding import (
281	        SessionBindingError,
282	        resolve_current_session,
283	    )
284	
285	    session_id_for_lease = "legacy"
286	    try:
287	        bound = resolve_current_session()
288	        if bound is not None:
289	            session_id_for_lease = bound.id
290	    except SessionBindingError:
291	        session_id_for_lease = "legacy"
292	    write_lease_init(
293	        run_dir,
294	        session_id=session_id_for_lease,
295	        plan_hash=plan_hash,
296	        timeline_id=timeline_id,
297	    )
298	    write_current_run(slug, run_id, root=projects_root)
299	
300	    events_path = run_dir / "events.jsonl"
301	    actor = task_actor_env()
302	    append_event(events_path, make_run_started_event(run_id, plan_hash, actor=actor))
303	
304	    agent_md = _AGENT_MD_TEMPLATE.format(
305	        preamble=PROHIBITION_PREAMBLE,
306	        qualified_id=args.orchestrator_id,
307	        run_id=run_id,
308	        slug=slug,
309	        timeline_id=timeline_id,
310	    )
311	    (run_dir / "AGENT.md").write_text(agent_md, encoding="utf-8")
312	
313	    print(f"started {args.orchestrator_id}")
314	    print(f"  project:   {slug}")
315	    print(f"  timeline:  {timeline_slug}")
316	    print(f"  run-id:    {run_id}")
317	    print(f"  plan-hash: {plan_hash}")
318	    return 0
319	
320	
321	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
322	# cmd_abort
323	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
324	
325	
326	def cmd_abort(
327	    argv: Sequence[str],
328	    *,
329	    projects_root: Optional[Path] = None,
330	) -> int:
331	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="astrid abort", add_help=True)
332	    parser.add_argument("--project", required=True, help="project slug")
333	    parser.add_argument("--reason", default=None, help="optional human-readable reason")
334	    try:
335	        args = parser.parse_args(list(argv))
336	    except SystemExit as exc:
337	        return int(exc.code or 2)
338	
339	    try:
340	        slug = validate_project_slug(args.project)
341	    except Exception as exc:
342	        _print_err(f"abort: {exc}")
343	        return 1
344	
345	    run_id = read_current_run(slug, root=projects_root)
346	    if run_id is None:
347	        # Idempotent — Phase 6 Stop-hook may invoke abort defensively.
348	        return 0
349	
350	    run_dir = project_dir(slug, root=projects_root) / "runs" / run_id
351	    events_path = run_dir / "events.jsonl"
352	    append_event(events_path, make_run_aborted_event(run_id, reason=args.reason))
353	    # DEC-010: clear the pointer AND release the writer lease so the run
354	    # is fully detached. A follow-up takeover would now see the lease as
355	    # orphan-pending.
356	    clear_current_run(slug, root=projects_root)
357	    try:
358	        release_writer_lease(run_dir)
359	    except FileNotFoundError:
360	        pass
361	    print(f"aborted {run_id}")
362	    return 0
363	
364

369	
370	def cmd_status(
371	    argv: Sequence[str],
372	    *,
373	    projects_root: Optional[Path] = None,
374	) -> int:
375	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="astrid status", add_help=True)
376	    parser.add_argument("--project", required=True, help="project slug")
377	    try:
378	        args = parser.parse_args(list(argv))
379	    except SystemExit as exc:
380	        return int(exc.code or 2)
381	
382	    try:
383	        slug = validate_project_slug(args.project)
384	    except Exception as exc:
385	        _print_err(f"status: {exc}")
386	        return 1
387	
388	    active_run = read_active_run(slug, root=projects_root)
389	    if active_run is None:
390	        _print_err(
391	            f"status: no active run for project {slug!r}; "
392	            f"recovery: astrid start <orchestrator-id> --project {slug}"
393	        )
394	        return 1
395	
396	    run_id = active_run["run_id"]
397	    plan_hash = active_run["plan_hash"]
398	    proj_root = project_dir(slug, root=projects_root)
399	    plan_path = proj_root / "plan.json"
400	    events_path = proj_root / "runs" / run_id / "events.jsonl"
401	
402	    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
403	    events = read_events(events_path)
404	    peek = peek_current_step(
405	        plan, events, slug, project_root=proj_root, run_id=run_id
406	    )
407	
408	    print(f"run-id:    {run_id}")
409	    print(f"plan-hash: {plan_hash}")
410	    if peek.exhausted or peek.step is None:
411	        print("current:   <run exhausted>")
412	    else:
413	        path_str = STEP_PATH_SEP.join(peek.path_tuple)
414	        kind = "nested" if is_group_step(peek.step) else (
415	            "attested" if is_attested_kind(peek.step) else "code"
416	        )
417	        suffix = ""
418	        if peek.iteration is not None:
419	            suffix += f"  iter={peek.iteration}"
420	        if peek.item_id is not None:
421	            suffix += f"  item={peek.item_id}"
422	        print(f"current:   {path_str} [{kind}] v{peek.step.version}{suffix}")
423	        if peek.step.produces:
424	            names = ", ".join(p.name for p in peek.step.produces)
425	            print(f"produces:  {names}")
426	
427	    pending = pending_count(proj_root / "runs" / run_id)
428	    if pending > 0:
429	        print(f"inbox:     {pending} pending")
430	
431	    print("recent events:")
432	    for ev in events[-5:]:
433	        kind = ev.get("kind", "?")
434	        ts = ev.get("ts", "")
435	        plan_step_id = ev.get("plan_step_id")
436	        if not isinstance(plan_step_id, str):
437	            path = ev.get("plan_step_path")
438	            plan_step_id = "/".join(path) if isinstance(path, list) else ""
439	        print(f"  {ts}  {kind}  {plan_step_id}")
440	    return 0
441	
442	
443	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
444	# cmd_next
445	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
446	
447	
448	def _format_ack_template(
449	    *, path_str: str, slug: str, ack_kind: str, has_repeat_for_each: bool
450	) -> str:
451	    identity = "--agent <id>" if ack_kind == "agent" else "--actor <name>"
452	    base = (
453	        f"astrid ack {path_str} --project {slug} --decision approve "
454	        f"{identity} [--evidence path ...]"
455	    )
456	    if has_repeat_for_each:
457	        base += " [--item <id>]"
458	    return base
459	
460	
461	def _find_step_by_path(plan, path_tuple):
462	    """Walk a TaskPlan to find the step at ``path_tuple`` (descending NestedStep
463	    children). Returns the step or None if the path does not resolve.
464	    """
465	    if not path_tuple:
466	        return None
467	    steps = plan.steps
468	    for segment in path_tuple[:-1]:
469	        match = next((s for s in steps if s.id == segment), None)
470	        if match is None or not is_group_step(match):
471	            return None
472	        steps = match.children or ()
473	    return next((s for s in steps if s.id == path_tuple[-1]), None)
474	
475	
476	def _completed_items_from_events(events, host_path):
477	    """Return the set of item ids that have a completed/attested event under
478	    ``host_path``. ``host_path`` is the STEP_PATH_SEP-joined string form.
479	    """
480	    path_list = host_path.split(STEP_PATH_SEP) if host_path else []
481	    completed: set[str] = set()
482	    for ev in events:
483	        if not isinstance(ev, dict):
484	            continue
485	        kind = ev.get("kind")
486	        if kind not in ("item_completed", "item_attested"):
487	            continue
488	        if ev.get("plan_step_path") != path_list:
489	            continue
490	        item_id = ev.get("item_id")
491	        if isinstance(item_id, str):
492	            completed.add(item_id)
493	    return completed
494	
495	
496	def cmd_next(
497	    argv: Sequence[str],
498	    *,
499	    projects_root: Optional[Path] = None,
500	) -> int:
501	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="astrid next", add_help=True)
502	    parser.add_argument("--project", required=True, help="project slug")
503	    try:
504	        args = parser.parse_args(list(argv))
505	    except SystemExit as exc:
506	        return int(exc.code or 2)
507	
508	    try:
509	        slug = validate_project_slug(args.project)
510	    except Exception as exc:
511	        # Preamble must precede every operator-facing message (SD-023).
512	        print(PROHIBITION_PREAMBLE)
513	        print()
514	        _print_err(f"next: {exc}")
515	        return 1
516	
517	    # Always print preamble first, verbatim, every call (SD-023) — even on
518	    # error / exhausted paths so Stop-hook context re-injection is consistent.
519	    print(PROHIBITION_PREAMBLE)
520	    print()
521	
522	    active_run = read_active_run(slug, root=projects_root)
523	    if active_run is None:
524	        _print_err(
525	            f"next: no active run for project {slug!r}; "
526	            f"recovery: astrid start <orchestrator-id> --project {slug}"
527	        )
528	        return 1
529	
530	    run_id = active_run["run_id"]
531	    proj_root = project_dir(slug, root=projects_root)
532	    plan_path = proj_root / "plan.json"
533	    events_path = proj_root / "runs" / run_id / "events.jsonl"
534	    run_dir = proj_root / "runs" / run_id
535	
536	    # FLAG-P8-005: cmd_next becomes state-mutating when inbox/ contains valid
537	    # files. Each entry is consumed best-effort so a single bad file cannot
538	    # crash the verb.
539	    for entry in scan_inbox(run_dir):
540	        try:
541	            consume_inbox_entry(
542	                run_dir, entry, slug=slug, projects_root=projects_root
543	            )
544	        except (TaskRunGateError, OSError, EventLogError):
545	            continue
546	
547	    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
548	    events = read_events(events_path)
549	    peek = peek_current_step(
550	        plan, events, slug, project_root=proj_root, run_id=run_id
551	    )
552	
553	    if peek.exhausted or peek.step is None:
554	        if _run_is_complete(plan, events):
555	            append_event(events_path, make_run_completed_event(run_id))
556	        else:
557	            print(
558	                "run not complete: some steps still awaiting_fetch or in-flight",
559	                file=sys.stderr,
560	            )
561	        return 0
562	
563	    path_str = STEP_PATH_SEP.join(peek.path_tuple)
564	
565	    if is_code_kind(peek.step):
566	        print(f"run: {peek.step.command}")
567	        print(
568	            "(rerun the same command if it failed; the gate detects re-entry "
569	            "and skips a duplicate step_dispatched event.)"
570	        )
571	    elif is_attested_kind(peek.step):
572	        print(peek.step.instructions or peek.step.command or "")
573	        print()
574	        # peek.step.repeat is None when the leaf is the body of a repeat
575	        # frame (the body is a clone with repeat stripped) — peek.item_id
576	        # being set is the reliable signal that we're inside a for_each
577	        # host. Fall back to looking up the host in the plan when item_id
578	        # is None to handle a top-level for_each that hasn't dispatched yet.
579	        host_has_for_each = peek.item_id is not None
580	        if not host_has_for_each:
581	            host_step = _find_step_by_path(plan, peek.path_tuple)
582	            if host_step is not None and isinstance(
583	                getattr(host_step, "repeat", None), RepeatForEach
584	            ):
585	                host_has_for_each = True
586	        print(
587	            _format_ack_template(
588	                path_str=path_str,
589	                slug=slug,
590	                ack_kind=(peek.step.ack.kind if peek.step.ack is not None else "agent"),
591	                has_repeat_for_each=host_has_for_each,
592	            )
593	        )
594	    else:
595	        # Defensive: peek_current_step should never surface a NestedStep.
596	        _print_err(f"next: unexpected step kind {type(peek.step).__name__}")
597	        return 1
598	
599	    # Iteration ledger: at peek.iteration == N (>=2), read iteration N-1's
600	    # cumulative feedback.json (written by write_iteration_feedback).
601	    if peek.iteration is not None and peek.iteration >= 2:
602	        prev_iter = peek.iteration - 1
603	        try:
604	            prev_dir = step_dir_for_path(
605	                slug,
606	                run_id,
607	                peek.path_tuple,
608	                step_version=1,
609	                iteration=prev_iter,
610	                root=projects_root,
611	            )
612	        except Exception:
613	            prev_dir = None
614	        if prev_dir is not None:
615	            feedback_path = prev_dir / "feedback.json"
616	            if feedback_path.is_file():
617	                try:
618	                    payload = json.loads(feedback_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
619	                except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
620	                    payload = None
621	                if isinstance(payload, list):
622	                    print()
623	                    print(f"feedback ledger (through iteration {prev_iter}):")
624	                    for idx, entry in enumerate(payload, start=1):
625	                        print(f"  [{idx}] {entry}")
626	
627	    # for_each item ledger: when peek.item_id is set, the leaf is the body
628	    # step inside a for_each frame; peek.path_tuple matches the host path
629	    # because _make_item_frame uses path_prefix = parent_prefix and the body
630	    # carries the host's id. Look the host step up directly from the plan
631	    # (peek does not persist for_each_expanded to events.jsonl, so
632	    # derive_cursor's for_each_progress would be empty here).
633	    if peek.item_id is not None:
634	        host_path = STEP_PATH_SEP.join(peek.path_tuple)
635	        host_step = _find_step_by_path(plan, peek.path_tuple)
636	        items: list[str] = []
637	        if host_step is not None and isinstance(
638	            getattr(host_step, "repeat", None), RepeatForEach
639	        ):
640	            host_for_each: RepeatForEach = host_step.repeat  # type: ignore[assignment]
641	            if host_for_each.items_source == "static":
642	                items = list(host_for_each.items)
643	            # Dynamic items source — items are resolved at gate dispatch from
644	            # a sibling produces JSON file. peek shares the same resolution
645	            # path; if events.jsonl has a for_each_expanded event for this
646	            # host (because dispatch ran earlier) we can recover items from
647	            # there instead.
648	        if not items:
649	            for ev in events:
650	                if (
651	                    isinstance(ev, dict)
652	                    and ev.get("kind") == "for_each_expanded"
653	                    and ev.get("plan_step_path") == list(peek.path_tuple)
654	                ):
655	                    raw = ev.get("item_ids") or []
656	                    if isinstance(raw, list):
657	                        items = [str(x) for x in raw]
658	                    break
659	        completed = _completed_items_from_events(events, host_path)
660	        if items:
661	            print()
662	            print(f"for_each items (host {host_path}):")
663	            for item in items:
664	                marker = "x" if item in completed else " "
665	                star = "  <- next" if item == peek.item_id else ""
666	                print(f"  [{marker}] {item}{star}")
667	
668	    return 0
669	
670	
671	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
672	# cmd_runs_ls
673	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
674	
675	
676	def _summarize_run_dir(run_dir: Path) -> tuple[str, str, str]:
677	    """Return (status, last_event_kind, last_ts) for a run directory.
678	
679	    Status:
680	    - ``completed`` — terminal ``run_completed`` event present (S5a contract).
681	    - ``aborted`` — terminal ``run_aborted`` event present (S1 contract).
682	    - ``in-flight`` — neither terminal event yet; the run is still being driven.
683	    """
684	    events_path = run_dir / "events.jsonl"
685	    if not events_path.is_file():
686	        return "in-flight", "", ""
687	    events = read_events(events_path)
688	    if not events:
689	        return "in-flight", "", ""
690	    last = events[-1]
691	    last_kind = str(last.get("kind", ""))
692	    last_ts = str(last.get("ts", ""))
693	    if last_kind == "run_aborted":
694	        status = "aborted"
695	    elif last_kind == "run_completed":
696	        status = "completed"
697	    else:
698	        status = "in-flight"
699	    return status, last_kind, last_ts
700	
701	
702	_RUNS_LS_STATUSES = ("completed", "in-flight", "aborted")
703	
704	
705	def cmd_runs_ls(
706	    argv: Sequence[str],
707	    *,
708	    projects_root: Optional[Path] = None,
709	) -> int:
710	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="astrid runs ls", add_help=True)
711	    parser.add_argument("--project", default=None, help="optional project slug filter")
712	    parser.add_argument(
713	        "--status",
714	        default=None,
715	        choices=_RUNS_LS_STATUSES,
716	        help="filter by terminal status",
717	    )
718	    try:
719	        args = parser.parse_args(list(argv))
720	    except SystemExit as exc:
721	        return int(exc.code or 2)
722	
723	    if args.project is not None:
724	        try:
725	            slug = validate_project_slug(args.project)
726	        except Exception as exc:
727	            _print_err(f"runs ls: {exc}")
728	            return 1
729	        project_dirs = [project_dir(slug, root=projects_root)]
730	    else:
731	        root = resolve_projects_root(projects_root)
732	        if not root.is_dir():
733	            return 0
734	        project_dirs = sorted(p for p in root.iterdir() if p.is_dir())
735	
736	    rows: list[tuple[str, str, str, str, str]] = []
737	    for proj in project_dirs:
738	        runs_root = proj / "runs"
739	        if not runs_root.is_dir():
740	            continue
741	        for run_dir in sorted(p for p in runs_root.iterdir() if p.is_dir()):
742	            status, last_kind, last_ts = _summarize_run_dir(run_dir)
743	            if args.status is not None and status != args.status:
744	                continue
745	            rows.append((proj.name, run_dir.name, status, last_kind, last_ts))
746	
747	    for slug, run_id, status, last_kind, last_ts in rows:
748	        print(f"{slug}\t{run_id}\t{status}\t{last_kind}\t{last_ts}")
749	    return 0
750	
751	
752	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
753	# cmd_step_retry_fetch
754	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
755	
756	
757	def cmd_step_retry_fetch(
758	    argv: Sequence[str],
759	    *,
760	    projects_root: Optional[Path] = None,
761	) -> int:
762	    """Retry artifact fetch for a step in ``awaiting_fetch`` state."""
763	    from astrid.core.adapter.remote_artifact_fetch import fetch_artifacts
764	    from astrid.core.task.plan_verbs import apply_mutations
765	    from astrid.core.task.plan import iter_steps_with_path
766	
767	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="astrid step retry-fetch", add_help=True)
768	    parser.add_argument("step_id", help="step id (e.g. transcribe, render)")
769	    parser.add_argument("--run", default=None, dest="run_id", help="run id")
770	    parser.add_argument("--project", default=None, help="project slug")
771	    try:
772	        args = parser.parse_args(list(argv))
773	    except SystemExit as exc:
774	        return int(exc.code or 2)
775	
776	    if args.project is not None:
777	        try:
778	            slug = validate_project_slug(args.project)
779	        except Exception as exc:
780	            _print_err(f"step retry-fetch: {exc}")
781	            return 1
782	    else:
783	        _print_err("step retry-fetch: --project is required")
784	        return 1
785	
786	    if args.run_id is not None:
787	        try:
788	            run_id = validate_run_id(args.run_id)
789	        except Exception as exc:
790	            _print_err(f"step retry-fetch: --run {exc}")
791	            return 1
792	    else:
793	        current = read_current_run(slug, root=projects_root)
794	        if current is None:
795	            _print_err(
796	                f"step retry-fetch: no active run for project {slug!r} "
797	                f"and --run not specified"
798	            )
799	            return 1
800	        run_id = current
801	
802	    proj_root = project_dir(slug, root=projects_root)
803	    run_dir = proj_root / "runs" / run_id
804	    if not run_dir.is_dir():
805	        _print_err(
806	            f"step retry-fetch: run {run_id!r} not found in project {slug!r}"
807	        )
808	        return 1
809	
810	    events_path = run_dir / "events.jsonl"
811	    if not events_path.is_file():
812	        _print_err(
813	            f"step retry-fetch: no events.jsonl for run {run_id!r}"
814	        )
815	        return 1
816	
817	    events = read_events(events_path)
818	    if not events:
819	        _print_err(
820	            f"step retry-fetch: empty events log for run {run_id!r}"
821	        )
822	        return 1
823	
824	    step_id = args.step_id
825	    latest_event = _latest_event_for_step(events, step_id)
826	    if latest_event is None:
827	        _print_err(
828	            f"step retry-fetch: no events found for step {step_id!r} "
829	            f"in run {run_id!r}"
830	        )
831	        return 1
832	
833	    latest_kind = latest_event.get("kind")
834	
835	    if latest_kind == "step_completed":
836	        _print_err(
837	            f"step retry-fetch: step {step_id!r} is already completed"
838	        )
839	        return 0
840	
841	    if latest_kind == "step_failed":
842	        _print_err(
843	            f"step retry-fetch: step {step_id!r} is failed, not awaiting_fetch"
844	        )
845	        return 1
846	
847	    if latest_kind != "step_awaiting_fetch":
848	        _print_err(
849	            f"step retry-fetch: step {step_id!r} is in state "

932	def _latest_event_for_step(
933	    events: list[dict[str, Any]],
934	    step_id: str,
935	) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
936	    """Return the latest event whose leaf step id matches *step_id*."""
937	    latest: dict[str, Any] | None = None
938	    for ev in events:
939	        if not isinstance(ev, dict):
940	            continue
941	        path_list = ev.get("plan_step_path")
942	        if isinstance(path_list, list) and path_list and path_list[-1] == step_id:
943	            latest = ev
944	    return latest
```

```
1	"""``astrid ack`` lifecycle verb (Phase 5 T9).
2	
3	Split out of ``lifecycle.py`` to keep both modules under the ~600-line size
4	budget. Implements the ack decision matrix per the Phase 5 brief:
5	
6	- ``approve`` (attested cursor): synthesizes the gate-bound incoming command
7	  as ``step.command + identity/evidence/item tokens`` (NOT ``ack --step ...``)
8	  so ``match_attested_command`` can strip identity tokens and compare the
9	  literal remainder to ``step.command`` for authored commands like
10	  ``review.sh``. Calls ``gate_command`` + ``record_dispatch_complete``.
11	  (FLAG-P5-001.)
12	- ``approve`` on a CodeStep cursor: rejected. Code steps advance via the
13	  printed argv, not via ``ack``.
14	- ``retry``: only valid on an AttestedStep cursor whose latest event for
15	  the path is ``produces_check_failed``. Calls ``validate_attested_identity``
16	  BEFORE mutating events, then appends ``cursor_rewind`` so the next
17	  ``next`` re-dispatches. (FLAG-P5-002.)
18	- ``iterate``: only valid on an AttestedStep cursor whose host has
19	  ``repeat.until.condition == 'user_approves'``. Requires non-empty
20	  ``--feedback``. Calls ``validate_attested_identity`` BEFORE mutating
21	  events, then ``write_iteration_feedback`` (cumulative ledger) and
22	  appends ``iteration_failed`` so the next ``next`` enters iteration N+1.
23	  (FLAG-P5-002.)
24	- ``abort``: administrative — delegates to ``cmd_abort`` and skips identity
25	  validation entirely.
26	"""
27

29

68	def _find_step_by_path(plan, path_tuple):
69	    """Walk a TaskPlan to find the step at ``path_tuple``."""
70	    if not path_tuple:
71	        return None
72	    steps = plan.steps
73	    for segment in path_tuple[:-1]:
74	        match = next((s for s in steps if s.id == segment), None)
75	        if match is None or not is_group_step(match):
76	            return None
77	        steps = match.children or ()
78	    return next((s for s in steps if s.id == path_tuple[-1]), None)
79

81	def _latest_event_for_path(events, path_tuple):
82	    path_str = STEP_PATH_SEP.join(path_tuple)
83	    path_list = list(path_tuple)
84	    for ev in reversed(events):
85	        if not isinstance(ev, dict):
86	            continue
87	        if ev.get("plan_step_id") == path_str:
88	            return ev
89	        if ev.get("plan_step_path") == path_list:
90	            return ev
91	    return None

97	            actor = ev.get("actor")
98	            return actor if isinstance(actor, str) else None
99	    return None

201	        )

225	        if isinstance(exc, (StaleEpochError, StaleTailError)):

235	def _ack_approve(args, slug, peek, projects_root, proj_root) -> int:
236	    if is_code_kind(peek.step):
237	        _print_err(
238	            "ack: approve is invalid for code steps. code steps advance via "
239	            f"subprocess; just run the printed command (astrid next --project {slug})."
240	        )
241	        return 1
242	    if not is_attested_kind(peek.step):
243	        _print_err("ack: cannot approve non-attested step")
244	        return 1
245	
246	    # FLAG-P5-001: synthesize the incoming command as step.command +
247	    # identity/evidence/item tokens, NOT 'ack --step ...'. step.command may
248	    # already be a multi-token command (e.g., "echo review"); split it so
249	    # match_attested_command's canonical rejoin compares token-for-token
250	    # rather than treating the whole prefix as a single quoted argument.
251	    parts: list[str] = shlex.split(peek.step.command)
252	    if args.agent:
253	        parts += ["--agent", args.agent]
254	    if args.actor:
255	        parts += ["--actor", args.actor]
256	    for ev in args.evidence:
257	        parts += ["--evidence", ev]
258	    if args.item:
259	        parts += ["--item", args.item]
260	    incoming = " ".join(shlex.quote(p) for p in parts)
261	
262	    try:
263	        decision = gate_command(slug, incoming, [], root=projects_root)
264	    except TaskRunGateError as exc:
265	        _print_err(f"ack: {exc.reason}; recovery: {exc.recovery}")
266	        return 1
267	
268	    # Attested step is "complete" at attestation; the gate already wrote
269	    # step_attested + ran inline produces checks. record_dispatch_complete
270	    # is a no-op for attested steps but we call it for symmetry with code
271	    # dispatch and to keep the post-dispatch surface consistent.
272	    record_dispatch_complete(decision, 0)
273	    print(f"acknowledged {STEP_PATH_SEP.join(peek.path_tuple)}")
274	    return 0
275	
276	
277	def _ack_retry(args, slug, peek, plan, events, events_path, run_id, proj_root) -> int:
278	    if not is_attested_kind(peek.step):
279	        _print_err(
280	            "ack: retry is only valid on attested steps. Code steps "
281	            "redispatch implicitly when you re-run the printed argv."
282	        )
283	        return 1
284	
285	    # FLAG-P5-002: validate identity BEFORE mutating events.
286	    attested_args = AttestedArgs(
287	        agent=args.agent,
288	        actor=args.actor,
289	        evidence=tuple(args.evidence),
290	        item=args.item,
291	    )
292	    try:
293	        validate_attested_identity(
294	            slug=slug,
295	            step=peek.step,
296	            args=attested_args,
297	            run_started_actor=_run_started_actor(events),
298	        )
299	    except TaskRunGateError as exc:
300	        _print_err(f"ack retry: {exc.reason}; recovery: {exc.recovery}")
301	        return 1
302	
303	    latest = _latest_event_for_path(events, peek.path_tuple)
304	    if not isinstance(latest, dict) or latest.get("kind") != "produces_check_failed":
305	        _print_err(
306	            "ack retry: only valid after a verifier failure (the latest event "
307	            f"for {STEP_PATH_SEP.join(peek.path_tuple)} must be "
308	            "produces_check_failed)."
309	        )
310	        return 1
311	
312	    append_event(
313	        events_path,
314	        make_cursor_rewind_event(peek.path_tuple, reason="ack retry"),
315	    )
316	    print(f"retry queued for {STEP_PATH_SEP.join(peek.path_tuple)}")
317	    return 0
318	
319	
320	def _ack_iterate(args, slug, peek, plan, events, events_path, run_id, proj_root) -> int:
321	    if not is_attested_kind(peek.step):
322	        _print_err("ack: iterate is only valid on attested steps")
323	        return 1
324	    if not args.feedback or not args.feedback.strip():
325	        _print_err("ack iterate: --feedback is required and must be non-empty")
326	        return 1
327	
328	    # FLAG-P5-002: validate identity BEFORE mutating events.
329	    attested_args = AttestedArgs(
330	        agent=args.agent,
331	        actor=args.actor,
332	        evidence=tuple(args.evidence),
333	        item=args.item,
334	    )
335	    try:
336	        validate_attested_identity(
337	            slug=slug,
338	            step=peek.step,
339	            args=attested_args,
340	            run_started_actor=_run_started_actor(events),
341	        )
342	    except TaskRunGateError as exc:
343	        _print_err(f"ack iterate: {exc.reason}; recovery: {exc.recovery}")
344	        return 1
345	
346	    # Find the host step in the plan (peek.step has repeat stripped because
347	    # it is the body of an iteration frame). peek.path_tuple == host path
348	    # because _make_iteration_frame uses path_prefix = parent_prefix.
349	    host = _find_step_by_path(plan, peek.path_tuple)
350	    if (
351	        host is None
352	        or not isinstance(getattr(host, "repeat", None), RepeatUntil)
353	        or host.repeat.condition != "user_approves"  # type: ignore[union-attr]
354	    ):
355	        condition = (
356	            host.repeat.condition  # type: ignore[union-attr]
357	            if host is not None and isinstance(getattr(host, "repeat", None), RepeatUntil)
358	            else "<no repeat>"
359	        )
360	        _print_err(
361	            "ack iterate: only valid for repeat.until.condition='user_approves' "
362	            f"(host condition={condition!r})"
363	        )
364	        return 1
365	    if peek.iteration is None:
366	        _print_err("ack iterate: cursor is not inside an iteration frame")
367	        return 1
368	
369	    decision = GateDecision(
370	        active=True,
371	        run_id=run_id,
372	        slug=slug,
373	        project_root=proj_root,
374	        plan_step_path=peek.path_tuple,
375	        iteration=peek.iteration,
376	        events_path=events_path,
377	    )
378	    write_iteration_feedback(decision, args.feedback)
379	    append_event(
380	        events_path,
381	        make_iteration_failed_event(
382	            peek.path_tuple, peek.iteration, reason="iterate_feedback"
383	        ),
384	    )
385	    print(

387	        f"{STEP_PATH_SEP.join(peek.path_tuple)}"
388	    )
389	    return 0
```

```
1	"""Single-entry six-invariant validator for plan mutations (Sprint 3 T7)."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	from typing import Any, Iterable, Literal
6	
7	from astrid.core.task.plan import (
8	    ADAPTERS,
9	    Step,
10	    TaskPlan,
11	    TaskPlanError,
12	    _assert_unique_paths,
13	    _validate_plan,
14	    iter_steps_with_path,
15	    parse_from_ref,
16	)
17	
18	
19	InvariantId = Literal[
20	    "I1_schema",
21	    "I2_sibling_uniqueness",
22	    "I3_produces_refs",
23	    "I4_adapter_shape",
24	    "I5_repeat_source",
25	    "I6_lease_epoch_cas",
26	]
27	
28	INVARIANTS: tuple[InvariantId, ...] = (
29	    "I1_schema",
30	    "I2_sibling_uniqueness",
31	    "I3_produces_refs",
32	    "I4_adapter_shape",
33	    "I5_repeat_source",
34	    "I6_lease_epoch_cas",
35	)
36	
37	
38	class MutationInvariantError(ValueError):
39	    """Raised when a plan mutation fails one of the six invariants."""
40	
41	    def __init__(self, invariant_id: InvariantId, element: str, reason: str) -> None:
42	        super().__init__(f"[{invariant_id}] {element}: {reason}")
43	        self.invariant_id = invariant_id
44	        self.element = element
45	        self.reason = reason
46	
47	
48	def validate_mutation(
49	    prior: TaskPlan | None,
50	    proposed: TaskPlan,
51	    *,
52	    lease_epoch_actual: int,
53	    lease_epoch_expected: int,
54	) -> None:
55	    """Run all six invariants against the proposed effective tree. Total rejection on first failure.
56	
57	    ``prior`` is unused by the invariant checks themselves but reserved for diff-aware
58	    sub-checks Sprint 5a may add. Pass None when there is no prior tree (initial plan).
59	    """
60	    _check_schema(proposed)
61	    _check_sibling_uniqueness(proposed)
62	    _check_produces_refs(proposed)
63	    _check_adapter_shape(proposed)
64	    _check_repeat_source(proposed)
65	    _check_lease_epoch(lease_epoch_actual, lease_epoch_expected)
66	
67	
68	# ---- I1: schema re-validate (round-trip through _validate_plan) ----
69	
70	def _check_schema(proposed: TaskPlan) -> None:
71	    try:
72	        _validate_plan(proposed.to_dict())
73	    except TaskPlanError as exc:
74	        raise MutationInvariantError("I1_schema", "plan", str(exc)) from exc
75	
76	
77	# ---- I2: sibling-id uniqueness at every frame ----
78	
79	def _check_sibling_uniqueness(proposed: TaskPlan) -> None:
80	    try:
81	        _assert_unique_paths(proposed)
82	    except TaskPlanError as exc:
83	        raise MutationInvariantError("I2_sibling_uniqueness", "plan", str(exc)) from exc
84	
85	
86	# ---- I3: produces reference integrity (including group-step re_export) ----
87	
88	def _collect_step_index(proposed: TaskPlan) -> dict[str, Step]:
89	    """Map step.id -> Step for every step in the tree."""
90	    return {step.id: step for _path, step in iter_steps_with_path(proposed)}
91	
92	
93	def _check_produces_refs(proposed: TaskPlan) -> None:
94	    index = _collect_step_index(proposed)
95	    for path, step in iter_steps_with_path(proposed):
96	        if step.re_export is None:
97	            continue
98	        for name, ref in step.re_export:
99	            try:
100	                child_id, produces_name = parse_from_ref(ref)
101	            except TaskPlanError as exc:
102	                raise MutationInvariantError(
103	                    "I3_produces_refs",
104	                    f"{'/'.join(path)}.re_export[{name!r}]",
105	                    f"unparseable ref {ref!r}: {exc}",
106	                ) from exc
107	            target = index.get(child_id)
108	            if target is None:
109	                raise MutationInvariantError(
110	                    "I3_produces_refs",
111	                    f"{'/'.join(path)}.re_export[{name!r}]",
112	                    f"references unknown step {child_id!r}",
113	                )
114	            if not any(p.name == produces_name for p in target.produces):
115	                raise MutationInvariantError(
116	                    "I3_produces_refs",
117	                    f"{'/'.join(path)}.re_export[{name!r}]",
118	                    f"step {child_id!r} declares no produces named {produces_name!r}",
119	                )
120	
121	
122	# ---- I4: adapter declared + command shape per-adapter ----
123	
124	def _check_adapter_shape(proposed: TaskPlan) -> None:
125	    for path, step in iter_steps_with_path(proposed):
126	        location = "/".join(path)
127	        if step.adapter not in ADAPTERS:
128	            raise MutationInvariantError(
129	                "I4_adapter_shape",
130	                location,
131	                f"adapter must be one of {ADAPTERS}, got {step.adapter!r}",
132	            )
133	        # Group steps don't carry commands; check applies to leaf steps only.
134	        if step.children is not None:
135	            continue
136	        if step.adapter == "manual":
137	            if step.command is None or not step.command.strip():
138	                raise MutationInvariantError(
139	                    "I4_adapter_shape",
140	                    location,
141	                    "manual adapter requires a non-empty command (dispatch payload)",
142	                )
143	        if step.adapter == "local":
144	            if step.command is None or not step.command.strip():
145	                raise MutationInvariantError(
146	                    "I4_adapter_shape",
147	                    location,
148	                    "local adapter requires a non-empty command",
149	                )
150	        # remote-artifact: command shape is permissive — runtime adapter rejects entirely.
151	
152	
153	# ---- I5: repeat.for_each.from_ref resolves to a real prior produces ----
154	
155	def _check_repeat_source(proposed: TaskPlan) -> None:
156	    # Walk siblings frame-by-frame so "prior" semantics match _validate_repeat_for_each.
157	    def _walk_frame(steps: tuple[Step, ...], prefix: tuple[str, ...]) -> None:
158	        for index, step in enumerate(steps):
159	            location = "/".join(prefix + (step.id,))
160	            if (
161	                step.repeat is not None
162	                and getattr(step.repeat, "kind", None) == "for_each"
163	                and getattr(step.repeat, "items_source", None) == "from"
164	                and step.repeat.from_ref is not None
165	            ):
166	                try:
167	                    target_id, produces_name = parse_from_ref(step.repeat.from_ref)
168	                except TaskPlanError as exc:
169	                    raise MutationInvariantError(
170	                        "I5_repeat_source",
171	                        f"{location}.repeat.for_each.from",
172	                        f"unparseable ref {step.repeat.from_ref!r}: {exc}",
173	                    ) from exc
174	                priors = steps[:index]
175	                target = next((s for s in priors if s.id == target_id), None)
176	                if target is None:
177	                    raise MutationInvariantError(
178	                        "I5_repeat_source",
179	                        f"{location}.repeat.for_each.from",
180	                        f"references unknown prior sibling {target_id!r}",
181	                    )
182	                if not any(p.name == produces_name for p in target.produces):
183	                    raise MutationInvariantError(
184	                        "I5_repeat_source",
185	                        f"{location}.repeat.for_each.from",
186	                        f"prior sibling {target_id!r} declares no produces named {produces_name!r}",
187	                    )
188	            if step.children is not None:
189	                _walk_frame(step.children, prefix + (step.id,))
190	
191	    _walk_frame(proposed.steps, ())
192	
193	
194	# ---- I6: lease-epoch CAS ----
195	
196	def _check_lease_epoch(actual: int, expected: int) -> None:
197	    if actual != expected:
198	        raise MutationInvariantError(
199	            "I6_lease_epoch_cas",
200	            "lease.writer_epoch",
201	            f"expected {expected}, got {actual} (another writer mutated under you)",
202	        )
203	
204	
205	__all__ = [
206	    "INVARIANTS",
207	    "InvariantId",
208	    "MutationInvariantError",
209	    "validate_mutation",
210	]
211
```

```
207	def _emit_artifact_rows(adir: Path, step_id: str, version: str, sub_label: str) -> None:
208	    """Print artifact rows for a single step version directory."""
209	    produces_dir = adir / "produces"
210	    if not produces_dir.is_dir():
211	        return
212	    remote_state_path = adir / "remote_state.json"
213	    declared: dict[str, str] = {}
214	    missing: set[str] = set()
215	    mismatched: set[str] = set()
216	    if remote_state_path.exists():
217	        try:
218	            state = json.loads(remote_state_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
219	            declared = state.get("manifest", {})
220	            if isinstance(declared, dict):
221	                declared = {k: v for k, v in declared.items() if isinstance(v, str)}
222	            else:
223	                declared = {}
224	            missing = set(state.get("missing", []))
225	            mismatched = set(state.get("mismatched", []))
226	        except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError):
227	            pass
228	
229	    # Walk produces directory for artifact files (skip subdirs like cost.json)
230	    for art_path in sorted(produces_dir.rglob("*")):
231	        if art_path.is_dir():
232	            continue
233	        if art_path.name == "cost.json":
234	            continue
235	        rel = art_path.relative_to(produces_dir)
236	        name = str(rel)
237	        short_path = str(rel)
238	        check_status = "ok"
239	        if name in missing:
240	            check_status = "missing"
241	        elif name in mismatched:
242	            check_status = "mismatched"
243	        sha256_val = ""
244	        try:
245	            import hashlib
246	            sha256_val = hashlib.sha256(art_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()[:16]
247	        except OSError:
248	            sha256_val = "unreadable"
249	
250	        cost_str = "-"
251	        cost_path = produces_dir / "cost.json"
252	        if cost_path.exists():
253	            try:
254	                cost_data = json.loads(cost_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
255	                amount = cost_data.get("amount")
256	                if isinstance(amount, (int, float)):
257	                    cost_str = f"${amount:.2f}"
258	            except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError):
259	                pass
260	
261	        iter_label = sub_label if adir.parent and adir.parent.name == "iterations" else ""
262	        item_label = sub_label if adir.parent and adir.parent.name == "items" else ""
263	        print(
264	            f"{step_id:30s} {version:>4s} {iter_label:>6s} {item_label:>10s} "
265	            f"{name:20s} {short_path:40s} {check_status:>12s} {sha256_val:>18s} {cost_str:>10s}"
266	        )

402	    :func:`astrid.core.task.validator.validate_mutation` to check that

433	    events_path = run_root / "events.jsonl"
434	    if not events_path.exists():
435	        print("verified: 0 events, plan_hash=(no events)")
436	        return 0
437	
438	    ok, line_idx, err_msg = verify_chain(events_path)
439	
440	    events = read_events(events_path)
441	    n_events = len(events)
442	
443	    # Extract plan_hash from run_started event
444	    plan_hash = "unknown"
445	    for e in events:
446	        if e.get("kind") == "run_started":
447	            plan_hash = str(e.get("plan_hash", "unknown"))
448	            break
449	
450	    if not ok:
451	        if line_idx == -1:
452	            print(f"broken: {err_msg}")
453	        else:
454	            print(f"broken at line {line_idx + 1}: {err_msg}")
455	        return 1
456	
457	    # ── --strict: replay plan mutations through the validator ──────────
458	    strict_failures = 0
459	    if args.strict:
460	        plan_path = proj_root / "plan.json"
461	        if not plan_path.exists():
462	            print("strict: plan.json not found; cannot validate mutations")
463	        else:
464	            try:
465	                from astrid.core.task.plan_verbs import (
466	                    PLAN_MUTATED_KIND,
467	                    _apply_diff as plan_apply_diff,
468	                )
469	                from astrid.core.task.validator import (
470	                    MutationInvariantError,
471	                    validate_mutation,
472	                )
473	
474	                plan = load_plan(plan_path)
475	                current = plan
476	                mutation_events = [
477	                    e for e in events if e.get("kind") == PLAN_MUTATED_KIND
478	                ]
479	                for i, ev in enumerate(mutation_events):
480	                    diff = ev.get("diff")
481	                    if not isinstance(diff, dict):
482	                        strict_failures += 1
483	                        print(
484	                            f"strict: mutation event {i + 1} missing diff field"
485	                        )
486	                        continue
487	                    try:
488	                        proposed = plan_apply_diff(current, diff)
489	                        # lease_epoch is not recoverable from event log;
490	                        # pass epoch=0,0 as a best-effort audit check
491	                        # (I6 is skipped in audit mode)
492	                        validate_mutation(
493	                            prior=current,
494	                            proposed=proposed,
495	                            lease_epoch_actual=0,
496	                            lease_epoch_expected=0,
497	                        )
498	                        current = proposed
499	                    except (MutationInvariantError, Exception) as exc:
500	                        strict_failures += 1
501	                        print(
502	                            f"strict: mutation event {i + 1} failed: {exc}"
503	                        )
504	            except Exception as exc:
505	                print(f"strict: error loading plan: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
506	
507	    print(f"verified: {n_events} events, plan_hash={plan_hash}")
508	    if args.strict:
509	        if strict_failures == 0:
510	            print("strict: all mutation events pass invariant checks")
511	        else:
512	            print(
513	                f"strict: {strict_failures} mutation event(s) failed validation"
514	            )
515	            return 1
516	    return 0
517	
518	
519	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
520	# events tail (Sprint 5b T6)
521	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
522	
523	
524	def cmd_events_tail(
525	    argv: Sequence[str],
526	    *,
527	    projects_root: Optional[Path] = None,
528	) -> int:
529	    """Print the last *N* events from a run's ``events.jsonl``.
530	
531	    ``-f`` polls the file every second for new lines (follow mode).
532	    ``-n`` controls how many lines to show (default: 20).
533	    """
534	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="astrid events tail", add_help=True)
535	    parser.add_argument("--run", required=True, dest="run_id", help="run identifier")
536	    parser.add_argument("--project", required=True, help="project slug")
537	    parser.add_argument(
538	        "-n",
539	        type=int,
540	        default=20,
541	        help="number of lines to print (default: 20)",
542	    )
543	    parser.add_argument(
544	        "-f",
545	        dest="follow",
546	        action="store_true",
547	        help="follow the file, polling every second for new lines",
548	    )
549	    try:
550	        args = parser.parse_args(list(argv))
551	    except SystemExit as exc:
552	        return int(exc.code or 2)
553	
554	    try:
555	        slug = validate_project_slug(args.project)
556	    except Exception as exc:
557	        print(f"events tail: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
558	        return 1
559	
560	    proj_root = project_dir(slug, root=projects_root)
561	    run_root = proj_root / "runs" / args.run_id
562	    if not run_root.is_dir():
563	        print(
564	            f"events tail: run {args.run_id!r} not found in project {slug!r}",
565	            file=sys.stderr,
566	        )
567	        return 1
568	
569	    events_path = run_root / "events.jsonl"
570	    if not events_path.exists():
571	        print("(no events)")
572	        return 0
573	
574	    _print_tail(events_path, n=args.n)
575	    if args.follow:
576	        last_mtime = events_path.stat().st_mtime
577	        try:
578	            while True:
579	                time.sleep(1)
580	                try:
581	                    cur_mtime = events_path.stat().st_mtime
582	                except FileNotFoundError:
583	                    break
584	                if cur_mtime > last_mtime:
585	                    last_mtime = cur_mtime
586	                    _print_tail(events_path, n=args.n)
587	        except KeyboardInterrupt:
588	            pass  # quiet exit on SIGINT
589	    return 0
590	
591	
592	def _print_tail(events_path: Path, *, n: int) -> None:
593	    """Print the last *n* events as one-line summaries."""
594	    events = read_events(events_path)
595	    tail = events[-n:] if len(events) > n else events
596	    for ev in tail:
597	        ts = str(ev.get("ts", ""))[:19]  # truncate fractional seconds
598	        kind = ev.get("kind", "?")
599	        plan_step_path = ev.get("plan_step_path")
600	        step_id = ""
601	        if isinstance(plan_step_path, list) and plan_step_path:
602	            step_id = str(plan_step_path[-1])
603	        elif isinstance(plan_step_path, str):
604	            step_id = plan_step_path
605	        rc = ev.get("returncode")
606	        rc_str = f" rc={rc}" if rc is not None else ""
607	        line = f"{ts}  {kind:24s}  {step_id}{rc_str}"
608	        print(line)
609	
610	
611	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
612	# helpers
613	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
614	
615	
616	def _run_status(events: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> str:
617	    """Derive run status from terminal events."""
618	    for e in events:
619	        if e.get("kind") == "run_aborted":
620	            return "aborted"
621	    for e in events:
622	        if e.get("kind") == "run_completed":
623	            return "completed"
624	    return "in-flight"
625	
626	
627	def _build_step_rows(
628	    events: list[dict[str, Any]],
629	    run_root: Path,
630	) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
631	    """Build per-step summary rows from events and on-disk state."""
632	    steps_root = run_root / "steps"
633	    latest_by_path: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
634	    cost_by_path: dict[str, float] = {}
635	
636	    for event in events:
637	        path_list = event.get("plan_step_path")
638	        if not isinstance(path_list, list) or not path_list:
639	            continue
640	        path_str = "/".join(str(p) for p in path_list)
641	        kind = event.get("kind")
642	        # Track latest event kind for state determination
643	        if kind in {
644	            "step_dispatched", "step_completed", "step_failed",
645	            "step_awaiting_fetch", "step_attested",
646	        }:
647	            latest_by_path[path_str] = {"kind": kind, "ts": event.get("ts", "")}
648	        # Accumulate costs from completed events
649	        if kind == "step_completed":
650	            cost = event.get("cost")
651	            if isinstance(cost, dict):
652	                amount = cost.get("amount")
653	                if isinstance(amount, (int, float)):
654	                    cost_by_path[path_str] = cost_by_path.get(path_str, 0) + float(amount)
655	
656	    rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
657	    if steps_root.is_dir():
658	        for step_dir in sorted(steps_root.iterdir()):
659	            if not step_dir.is_dir():
660	                continue
661	            step_id = step_dir.name
662	            for vdir in sorted(step_dir.iterdir()):
663	                if not vdir.is_dir() or not vdir.name.startswith("v"):
664	                    continue
665	                version = int(vdir.name[1:])
666	                # Find the event path that ends with this step_id
667	                path_str = step_id
668	                state = "pending"
669	                for ps, info in latest_by_path.items():
670	                    parts = ps.split("/")
671	                    if parts and parts[-1] == step_id:
672	                        state = info.get("kind", "pending")
673	                        break
674	                cost_val = cost_by_path.get(path_str)
675	                extras = ""
676	                if state == "step_awaiting_fetch":
677	                    remote_path = vdir / "remote_state.json"
678	                    if remote_path.exists():
679	                        try:
680	                            st = json.loads(remote_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
681	                            missing = st.get("missing", [])
682	                            mismatched = st.get("mismatched", [])
683	                            if missing or mismatched:
684	                                extras = f"({len(missing)} missing, {len(mismatched)} mismatched)"
685	                        except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError):
686	                            pass
687	                rows.append({
688	                    "step_id": step_id,
689	                    "version": version,
690	                    "state": state,
691	                    "cost": cost_val,
692	                    "extras": extras,
693	                })
694	    return rows
695	
696	
697	def _cost_by_source(events: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
698	    """Aggregate costs from events grouped by cost.source field."""
699	    by_source: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
700	    for event in events:
701	        kind = event.get("kind", "")
702	        if kind not in {"step_completed", "step_failed"}:
703	            continue
704	        cost = event.get("cost")
705	        if not isinstance(cost, dict):
706	            continue
707	        amount = cost.get("amount")
708	        currency = cost.get("currency", "USD")
709	        source = cost.get("source", "unknown")
710	        if not isinstance(amount, (int, float)):
711	            continue
712	        if source not in by_source:
713	            by_source[source] = {"amount": 0.0, "currency": currency, "source": source}
714	        by_source[source]["amount"] += float(amount)
715	        by_source[source]["currency"] = currency
716	    return by_source
717	
718	
719	__all__ = [
720	    "cmd_events_tail",
721	    "cmd_events_verify",
722	    "cmd_run_artifacts",
723	    "cmd_run_cost",
724	    "cmd_run_show",
725	    "cmd_run_trace",
726	]
```

```
1	"""Inbox surface for external completion signals (Sprint 3 T10).
2	
3	External processes drop JSON files into ``runs/<run-id>/inbox/`` to signal
4	that a step has completed. ``astrid next`` consumes these entries before
5	computing the next step.
6	
7	Sprint 3 changes:
8	- Entries match on ``(plan_step_path, step_version, item_id?)`` (not bare step_id).
9	- ``schema_version: 2`` discriminates new entries from legacy.
10	- Manual-adapter completion requires ``submitted_by`` AND ``submitted_by_kind``.
11	- Stale entries (tombstoned or fully-superseded) route to ``.rejected/``.
12	- STOP-LINE: every entry lands in exactly one of inbox/, .consumed/, or .rejected/.
13	"""
14	
15	from __future__ import annotations
16	
17	import hashlib
18	import json
19	import logging
20	import os
21	import shlex
22	from dataclasses import dataclass

40	    STEP_PATH_SEP,
41	    is_attested_kind,
42	    is_code_kind,
43	    load_plan,
44	)
45	from astrid.core.task.events import read_events
46	
47	INBOX_DIR_NAME = "inbox"
48	CONSUMED_DIR_NAME = ".consumed"
49	REJECTED_DIR_NAME = ".rejected"
50	
51	_VALID_DECISIONS = ("approve", "retry", "abort")
52	_VALID_SUBMITTED_BY_KINDS = ("agent", "actor")
53	
54	_LOGGER = logging.getLogger("astrid.core.task.inbox")
55	
56	
57	class InboxValidationError(Exception):
58	    """Raised internally when an inbox file fails schema validation."""
59	
60	
61	@dataclass(frozen=True)
62	class InboxEntry:
63	    path: Path
64	    step_id: str
65	    plan_step_path: tuple[str, ...] | None
66	    step_version: int
67	    schema_version: int
68	    decision: str
69	    evidence: tuple[str, ...]
70	    submitted_at: str
71	    submitted_by: str
72	    submitted_by_kind: str | None
73	    item_id: str | None
74	    raw: dict
75	
76	
77	def inbox_dir(run_dir: Path) -> Path:
78	    return run_dir / INBOX_DIR_NAME
79	
80	
81	def _parse_entry(file_path: Path, raw: dict) -> InboxEntry:
82	    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
83	        raise InboxValidationError("payload must be a JSON object")
84	
85	    schema_version = raw.get("schema_version")
86	    if schema_version is None:
87	        # Legacy entry (no schema_version field) — handled by T19 migration.
88	        return _parse_legacy_entry(file_path, raw)
89	
90	    if schema_version != 2 or isinstance(schema_version, bool):
91	        raise InboxValidationError(
92	            f"schema_version must be 2 (got {schema_version!r})"
93	        )
94	
95	    # --- schema_version:2 required fields ---
96	
97	    plan_step_path_raw = raw.get("plan_step_path")
98	    if not isinstance(plan_step_path_raw, list) or not plan_step_path_raw:
99	        raise InboxValidationError(
100	            "plan_step_path must be a non-empty list of strings"
101	        )
102	    plan_step_path: tuple[str, ...] = tuple(
103	        s for s in plan_step_path_raw if isinstance(s, str) and s
104	    )
105	    if len(plan_step_path) != len(plan_step_path_raw):
106	        raise InboxValidationError(
107	            "plan_step_path elements must be non-empty strings"
108	        )
109	    step_id = plan_step_path[-1]
110	
111	    step_version = raw.get("step_version")
112	    if not isinstance(step_version, int) or isinstance(step_version, bool) or step_version < 1:
113	        raise InboxValidationError("step_version must be an int >= 1")
114	
115	    decision = raw.get("decision")
116	    if decision not in _VALID_DECISIONS:
117	        raise InboxValidationError(
118	            f"decision must be one of {_VALID_DECISIONS}, got {decision!r}"
119	        )
120	
121	    submitted_at = raw.get("submitted_at")
122	    if not isinstance(submitted_at, str):
123	        raise InboxValidationError("submitted_at must be a string")
124	
125	    submitted_by = raw.get("submitted_by")
126	    if not isinstance(submitted_by, str) or not submitted_by:
127	        raise InboxValidationError("submitted_by must be a non-empty string")
128	
129	    # --- Identity enforcement: manual-adapter entries MUST carry submitted_by_kind ---
130	    submitted_by_kind = raw.get("submitted_by_kind")
131	    if submitted_by_kind is None:
132	        raise InboxValidationError(
133	            "submitted_by_kind is required (must be 'agent' or 'actor'); "
134	            "missing identity — entry will be rejected"
135	        )
136	    if submitted_by_kind not in _VALID_SUBMITTED_BY_KINDS:
137	        raise InboxValidationError(
138	            f"submitted_by_kind must be 'agent' or 'actor', got {submitted_by_kind!r}"
139	        )
140	
141	    # --- Optional fields ---
142	
143	    evidence_raw = raw.get("evidence")
144	    if evidence_raw is None:
145	        evidence: tuple[str, ...] = ()
146	    else:
147	        if not isinstance(evidence_raw, dict):
148	            raise InboxValidationError("evidence must be a JSON object")
149	        evidence_values: list[str] = []
150	        for key, value in evidence_raw.items():
151	            if not isinstance(value, str) or not value:
152	                raise InboxValidationError(
153	                    f"evidence value for {key!r} must be a non-empty string"
154	                )
155	            evidence_values.append(value)
156	        evidence = tuple(evidence_values)
157	
158	    item_id_raw = raw.get("item_id")
159	    if item_id_raw is None:
160	        item_id: str | None = None
161	    elif isinstance(item_id_raw, str) and item_id_raw:
162	        item_id = item_id_raw
163	    else:
164	        raise InboxValidationError("item_id must be a non-empty string when present")
165	
166	    return InboxEntry(
167	        path=file_path,
168	        step_id=step_id,
169	        plan_step_path=plan_step_path,
170	        step_version=step_version,
171	        schema_version=2,
172	        decision=decision,
173	        evidence=evidence,
174	        submitted_at=submitted_at,
175	        submitted_by=submitted_by,
176	        submitted_by_kind=submitted_by_kind,
177	        item_id=item_id,
178	        raw=raw,
179	    )

182	def _parse_legacy_entry(file_path: Path, raw: dict) -> InboxEntry:
183	    """Parse a legacy (no schema_version) inbox entry.
184	
185	    This path exists so pre-T19 inbox entries don't crash the parser.
186	    The T19 migration script rewrites these to schema_version:2; until
187	    then we treat the lack of schema_version as an implicit v1 entry.
188	    """
189	    step_id = raw.get("step_id")
190	    if not isinstance(step_id, str) or not step_id:
191	        raise InboxValidationError("missing or empty step_id")
192	
193	    decision = raw.get("decision")
194	    if decision not in _VALID_DECISIONS:
195	        raise InboxValidationError(
196	            f"decision must be one of {_VALID_DECISIONS}, got {decision!r}"
197	        )
198	
199	    submitted_at = raw.get("submitted_at")
200	    if not isinstance(submitted_at, str):
201	        raise InboxValidationError("submitted_at must be a string")
202	
203	    submitted_by = raw.get("submitted_by")
204	    if not isinstance(submitted_by, str):
205	        raise InboxValidationError("submitted_by must be a string")
206	
207	    evidence_raw = raw.get("evidence")
208	    if evidence_raw is None:
209	        evidence: tuple[str, ...] = ()
210	    else:
211	        if not isinstance(evidence_raw, dict):
212	            raise InboxValidationError("evidence must be a JSON object")
213	        evidence_values: list[str] = []
214	        for key, value in evidence_raw.items():
215	            if not isinstance(value, str) or not value:
216	                raise InboxValidationError(
217	                    f"evidence value for {key!r} must be a non-empty string"
218	                )
219	            evidence_values.append(value)
220	        evidence = tuple(evidence_values)
221	
222	    item_id_raw = raw.get("item_id")
223	    if item_id_raw is None:
224	        item_id: str | None = None
225	    elif isinstance(item_id_raw, str) and item_id_raw:
226	        item_id = item_id_raw
227	    else:
228	        raise InboxValidationError("item_id must be a non-empty string when present")
229	
230	    return InboxEntry(
231	        path=file_path,
232	        step_id=step_id,
233	        plan_step_path=None,  # legacy entries only have step_id
234	        step_version=1,  # pre-versioned world
235	        schema_version=0,  # sentinel: legacy / not yet migrated
236	        decision=decision,
237	        evidence=evidence,
238	        submitted_at=submitted_at,
239	        submitted_by=submitted_by,
240	        submitted_by_kind=None,
241	        item_id=item_id,
242	        raw=raw,
243	    )

245	
246	def scan_inbox(run_dir: Path) -> list[InboxEntry]:
247	    """Read and validate inbox entries.
248	
249	    Returns ``[]`` when the inbox directory is absent (opt-in behavior).
250	    Subdirectories and dot-prefixed names are skipped. Malformed files are
251	    logged via ``_LOGGER.warning`` and skipped — never raised.
252	    Entries are sorted by ``(submitted_at, filename)`` for deterministic ordering.
253	    """
254	    directory = inbox_dir(run_dir)
255	    if not directory.is_dir():
256	        return []
257	
258	    entries: list[InboxEntry] = []
259	    rejected_dir = directory / REJECTED_DIR_NAME
260	
261	    for child in directory.iterdir():
262	        if child.name.startswith("."):
263	            continue
264	        if not child.is_file():
265	            continue
266	        try:
267	            data = child.read_bytes()
268	            payload = json.loads(data)
269	            entry = _parse_entry(child, payload)
270	        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError, InboxValidationError) as exc:
271	            _LOGGER.warning("inbox: skipping %s: %s", child.name, exc)
272	            # Malformed entries that fail identity enforcement get routed to .rejected/
273	            _move_to(child, rejected_dir)
274	            continue
275	        entries.append(entry)
276	
277	    entries.sort(key=lambda e: (e.submitted_at, e.path.name))
278	    return entries
279	
280	
281	def pending_count(run_dir: Path) -> int:
282	    return len(scan_inbox(run_dir))
283	
284	
285	def _move_to(file_path: Path, dest_dir: Path) -> None:
286	    """Move ``file_path`` to ``dest_dir/<sha256>``.
287	
288	    The sha256-of-file-bytes filename (FLAG-P8-002) avoids any post-hoc
289	    events.jsonl re-read to derive the just-appended event hash. On OSError
290	    we unlink the original so a stuck file does not loop the inbox.
291	    """
292	    try:
293	        dest_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
294	        digest = hashlib.sha256(file_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
295	        target = dest_dir / digest
296	        os.replace(file_path, target)
297	    except OSError as exc:
298	        _LOGGER.warning(
299	            "inbox: failed to move %s to %s (%s); unlinking",
300	            file_path.name,
301	            dest_dir.name,
302	            exc,
303	        )
304	        try:
305	            file_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
306	        except OSError:
307	            pass
308	
309	
310	def _resolve_plan_step_path(entry: InboxEntry) -> tuple[str, ...]:
311	    """Return the plan_step_path from an entry, handling legacy fallback."""
312	    if entry.plan_step_path is not None:
313	        return entry.plan_step_path
314	    # Legacy entry: step_id only. Treat as root-level step.
315	    return (entry.step_id,)
316	
317	
318	def _is_step_tombstoned(plan, step_path: tuple[str, ...]) -> bool:
319	    """Check whether the step at ``step_path`` has been removed/tombstoned in the plan."""
320	    steps = plan.steps
321	    for segment in step_path[:-1]:
322	        match = next((s for s in steps if s.id == segment), None)
323	        if match is None or match.children is None:
324	            return True  # intermediate segment missing → tombstoned
325	        steps = match.children
326	    return not any(s.id == step_path[-1] for s in steps)
327	
328	
329	def _is_step_fully_superseded(
330	    events: list[dict], step_path: tuple[str, ...], entry_version: int
331	) -> bool:
332	    """Return True if the entry's version is older than the latest supersede target."""
333	    path_str = "/".join(step_path)
334	    latest_supersede_to: int | None = None
335	    for ev in events:
336	        if not isinstance(ev, dict) or ev.get("kind") != "plan_mutated":
337	            continue
338	        diff = ev.get("diff")
339	        if not isinstance(diff, dict):
340	            continue
341	        if diff.get("op") != "supersede":
342	            continue
343	        if diff.get("path") != path_str:
344	            continue
345	        to_version = diff.get("to_version")
346	        if isinstance(to_version, int) and not isinstance(to_version, bool):
347	            latest_supersede_to = to_version
348	    if latest_supersede_to is not None and entry_version < latest_supersede_to:
349	        return True
350	    return False
351	
352	
353	def _compute_stale(
354	    plan, events: list[dict], entry: InboxEntry, run_dir: Path
355	) -> tuple[bool, str | None]:
356	    """Determine whether ``entry`` is stale and why. Returns (is_stale, reason)."""
357	    step_path = _resolve_plan_step_path(entry)
358	
359	    if _is_step_tombstoned(plan, step_path):
360	        return True, f"step {'/'.join(step_path)!r} is tombstoned"
361	
362	    if _is_step_fully_superseded(events, step_path, entry.step_version):
363	        return True, (
364	            f"step {'/'.join(step_path)!r} v{entry.step_version} is superseded "
365	            f"(newer version exists)"
366	        )
367	
368	    return False, None
369	
370	
371	def consume_inbox_entry(
372	    run_dir: Path,
373	    entry: InboxEntry,
374	    *,
375	    slug: str,
376	    projects_root: Path | None,
377	) -> bool:
378	    """Validate ``entry`` against the current cursor and consume it.
379	
380	    Returns ``True`` when an event was appended (file moved to .consumed/),
381	    ``False`` for stale or rejected entries.  Stale-cursor entries are left
382	    in inbox/ so a future call can revisit them once the cursor advances;
383	    rejected entries (tombstoned, superseded, identity-missing, gate failure)
384	    move to .rejected/<sha256> so they do not loop.
385	
386	    STOP-LINE: every entry lands in exactly one of inbox/, .consumed/, or .rejected/.
387	    """
388	    project_root = project_dir(slug, root=projects_root)
389	    plan_path = project_root / "plan.json"
390	    run_id = run_dir.name
391	    events_path = run_dir / "events.jsonl"
392	    consumed_dir = run_dir / INBOX_DIR_NAME / CONSUMED_DIR_NAME
393	    rejected_dir = run_dir / INBOX_DIR_NAME / REJECTED_DIR_NAME
394	
395	    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
396	    events = read_events(events_path)
397	
398	    # --- Stale-entry check (before cursor check) ---
399	    is_stale, stale_reason = _compute_stale(plan, events, entry, run_dir)
400	    if is_stale:
401	        _LOGGER.warning(
402	            "inbox: rejecting %s as stale: %s", entry.path.name, stale_reason
403	        )
404	        _move_to(entry.path, rejected_dir)
405	        return False
406	
407	    peek = peek_current_step(
408	        plan, events, slug, project_root=project_root, run_id=run_id
409	    )
410	
411	    if entry.decision == "abort":
412	        append_event(
413	            events_path,
414	            make_run_aborted_event(run_id, reason=f"inbox abort by {entry.submitted_by}"),
415	        )
416	        clear_active_run(slug, root=projects_root)
417	        _move_to(entry.path, consumed_dir)
418	        return True
419	
420	    # approve / retry both require the cursor to be on a matching step.
421	    if peek.exhausted or peek.step is None or is_code_kind(peek.step):
422	        _LOGGER.warning(
423	            "inbox: skipping %s: cursor not on an attested step", entry.path.name
424	        )
425	        return False
426	    if not is_attested_kind(peek.step):
427	        _LOGGER.warning(
428	            "inbox: skipping %s: cursor not on an attested step", entry.path.name
429	        )
430	        return False
431	
432	    # Sprint 3 T10: match on (plan_step_path, step_version) not bare step_id.
433	    entry_path = _resolve_plan_step_path(entry)
434	    cursor_path = peek.path_tuple
435	
436	    if entry_path != cursor_path:
437	        _LOGGER.warning(
438	            "inbox: skipping %s: plan_step_path %s does not match current cursor %s",
439	            entry.path.name,
440	            "/".join(entry_path),
441	            "/".join(cursor_path),
442	        )
443	        return False
444	
445	    if entry.step_version != 1:
446	        # Versioned entry: verify the cursor version matches.
447	        pass  # Cursor version tracking lands fully in T14; for now tolerate any version.
448	
449	    if entry.decision == "approve":
450	        if peek.step.ack.kind == "actor":
451	            _LOGGER.warning(
452	                "inbox: skipping %s: ack.kind=actor not supported by inbox protocol "
453	                "(use astrid ack ...)",
454	                entry.path.name,
455	            )
456	            _move_to(entry.path, rejected_dir)
457	            return False
458	        # ack.kind == 'agent'
459	        parts: list[str] = [peek.step.command, "--agent", entry.submitted_by]
460	        for ev in entry.evidence:
461	            parts.extend(["--evidence", ev])
462	        if entry.item_id is not None:
463	            parts.extend(["--item", entry.item_id])
464	        synthesized = " ".join(shlex.quote(p) for p in parts)
465	        try:
466	            gate_command(slug, synthesized, [], root=projects_root)
467	        except TaskRunGateError as exc:
468	            _LOGGER.warning("inbox: rejecting %s: %s", entry.path.name, exc.reason)
469	            _move_to(entry.path, rejected_dir)
470	            return False
471	        _move_to(entry.path, consumed_dir)
472	        return True
473	
474	    # retry
475	    latest = _latest_event_for_path(events, peek.path_tuple)
476	    if not isinstance(latest, dict) or latest.get("kind") != "produces_check_failed":
477	        _LOGGER.warning(
478	            "inbox: skipping %s: retry requires latest event to be produces_check_failed",
479	            entry.path.name,
480	        )
481	        return False
482	    if peek.step.ack.kind != "agent":
483	        _LOGGER.warning(
484	            "inbox: skipping %s: retry only supported for ack.kind=agent",
485	            entry.path.name,
486	        )
487	        _move_to(entry.path, rejected_dir)
488	        return False
489	    args = AttestedArgs(
490	        agent=entry.submitted_by,
491	        actor=None,
492	        evidence=entry.evidence,
493	        item=entry.item_id,
494	    )
495	    try:
496	        validate_attested_identity(
497	            slug=slug, step=peek.step, args=args, run_started_actor=None
498	        )
499	    except TaskRunGateError as exc:
500	        _LOGGER.warning("inbox: rejecting %s: %s", entry.path.name, exc.reason)
501	        _move_to(entry.path, rejected_dir)
502	        return False
503	    append_event(
504	        events_path,
505	        make_cursor_rewind_event(peek.path_tuple, reason="inbox retry"),
506	    )
507	    _move_to(entry.path, consumed_dir)
508	    return True
509	
510	
511	def _latest_event_for_path(events, path_tuple: tuple[str, ...]):
512	    path_str = STEP_PATH_SEP.join(path_tuple)
513	    path_list = list(path_tuple)
514	    for ev in reversed(events):
515	        if not isinstance(ev, dict):
516	            continue
517	        if ev.get("plan_step_id") == path_str:
518	            return ev
519	        if ev.get("plan_step_path") == path_list:
520	            return ev
521	    return None
```

```
132	def _verb_is_unbound_allowlisted(raw: list[str]) -> bool:
133	    """Decide whether the invocation may run without a bound session.
134	
135	    The allowlist is the canonical Sprint 1 set (brief §CLI gate):
136	
137	    * ``attach``, ``init``, ``-h`` / ``--help`` (full-verb).
138	    * ``status`` — both the new session breadcrumb (no ``--project``) and
139	      the legacy ``astrid status --project <slug>``.
140	    * ``projects ls`` and ``projects create``.
141	    * ``sessions ls`` / ``sessions takeover`` / ``sessions detach``.
142	    * ``author test --project <slug>`` — documented exception for the
143	      workflow test runner.
144	    """
145	
146	    if not raw:
147	        return True  # empty argv → entrypoint help
148	
149	    top = raw[0]
150	    if top in {"-h", "--help"}:
151	        return True
152	    if top in {"attach", "init", "status"}:
153	        return True
154	    if top == "projects" and len(raw) >= 2 and raw[1] in _UNBOUND_PROJECTS_SUBVERBS:
155	        return True
156	    if top == "timelines" and len(raw) >= 2 and raw[1] == "ls":
157	        return True
158	    if top == "sessions" and len(raw) >= 2 and raw[1] in _UNBOUND_SESSIONS_SUBVERBS:
159	        return True
160	    if top == "runpod":
161	        # --help anywhere in the runpod subcommand tree is always allowed.
162	        if "--help" in raw or "-h" in raw or len(raw) == 1:
163	            return True
164	        # `runpod volumes ls/create` and `runpod ensure-storage` operate on RunPod
165	        # cloud state only — no Astrid run/lease/event mutation, so unbound is fine.
166	        if len(raw) >= 2 and raw[1] == "volumes":
167	            return True
168	        if len(raw) >= 2 and raw[1] == "ensure-storage":
169	            return True
170	        # `runpod sweep` writes pod_terminated_by_sweep events to owning runs'
171	        # events.jsonl — requires a bound session per the S4 brief.
172	        return False
173	    # `author test --project <slug>` exception. The orchestrate.cli wires the
174	    # `test` sub-verb regardless of whether a session is bound, so we open the
175	    # gate explicitly to match.
176	    if top == "author" and "test" in raw[1:] and "--project" in raw:
177	        return True
178	    return False
```

```
25	def _make_event(fields: dict, prev_hash: str) -> dict:
26	    """Build an event dict with a correct ``hash`` field."""
27	    ev = dict(fields)
28	    ev["hash"] = _event_hash(prev_hash, ev)
29	    return ev
30	
31	
32	def _build_chain(events: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
33	    """Given events without hashes, compute the chain and return with hashes."""
34	    chain: list[dict] = []
35	    prev = ZERO_HASH
36	    for raw in events:
37	        ev = dict(raw)
38	        ev["hash"] = _event_hash(prev, ev)
39	        chain.append(ev)
40	        prev = ev["hash"]
41	    return chain
42	
43	
44	def _write_events(path: Path, events: list[dict]) -> None:
45	    """Write events to a JSONL file."""
46	    with path.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
47	        for ev in events:
48	            fh.write(json.dumps(ev, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n")
49

51	# ── Known-good chain passes ─────────────────────────────────────────────
```
