54	def test_clean_chain_passes(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
55	    """A synthetically correct hash chain passes ``verify_chain``."""
56	    raw_events = [
57	        {"kind": "run_started", "run_id": "r1", "plan_hash": "sha256:abc", "ts": "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z"},
58	        {"kind": "step_dispatched", "plan_step_path": ["s1"], "ts": "2026-01-01T00:00:01Z"},
59	        {"kind": "step_completed", "plan_step_path": ["s1"], "returncode": 0, "ts": "2026-01-01T00:00:02Z"},
60	        {"kind": "run_completed", "ts": "2026-01-01T00:00:03Z"},
61	    ]
62	    chain = _build_chain(raw_events)
63	    events_path = tmp_path / "events.jsonl"
64	    _write_events(events_path, chain)
65	
66	    ok, line_idx, err_msg = verify_chain(events_path)
67	    assert ok is True
68	    assert err_msg is None
69	    assert line_idx == 3  # zero-indexed, 4 events → last is index 3
70

72	def test_empty_events_file_passes(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
73	    """An empty events.jsonl is trivially valid."""

81	# ── Corrupted chain caught ──────────────────────────────────────────────
82	
83	
84	def test_tampered_hash_caught(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
85	    """If a line's hash does not match the recomputed hash, verify_chain fails."""
86	    raw_events = [
87	        {"kind": "run_started", "run_id": "r1", "plan_hash": "sha256:abc", "ts": "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z"},
88	        {"kind": "step_completed", "plan_step_path": ["s1"], "returncode": 0, "ts": "2026-01-01T00:00:01Z"},
89	    ]
90	    chain = _build_chain(raw_events)
91	
92	    # Tamper: change the kind of line 2 but keep its old hash
93	    chain[1] = dict(chain[1], kind="step_failed")
94	
95	    events_path = tmp_path / "events.jsonl"
96	    _write_events(events_path, chain)
97	
98	    ok, line_idx, err_msg = verify_chain(events_path)
99	    assert ok is False
100	    assert line_idx == 1  # zero-indexed line 1 (second event)
101	    assert err_msg is not None
102	    assert "hash mismatch" in (err_msg or "").lower() or f"line {line_idx + 1}" in (err_msg or "")
103	
104	
105	def test_broken_prev_hash_caught(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
106	    """If prev_hash link is broken, verify_chain catches it at the affected line."""
107	    raw_events = [
108	        {"kind": "run_started", "run_id": "r1", "plan_hash": "sha256:abc", "ts": "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z"},
109	        {"kind": "step_dispatched", "plan_step_path": ["s1"], "ts": "2026-01-01T00:00:01Z"},
110	        {"kind": "step_completed", "plan_step_path": ["s1"], "returncode": 0, "ts": "2026-01-01T00:00:02Z"},
111	    ]
112	    chain = _build_chain(raw_events)
113	
114	    # Corrupt: swap event 2 (index 1) hash with something bogus
115	    chain[1] = dict(chain[1], hash="sha256:" + "ff" * 64)
116	
117	    events_path = tmp_path / "events.jsonl"
118	    _write_events(events_path, chain)
119	
120	    ok, line_idx, err_msg = verify_chain(events_path)
121	    assert ok is False
122	    # The mismatch should be at line 1 (the corrupted event)
123	    assert err_msg is not None and ("hash" in err_msg.lower() or "broken" in err_msg.lower())
124

150	def test_strict_mode_with_clean_plan(
151	    tmp_path: Path, tmp_projects_root: Path
152	) -> None:
153	    """``--strict`` replays plan_mutated events through the validator.
154	
155	    Constructs a minimal project with a valid v2 plan and a run with
156	    a plan_mutated event whose diff passes the six-invariant check.
157	    """
158	    from astrid.core.project.project import create_project
159	    from astrid.core.task.active_run import write_active_run
160	
161	    slug = "strict-test"
162	    run_id = "run-strict"
163	    create_project(slug, root=tmp_projects_root)
164	    proj_root = tmp_projects_root / slug
165	    runs_dir = proj_root / "runs"
166	    runs_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
167	    run_root = runs_dir / run_id
168	    run_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
169	
170	    # v2 plan with two steps
171	    plan_payload = {
172	        "plan_id": "p-strict",
173	        "version": 2,
174	        "steps": [
175	            {
176	                "id": "s1",
177	                "kind": "code",
178	                "adapter": "local",
179	                "command": "echo one",
180	                "cost": {"amount": 0, "currency": "USD", "source": "local"},
181	            },
182	            {
183	                "id": "s2",
184	                "kind": "code",
185	                "adapter": "local",
186	                "command": "echo two",
187	                "cost": {"amount": 0, "currency": "USD", "source": "local"},
188	            },
189	        ],
190	    }
191	    plan_path = proj_root / "plan.json"
192	    plan_path.write_text(json.dumps(plan_payload), encoding="utf-8")
193	
194	    from astrid.core.task.plan import compute_plan_hash
195	    plan_hash = compute_plan_hash(plan_path)
196	
197	    # Load the plan to get canonical step dicts for the diff
198	    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
199	
200	    # Build a synthetic plan_mutated event (add-step after s1)
201	    raw_events = [
202	        {
203	            "kind": "run_started",
204	            "run_id": run_id,
205	            "plan_hash": plan_hash,
206	            "ts": "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z",
207	        },
208	        {
209	            "kind": "plan_mutated",
210	            "run_id": run_id,
211	            "diff": {
212	                "op": "add",
213	                "after": "s1",
214	                "step": {
215	                    "id": "s3",
216	                    "kind": "code",
217	                    "adapter": "local",
218	                    "command": "echo three",
219	                    "cost": {"amount": 0, "currency": "USD", "source": "local"},
220	                },
221	            },
222	            "ts": "2026-01-01T00:00:01Z",
223	        },
224	        {"kind": "run_completed", "ts": "2026-01-01T00:00:02Z"},
225	    ]
226	    chain = _build_chain(raw_events)
227	    events_path = run_root / "events.jsonl"
228	    _write_events(events_path, chain)
229	
230	    write_active_run(slug, run_id=run_id, plan_hash=plan_hash, root=tmp_projects_root)
231	
232	    # Invoke cmd_events_verify with --strict
233	    from astrid.core.task.run_audit import cmd_events_verify
234	
235	    rc = cmd_events_verify(
236	        ["--run", run_id, "--project", slug, "--strict"],
237	        projects_root=tmp_projects_root,
238	    )
239	    assert rc == 0

Wait — this test expects `plan_step_id` on `step_completed` events but the code uses `plan_step_path`. Let me check the actual event payload.

Looking at `make_step_completed_event` — it produces `plan_step_path` as a list. But the test checks `event["plan_step_id"]`. The list-form path is converted from `path.split("/")` if "/" in path else `[path]`. So with `path_str = "step-1"`, it's `["step-1"]`. The test reads `event["plan_step_id"]` — that wouldn't work as-is. Let me check `read_events` and how `make_step_completed_event` is invoked.

> AGENT

Now for repeat.until — when the body emits `step_skipped`, the loop should exit cleanly. Let me think. In the cursor model:

- A `RepeatUntil` host pushes an iteration frame; the body is the host with `repeat=None` as a single-step plan.
- If the body is skipped via `step_skipped` on the host path, the cursor would advance the body's child_index past the end of the iteration body plan. Then `_finalize_cursor` should pop the iteration frame and advance the host. But it currently checks `pending[-1]==None` to pop iteration frames, and uses `iteration_failed` to drop frames mid-loop.

But wait — the host itself is the step the body wraps. Path tuples for `step_skipped` on a repeat-host: the path is `host.id`. The cursor's frames at the point of skip dispatch would have the iteration frame on top (child_index=0, plan=single-step body) and the body step has the same id as the host. So `step_skipped` on the body would land inside the iteration frame, advance child_index to 1 (past end). Then `_finalize_cursor` would pop the iteration frame.

Let me check `_finalize_cursor` — yes, for `top.repeat_step_id is not None and top.child_index >= len(top.plan.steps) and top.item_id is None`, it pops the frame and advances the host. Good — `step_skipped` would naturally exit the loop. No special handling needed for repeat.until.

For for_each, similarly — `item_skipped` is now in the same branch as `item_completed/item_attested`. It adds to `completed` and pops the item frame, then if all items done advances the host. But wait — `item_skipped` should not require *all* items skipped to advance; it skips one item, and the rest run normally. That's already the behavior — each item independently marks completed. Good.

Now for a group step skip — the spec says "cursor advances past entire subtree, no descendant `step_dispatched` events." With the new logic, if `step_skipped` is emitted on a group step path, the top-of-frame check `step = top.plan.steps[top.child_index]` finds the group step and advances child_index. Excellent.

But wait — `_auto_traverse_to_leaf` would normally have already auto-descended into the group via `nested_entered`. For a group skip to work without dispatch, the operator has to issue `skip` BEFORE `next`. The `peek_current_step` shouldn't auto-enter the group when it has `optional=True`. Actually re-reading — the skip command operates on what would be the next dispatch. We need to think about this.

For a `skip` to be operative on a group step, the cursor must point AT the group step, not have descended into it. The current peek logic auto-descends groups via `nested_entered`. But peek uses an in-memory append_fn that doesn't write to disk — so peek can probe what's next. Hmm.

The spec says: `cmd_skip` "asserts the step is the current cursor frontier (i.e. would be the next dispatch) — refuse to skip arbitrary future steps."

For a group step, the "frontier" is ambiguous — the gate would normally auto-enter and dispatch the first leaf. We need `cmd_skip` to operate on the un-traversed cursor — meaning: the top-of-frame's current step. If that step is `optional=True`, allow the skip regardless of whether it's a leaf or group. This is straightforward — we use `derive_cursor` directly (not peek) and look at `frames[-1].plan.steps[frames[-1].child_index]`.

Let me design `cmd_skip` to use `derive_cursor` (no auto-traverse) and look at the cursor's immediate next step.

For `astrid next --skip` though, the spec says "if the peeked step is optional=True, emit step_skipped". `peek_current_step` auto-descends through groups, so it lands on a leaf. So `next --skip` only skips leaves. For groups, the operator would use `astrid skip <group-path>` instead. This is consistent.

Now let me check: the path emitted for a `step_skipped` event on the leaf body inside an iteration. In the iteration frame, `path_prefix = parent_prefix` and the body step has same id as host. So the leaf path is `parent_prefix + (host.id,)` = same as host path. That's the path we'd emit. Good.

For for_each items, `path_prefix = parent_prefix` (same), body step id is host id, and `item_id` is set in the frame. So path tuple is same as host's. For `item_skipped`, we set `item_id` and use host path.

given\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--actor\",\n        default=None,\n        help=\"actor name (mutually exclusive with --agent)\",\n    )\n    try:\n        args = parser.parse_args(list(argv))\n    except SystemExit as exc:\n        return int(exc.code or 2)\n\n    if args.agent is not None and args.actor is not None:\n        _print_err(\"skip: --agent and --actor are mutually exclusive\")\n        return 1\n    if args.agent is not None:\n        actor_kind, actor_id = \"agent\", args.agent\n    elif args.actor is not None:\n        actor_kind, actor_id = \"actor\", args.actor\n    else:\n        actor_kind, actor_id = \"agent\", \"cli\"\n\n    try:\n        slug = validate_project_slug(args.project)\n    except Exception as exc:\n _read_tail_hash_safe(events_path)\n    try:\n        append_event_locked(\n            run_dir,\n            event,\n            expected_writer_epoch=expected_epoch,\n step (un-traversed — group steps with\n        # optional=True must be skippable as a unit, mirroring cmd_skip).\n        from astrid.core.task.gate import derive_cursor as _derive_cursor\n        cursor = _derive_cursor(plan, events)\n        if cursor.pinned_failure is not None or cursor.at_root_done:\n            _LOGGER.warning(\n                \"inbox: rejecting %s: run is exhausted, nothing to skip\",\n                entry.path.name,\n            )\n            _move_to(entry.path, rejected_dir)\n            return False\n        top = cursor.frames[-1]\n        if top.child_index >= len(top.plan.steps):\n            _LOGGER.warning(\n                \"inbox: rejecting %s: cursor frame exhausted\", entry.path.name\n            )\n            _move_to(entry.path, rejected_dir)\n            return False\n        cur_step = top.plan.steps[top.child_index]\n        cur_path = top.path_prefix + (cur_step.id,)\n        entry_path_tuple = _resolve_plan_step_path(entry)\n        if entry_path_tuple != cur_path:\n            _LOGGER.warning(\n                \"inbox: rejecting %s: plan_step_path %s does not match cursor frontier %s\",\n                entry.path.name,\n                \"/\".join(entry_path_tuple),\n                \"/\".join(cur_path),\n            )\n            _move_to(entry.path, rejected_dir)\n            return False\n        if entry.item_id is not None:\n            repeat = getattr(cur_step, \"repeat\", None)\n            from astrid.core.task.plan import RepeatForEach as _RepeatForEach\n            if not isinstance(repeat, _RepeatForEach):\n                _LOGGER.warning(\n                    \"inbox: rejecting %s: --item skip requires repeat.for_each\",\n                    entry.path.name,\n                )\n                _move_to(entry.path, rejected_dir)\n                return False\n            skip_event = make_item_skipped_event(\n                cur_path,\n                entry.item_id,\n                actor_kind=entry.submitted_by_kind or \"agent\",\n                actor_id=entry.submitted_by,\n                reason=f\"inbox skip by {entry.submitted_by}\",\n            )\n        else:\n            if not cur_step.optional:\n                _LOGGER.warning(\n                    \"inbox: rejecting %s: target step %s is not optional\",\n                    entry.path.name,\n                    \"/\".join(cur_path),\n                )\n                _move_to(entry.path, rejected_dir)\n                return False\n            skip_event = make_step_skipped_event(\n                \"/\".join(cur_path),\n                actor_kind=entry.submitted_by_kind or \"agent\",\n                actor_id=entry.submitted_by,\n                reason=f\"inbox skip by {entry.submitted_by}\",\n            )\n        append_event(events_path, skip_event)\n        _move_to(entry.path, consumed_dir)\n        return True\n\n    # approve / retry both require the cursor to be on a matching step."
}

The strict block already builds `current` (after applying mutations); we need to also walk skip events and verify the target was `optional=True`.

cannot validate mutations\")\n        else:\n            try:\n                from astrid.core.task.plan_verbs import (\n                    PLAN_MUTATED_KIND,\n                    _apply_diff as plan_apply_diff,\n                )\n                from astrid.core.task.validator import (\n                    MutationInvariantError,\n                    validate_mutation,\n                )\n\n                plan = load_plan(plan_path)\n                current = plan\n                mutation_events = [\n                    e for e in events if e.get(\"kind\") == PLAN_MUTATED_KIND\n                ]\n                for i, ev in enumerate(mutation_events):\n                    diff = ev.get(\"diff\")\n                    if not isinstance(diff, dict):\n                        strict_failures += 1\n                        print(\n                            f\"strict: mutation event {i + 1} missing diff field\"\n                        )\n                        continue\n                    try:\n                        proposed = plan_apply_diff(current, diff)\n                        # lease_epoch is not recoverable from event log;\n                        # pass epoch=0,0 as a best-effort audit check\n                        # (I6 is skipped in audit mode)\n                        validate_mutation(\n                            prior=current,\n                            proposed=proposed,\n                            lease_epoch_actual=0,\n                            lease_epoch_expected=0,\n                        )\n                        current = proposed\n                    except (MutationInvariantError, Exception) as exc:\n                        strict_failures += 1\n                        print(\n                            f\"strict: mutation event {i + 1} failed: {exc}\"\n                        )\n            except Exception as exc:\n                print(f\"strict: error loading plan: {exc}\", file=sys.stderr)",
  "new_string": "    # ── --strict: replay plan mutations through the validator ──────────\n    strict_failures = 0\n    if args.strict:\n        plan_path = proj_root / \"plan.json\"\n        if not plan_path.exists():\n            print(\"strict: plan.json not found; cannot validate mutations\")\n        else:\n            try:\n                from astrid.core.task.plan_verbs import (\n                    PLAN_MUTATED_KIND,\n                    _apply_diff as plan_apply_diff,\n                )\n                from astrid.core.task.validator import (\n                    MutationInvariantError,\n                    validate_mutation,\n                )\n\n                plan = load_plan(plan_path)\n                current = plan\n                mutation_events = [\n                    e for e in events if e.get(\"kind\") == PLAN_MUTATED_KIND\n                ]\n                for i, ev in enumerate(mutation_events):\n                    diff = ev.get(\"diff\")\n                    if not isinstance(diff, dict):\n                        strict_failures += 1\n                        print(\n                            f\"strict: mutation event {i + 1} missing diff field\"\n                        )\n                        continue\n                    try:\n                        proposed = plan_apply_diff(current, diff)\n                        # lease_epoch is not recoverable from event log;\n                        # pass epoch=0,0 as a best-effort audit check\n                        # (I6 is skipped in audit mode)\n                        validate_mutation(\n                            prior=current,\n                            proposed=proposed,\n                            lease_epoch_actual=0,\n                            lease_epoch_expected=0,\n                        )\n                        current = proposed\n                    except (MutationInvariantError, Exception) as exc:\n                        strict_failures += 1\n                        print(\n                            f\"strict: mutation event {i + 1} failed: {exc}\"\n                        )\n\n                # Sprint 5b: reject skip events whose target step is not\n                # optional=True in the effective (post-mutation) plan tree.\n                # This is defence-in-depth on already-written event logs.\n                from astrid.core.task.plan import (\n                    iter_steps_with_path as _iter_steps,\n                )\n                effective_index = {\n                    path: step for path, step in _iter_steps(current)\n                }\n                for i, ev in enumerate(events):\n                    if ev.get(\"kind\") not in {\"step_skipped\", \"item_skipped\"}:\n                        continue\n                    path_list = ev.get(\"plan_step_path\")\n                    if not isinstance(path_list, list) or not path_list:\n                        strict_failures += 1\n                        print(\n                            f\"strict: skip event {i + 1} missing plan_step_path\"\n                        )\n                        continue\n                    target_path = tuple(str(p) for p in path_list)\n                    target = effective_index.get(target_path)\n                    if target is None:\n                        strict_failures += 1\n                        print(\n                            f\"strict: skip event {i + 1} references unknown \"\n                            f\"step path {'/'.join(target_path)!r}\"\n                        )\n                        continue\n                    # item_skipped is allowed even if the host is not optional\n                    # (per-item skip is independent of host optionality).\n                    if ev.get(\"kind\") == \"item_skipped\":\n                        continue\n                    if not getattr(target, \"optional\", False):\n                        strict_failures += 1\n                        print(\n                            f\"strict: skip event {i + 1} targets non-optional \"\n                            f\"step {'/'.join(target_path)!r}\"\n                        )\n            except Exception as exc:\n                print(f\"strict: error loading plan: {exc}\", file=sys.stderr)"
}

1	"""Shared helpers for Phase 5 lifecycle tests (T12-T20).
2	
3	Not a test module (doesn't match test_*.py glob). Provides:
4	- ``make_pack``: write a tiny DSL pack module under packs/<pack>/<name>.py
5	- ``setup_packs_and_compile``: build packs root + compile a qualified id to JSON
6	- ``setup_run``: build packs + projects roots, compile, and call cmd_start
7	"""
8	
9	from __future__ import annotations
10	
11	import io
12	import os
13	from contextlib import redirect_stdout
14	from pathlib import Path
15	
16	from astrid.core.task.lifecycle import cmd_start
17	from astrid.orchestrate.compile import compile_to_path
18	
19	
20	def migrate_v1_plan(plan: dict) -> dict:
21	    """Rewrite a Sprint 2 (v1) plan dict in place to the Sprint 3 (v2) collapsed schema.
22	
23	    Reuses the production migration logic so legacy test fixtures keep working
24	    without per-test schema rewrites. Idempotent: v2 plans pass through unchanged.
25	    """
26	    import importlib.util
27	    import sys as _sys
28	
29	    if plan.get("version") == 2:
30	        return plan
31	    _spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(
32	        "_astrid_sprint3_migrate_plans",
33	        Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
34	        / "scripts" / "migrations" / "sprint-3" / "migrate_plans.py",
35	    )
36	    _mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(_spec)
37	    _sys.modules[_spec.name] = _mod
38	    _spec.loader.exec_module(_mod)
39	    new_steps = [_mod._migrate_step(s) if isinstance(s, dict) else s for s in plan.get("steps", [])]
40	    return {"plan_id": plan.get("plan_id", "unknown"), "version": 2, "steps": new_steps}
41	
42	
43	def make_pack(packs_root: Path, pack: str, module_name: str, body: str) -> Path:
44	    pack_dir = packs_root / pack
45	    pack_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
46	    module_path = pack_dir / f"{module_name}.py"
47	    module_path.write_text(body, encoding="utf-8")
48	    return module_path
49	
50	
51	def setup_packs_and_compile(
52	    tmp_path: Path,
53	    pack: str,
54	    module_name: str,
55	    body: str,
56	    qualified_id: str,
57	) -> tuple[Path, Path]:
58	    """Returns (packs_root, projects_root). Compiles the qualified id."""
59	    packs = tmp_path / "packs"
60	    projects = tmp_path / "projects"
61	    packs.mkdir()
62	    projects.mkdir()
63	    make_pack(packs, pack, module_name, body)
64	    compile_to_path(qualified_id, packs_root=packs)
65	    return packs, projects
66	
67	
68	def setup_run(
69	    tmp_path: Path,
70	    pack: str,
71	    module_name: str,
72	    body: str,
73	    qualified_id: str,
74	    *,
75	    run_id: str,
76	    project: str = "p",
77	    start_actor: str = "bob",
78	) -> tuple[Path, Path]:
79	    """Compile + cmd_start. Returns (packs_root, projects_root). Sets
80	    ARTAGENTS_ACTOR=start_actor before cmd_start so run_started.actor==start_actor.
81	    Caller is responsible for adjusting ARTAGENTS_ACTOR before subsequent ack calls.
82	    """
83	    packs, projects = setup_packs_and_compile(tmp_path, pack, module_name, body, qualified_id)
84	    os.environ["ARTAGENTS_ACTOR"] = start_actor
85	    with redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
86	        rc = cmd_start(
87	            [qualified_id, "--project", project, "--name", run_id],
88	            packs_root=packs,
89	            projects_root=projects,
90	        )
91	    if rc != 0:
92	        raise RuntimeError(f"setup_run: cmd_start rc={rc}")
93	    return packs, projects
94
```

> AGENT

The cmd_next --skip is looping past s1, hitting s2 (mandatory), and erroring. The spec is actually ambiguous here. Re-reading:

> If the peeked step is optional=True, emit step_skipped instead of printing the dispatch, then loop back and re-peek.
> If optional=False, exit non-zero with a clear error.

The intent is: skip optional, then print dispatch for the *next* step. Let me re-read my implementation. The loop continues while optional — when it stops (reached non-optional), it should fall through to print the dispatch for that step, not error. Let me fix the logic:

> TOOL

  "old_string": "    # --skip: emit step_skipped events for optional leaves until either the\n    # next leaf is non-optional or the cursor exhausts. We loop because\n    # multiple optional steps could be queued back-to-back.\n    if args.skip:\n        while not (peek.exhausted or peek.step is None):\n            if not peek.step.optional:\n                _print_err(\n                    f\"next --skip: cursor step {STEP_PATH_SEP.join(peek.path_tuple)!r} \"\n                    f\"is not optional; remove --skip to dispatch it\"\n                )\n                return 1\n            from astrid.core.task.events import make_step_skipped_event\n            skip_event = make_step_skipped_event(\n                STEP_PATH_SEP.join(peek.path_tuple),\n                actor_kind=\"agent\",\n                actor_id=\"cli\",\n                reason=args.reason,\n            )\n            append_event(events_path, skip_event)\n            print(f\"skipped {STEP_PATH_SEP.join(peek.path_tuple)}\")\n            events = read_events(events_path)\n            peek = peek_current_step(\n                plan, events, slug, project_root=proj_root, run_id=run_id\n            )\n        if peek.exhausted or peek.step is None:\n            if _run_is_complete(plan, events):\n                append_event(events_path, make_run_completed_event(run_id))\n            return 0\n        # Fall through into normal print of the now-non-optional step.",
  "new_string": "    # --skip: emit step_skipped events for optional leaves until either the\n    # next leaf is non-optional or the cursor exhausts. The very first\n    # peek MUST be optional — refusing to start otherwise — but subsequent\n    # iterations naturally stop at the first non-optional leaf and fall\n    # through to print its dispatch.\n    if args.skip:\n        if peek.exhausted or peek.step is None:\n            _print_err(\n                f\"next --skip: run is exhausted; recovery: astrid abort --project {slug}\"\n            )\n            return 1\n        if not peek.step.optional:\n            _print_err(\n                f\"next --skip: cursor step {STEP_PATH_SEP.join(peek.path_tuple)!r} \"\n                f\"is not optional; remove --skip to dispatch it\"\n            )\n            return 1\n        from astrid.core.task.events import make_step_skipped_event\n        while (\n            not (peek.exhausted or peek.step is None)\n            and peek.step.optional\n        ):\n            skip_event = make_step_skipped_event(\n                STEP_PATH_SEP.join(peek.path_tuple),\n                actor_kind=\"agent\",\n                actor_id=\"cli\",\n                reason=args.reason,\n            )\n            append_event(events_path, skip_event)\n            print(f\"skipped {STEP_PATH_SEP.join(peek.path_tuple)}\")\n            events = read_events(events_path)\n            peek = peek_current_step(\n                plan, events, slug, project_root=proj_root, run_id=run_id\n            )\n        if peek.exhausted or peek.step is None:\n            if _run_is_complete(plan, events):\n                append_event(events_path, make_run_completed_event(run_id))\n            return 0\n        # Fall through into normal print of the now-non-optional step."

All 14 new tests pass. Let me also confirm there's no issue with the `peek_current_step` auto-traversal — since the new logic in cmd_next --skip uses peek which auto-descends groups. The issue: if a group step is optional, peek would auto-enter it (via the test-only events_view) and land on a leaf inside. The skip would then target the leaf. But the group itself is the optional thing.

Actually re-reading my implementation — peek auto-traverses groups, so `cmd_next --skip` operates on leaves only. The spec mentions cmd_skip for groups. That's consistent. Good.

> AGENT

All audit tests pass. Let me check if there's anything else to verify with the gate's auto-traversal handling skips for groups. Looking at my edit — for a group step skip, the cursor advances past the group at the root frame. The `step_skipped` event handler in derive_cursor advances child_index for the *top* frame's current step. For a top-level group step, that's correct. For a group step nested inside another, `step_skipped` would emit with the full path tuple. The handler matches by top.plan.steps[top.child_index]. If the cursor was previously at a different frame (e.g., descended), the handler would advance whatever's at the cursor — that's the right behavior because cmd_skip refuses to skip non-frontier steps.

# Report

**Feature**: optional steps + `step_skipped` / `item_skipped` events for the Astrid task kernel (Sprint 5b).

## Files modified
- `astrid/core/task/plan.py`: added `Step.optional: bool = False`; `__post_init__` and `_validate_step` reject `optional=True` with `requires_ack=True`; serializer omits when False.
- `astrid/core/task/events.py`: new `make_step_skipped_event` and `make_item_skipped_event` factories; `__all__` updated; `_run_is_complete` accepts `step_skipped` as a terminal state.
- `astrid/core/task/gate.py:254–298`: `derive_cursor` advances on `step_skipped` / `item_skipped`. Skipped steps bypass produces-check gating entirely (always advance regardless of declared produces). For_each items merge into the `completed` set like `item_completed`. Repeat-until bodies exit cleanly via the existing finalize logic — no `iteration_exhausted`.
- `astrid/core/task/lifecycle.py`: `cmd_next` accepts `--skip` / `--reason`; loops emitting `step_skipped` for optional leaves until the cursor reaches a non-optional step or exhausts (then falls through to normal dispatch print). Re-exports `cmd_skip`.
- `astrid/core/task/inbox.py`: `_VALID_DECISIONS` adds `"skip"`; `consume_inbox_entry` routes skip entries — verifies cursor frontier, requires `optional=True` for whole-step skips, allows per-item skip independent of host optionality; rejects to `.rejected/` otherwise.
- `astrid/core/task/run_audit.py`: adds `skipped_steps` count to run summary (pretty + JSON); `cmd_events_verify --strict` rejects `step_skipped` events targeting non-optional steps (defence-in-depth on already-written logs); `item_skipped` is always allowed.
- `astrid/pipeline.py`: `skip` added to `LIFECYCLE_VERBS`; dispatcher routes `astrid skip ...` to `cmd_skip`.

## Files added
- `astrid/core/task/lifecycle_skip.py`: `cmd_skip` verb — resolves cursor frontier via `derive_cursor` (not `peek_current_step`) so group steps stay un-traversed and skippable as a unit; refuses non-frontier targets; emits `step_skipped` / `item_skipped` via `append_event_locked` under writer-epoch + tail-hash CAS, mirroring `cmd_ack`.
- `tests/task/test_optional_steps.py`: 14 tests covering schema rejection, serialization, leaf skip, group skip (no descendant `step_dispatched`), `cmd_next --skip` on optional and mandatory, for_each item skip, repeat.until body skip (no `iteration_exhausted`), and `events verify --strict` accept/reject paths.

## Test command + summary
- `python -m pytest tests/task/test_optional_steps.py` → 14 passed.
- `python -m pytest tests/task/ tests/test_task_kernel_e2e.py tests/test_task_kernel_events.py -q` → 164 passed, 2 failed. **Both failures are pre-existing user WIP**, verified by `git stash; pytest; git stash pop`:
  - `tests/task/test_plan_schema_collapsed.py::test_load_plan_rejects_non_v2` (test-only auto-migration shim in `tests/conftest.py:30` silently upgrades v1→v2 inside `_validate_plan`).
  - `tests/test_task_kernel_e2e.py::test_two_step_plan_drives_kernel_end_to_end` (asserts `event["plan_step_id"]` but `make_step_completed_event` only emits `plan_step_path`).
- Inbox and lifecycle_ack failures observed in the wider suite are also pre-existing on baseline (paths in error output reference an older sibling repo `ArtAgents`).

## Deviations from spec
- Spec said to add skip rejection to `astrid/core/task/validator.py`. The validator is plan-mutation-only (six invariants); event-stream replay validation lives entirely in `run_audit.py::cmd_events_verify --strict`. I put the skip-legality check there alongside the existing mutation-replay loop — this is the path the spec said "events verify --strict" exercises, so the same code-path now also rejects illegal skips. No changes to `validator.py`.
- `cmd_next --skip` falls through to print the dispatch for the first non-optional step after consuming optional ones, rather than exiting after the skips. The spec's wording ("loop back and re-peek") implied this but wasn't explicit; chose the more useful behaviour so an operator sees what to run next in one call.
- Per-item skip via inbox (`--item` with `decision: "skip"`) does NOT require the host step to be `optional=True` — matches the spec's e2e test case ("for_each parent with optional=False, item-level skip via --item: that item skipped, others run"). The same relaxation applies to `cmd_skip --item`.

## Follow-ups worth a human eye
- The `consume_inbox_entry` skip path uses `entry.submitted_by_kind or "agent"` as `actor_kind`. v2-schema entries are required to carry `submitted_by_kind`; the `or` fallback is dead for v2 but quiet for legacy entries — if you want strict-only behaviour, drop the fallback and reject legacy skip entries.
- The audit JSON now includes a new `skipped_steps` top-level field. Any downstream consumers of `astrid run show --json` should be told.
- Pre-existing failures in `test_plan_schema_collapsed.py` and `test_task_kernel_e2e.py` are visible WIP and worth fixing on this branch independently of this feature.
